### [1] [FR3 for 6G Networks: A Comparative Study against FR1 and FR2 Across Diverse Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07482)
*Fahimeh Aghaei,Mehdi Monemi,Mehdi Rasti,Murat Uysal*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: FR3（7.125-24.25 GHz）作为6G和C-V2B通信的潜力频段，在覆盖与容量间提供平衡。通过射线追踪建模显示，在相同孔径下，FR3在小区边缘UE的数据率优于FR2毫米波，且从车载到行人UE的覆盖概率差异极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无线容量需求增长和6G发展，新定义的FR3频段（上中频带）因其在毫米波大带宽与sub-6 GHz良好传播特性间的平衡优势，成为增强6G和C-V2B通信覆盖与容量的潜力频谱。但需通过准确信道建模解决关键技术挑战。

Method: 采用射线追踪工具表征下行传播，为可靠C-V2B链路实现详细信道建模。评估FR1（sub-6 GHz）、FR3和FR2（毫米波）频段在不同城市环境（郊区、城市、高层城市）下的数据率性能，使用针对不同环境设计的阵列配置。将单手握持行人UE模型集成到射线追踪器中分析覆盖概率差异。

Result: 在相同孔径尺寸下，FR3在干扰和无干扰场景下对小区边缘UE的数据率均高于FR2毫米波，表明毫米波的额外阵列增益不足以完全补偿严重的路径损耗。从车载到行人UE的过渡在所有频段仅产生约1%-3%的覆盖概率差异，FR3（特别是8.2 GHz）差异最小。

Conclusion: FR3频段在6G和C-V2B通信中展现出显著优势，在覆盖与容量间提供良好平衡。其性能优于毫米波频段，特别是在小区边缘场景，且对UE类型变化具有鲁棒性，是未来无线系统的有前景的频谱选择。

Abstract: Motivated by increasing wireless capacity demands and 6G advancements, the newly defined Frequency Range 3 (FR3, 7.125-24.25 GHz), also known as the upper mid-band, has emerged as a promising spectrum candidate. It offers a balance between the large bandwidth potential of millimeter-wave bands and the favorable propagation characteristics of sub-6 GHz bands. As a result, the upper mid-band presents a strong opportunity to enhance both coverage and capacity, particularly for 6G systems and Cellular Vehicle-to-Base Station (C-V2B) communications. Harnessing this potential, however, requires addressing key technical challenges through accurate and realistic channel modeling across diverse urban environments, including Suburban, Urban, and HighRise Urban scenarios. To this end, we employ a ray-tracing tool to characterize downlink propagation and enable detailed channel modeling for reliable C-V2B links. We evaluate data rate performance across FR1 (sub-6 GHz), FR3, and FR2 (mmWave) bands using antenna array configurations designed for different urban environments. The results show that, under equal aperture sizes, FR3 achieves higher data rates than FR2 for cell-edge User Equipment (UEs) in both interference-free and full-interference scenarios, indicating that the additional array gain at mmWave is insufficient to fully compensate for the severe experienced path loss. Integrating one-hand-grip pedestrian UEs model into ray tracer shows that transitioning from vehicular to pedestrian UEs results in negligible differences in coverage probability (about 1\%--3\%) across all frequencies, with the minimum differences observed in FR3, particularly at 8.2 GHz.

</details>


### [2] [Energy-Efficient Drone Logistics for Last-Mile Delivery: Implications of Payload-Dependent Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07514)
*Ziyue Li,Qianwen,Guo,Paul Schonfeld*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文研究绿色无人机路径规划问题，发现基于能耗优化的路径策略与传统距离最小化策略存在显著差异，在67%的实例中实现平均2.17%的能耗节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机配送作为最后一公里配送的经济高效替代方案，其能耗不仅取决于飞行距离，还受有效载荷影响。随着配送过程中重量动态变化，传统基于距离最小化的路径规划无法实现能耗最优，需要研究能源效率最大化的绿色路径规划方法。

Method: 通过一系列动机示例和数值实验，研究能源感知的无人机路径规划问题。分析对比了传统距离最小化策略与能源优化策略，考虑了多站点配送、单客户独立配送以及异构无人机车队匹配等场景。

Result: 研究发现能源感知路径规划产生与传统策略相悖的三种反直觉策略：1) 较长路径可能比短路径能耗更低；2) 独立单客户配送可能优于多站点配送；3) 异构无人机车队通过容量与需求匹配实现更高效率。在67%的实例中实现能耗节约，平均节约2.17%，最大节约5.97%。

Conclusion: 绿色无人机路径规划策略能够显著提高最后一公里配送的可持续性，能源感知的路径优化策略相比传统距离最小化策略具有实际应用价值，为无人机配送的能源效率优化提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Drone delivery is rapidly emerging as a cost-effective and energy efficient alternative for last-mile delivery. Unlike ground vehicles, a drone's energy consumption depends on its payload in addition to travel distance. This creates a unique environmental challenge for multi-stop delivery tours, as the drone's total weight, and therefore its energy consumption rate, dynamically changes after each delivery. This paper investigates a novel green drone routing problem focused on maximizing energy efficiency. Through a series of motivating examples and numerical experiments, we demonstrate that energy-aware routing leads to several counter-intuitive routing strategies that contradict traditional distance-minimization delivery: a longer route may actually consume less energy than a shorter one; separate single-customer tours can be superior to a multi-stop tour; and a heterogeneous fleet, with drones of varying sizes, can achieve greater efficiency by matching drone capacity to specific delivery demands. In the numerical study, the green routing strategy shows energy savings in 67% of the instances. For these cases, the average energy saving is 2.17%, with a maximum saving of 5.97%, compared to minimum distance routing. These findings highlight the potential for green drone routing strategies to improve the sustainability of last-mile delivery.

</details>


### [3] [Analytical Modeling of Dispersive Closed-loop MC Channels with Pulsatile Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08307)
*Theofilos Symeonidis,Fardad Vakilipoor,Robert Schober,Nunzio Tuccitto,Maximilian Schäfer*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个考虑脉动流的时变一维信道模型，用于分析人体心血管系统中的分子通信，推导了遵循包裹正态分布的脉冲响应解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子通信模型大多假设恒定流速，但人体生理流动本质上是脉动的（受心脏周期影响）。需要更准确的时变模型来描述心血管系统中的分子通信。

Method: 提出时变一维信道模型，考虑脉动流对分子传输的影响。推导出信道脉冲响应的解析表达式，该表达式遵循具有时变均值和方差的包裹正态分布。通过三维粒子模拟验证模型准确性。

Result: 模型成功揭示了信道的循环平稳特性，量化了脉动对时间浓度分布的影响。三维粒子模拟验证显示模型与仿真结果高度一致，能够高效分析信道特性。

Conclusion: 该时变模型比传统恒定流速模型更准确地描述了人体心血管系统中的分子通信，为医疗应用提供了更真实的信道表征工具。

Abstract: Molecular communication (MC) is a communication paradigm in which information is conveyed through the controlled release, propagation, and reception of molecules. Many envisioned healthcare applications of MC are expected to operate inside the human body. In this environment, the cardiovascular system ( CVS) acts as the physical channel, which forms a closed-loop network where particle transport is mainly governed by the combined effects of diffusion and flow. Despite the fact that physiological flows in many parts of the human body are inherently pulsatile due to the cardiac cycle, most existing models for dispersive closed-loop MC channels assume a constant flow velocity. In this paper, we present a time-variant one-dimensional (1D ) channel model for dispersive closed-loop MC systems with pulsatile flow. We derive an analytical expression for the channel impulse response (CIR ), which follows a wrapped Normal distribution with time-variant mean and variance. The obtained model reveals the cyclostationary nature of the channel and quantifies the influence of pulsation on the temporal concentration profile compared to steady-flow systems. Finally, the model is validated by three-dimensional ( 3D ) particle-based simulations (PBS s), showing excellent agreement and enabling an efficient analytical characterization of the channel.

</details>

### [4] [Optimization of 32-bit Unsigned Division by Constants on 64-bit Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07902)
*Shigeo Mitsunari,Takashi Hoshino*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对64位CPU的32位无符号整数除以常数的优化方法，相比现有的32位CPU优化方法，在64位架构上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Granlund-Montgomery方法（GM方法）及其改进版本主要针对32位CPU设计，在64位CPU上未能充分利用硬件能力。例如，对于x/7这样的运算，现有编译器生成的代码没有充分利用64位处理器的优势。

Method: 提出了一种针对64位CPU的32位无符号整数除以常数的优化方法。作者为LLVM和GCC编译器实现了补丁，利用64位架构的特性来优化32位无符号整数除法运算。

Result: 在Intel Xeon w9-3495X（Sapphire Rapids）上实现了1.67倍加速，在Apple M4（Apple M-series SoC）上实现了1.98倍加速。LLVM补丁已合并到llvm:main分支，证明了该方法的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 提出的64位CPU优化方法显著提升了32位无符号整数除以常数的性能，已被主流编译器采纳，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Granlund and Montgomery proposed an optimization method for unsigned integer division by constants [3]. Their method (called the GM method in this paper) was further improved in part by works such as [1] and [7], and is now adopted by major compilers including GCC, Clang, Microsoft Compiler, and Apple Clang. However, for example, for x/7, the generated code is designed for 32-bit CPUs and therefore does not fully exploit 64-bit capabilities. This paper proposes an optimization method for 32-bit unsigned division by constants targeting 64-bit CPUs. We implemented patches for LLVM/GCC and achieved speedups of 1.67x on Intel Xeon w9-3495X (Sapphire Rapids) and 1.98x on Apple M4 (Apple M-series SoC) in the microbenchmark described later. The LLVM patch has already been merged into llvm:main [6], demonstrating the practical applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [5] [PG-MDP: Profile-Guided Memory Dependence Prediction for Area-Constrained Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08445)
*Luke Panayi,Johan Jino,Sebastian S. Kim,Alberto Ros,Alexandra Jimborean,Jim Whittaker,Martin Berger,Paul Kelly*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: PG-MDP通过软件协同设计标记持续内存独立的load指令，使其绕过MDP查询，减少小面积核心中MDP表的假依赖问题，性能接近大预测器但无面积成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面积受限的核心（如能效或边缘系统）中MDP表空间有限，导致内存独立load与无关预测器条目发生别名冲突，产生高假依赖率，引起处理器流水线不必要停顿。传统增大预测器尺寸或复杂度的方法不适用于面积受限核心。

Method: 提出基于配置文件的存储器依赖预测（PG-MDP），一种软件协同设计方法：通过配置文件分析标记持续内存独立的load指令（基于操作码），将其从MDP工作集中移除。这些load在调度时绕过MDP查询，始终尽快发射。

Result: 在SPEC2017 CPU intspeed基准测试中，PG-MDP将MDP查询率降低79%，假依赖减少77%，小模拟核心的几何平均IPC提升1.47%（接近使用16倍预测器条目的性能，差距仅0.5%），无面积成本和额外指令带宽开销。

Conclusion: 针对预测器工作集进行优化与增大预测器尺寸同样有效。PG-MDP能在使用极小预测器的同时实现与大预测器竞争的性能，为面积受限核心提供无成本的性能提升方案。

Abstract: Memory Dependence Prediction (MDP) is a speculative technique to determine which stores, if any, a given load will depend on. Area-constrained cores are increasingly relevant in various applications such as energy-efficient or edge systems, and often have limited space for MDP tables. This leads to a high rate of false dependencies as memory independent loads alias with unrelated predictor entries, causing unnecessary stalls in the processor pipeline.
  The conventional way to address this problem is with greater predictor size or complexity, but this is unattractive on area-constrained cores. This paper proposes that targeting the predictor working set is as effective as growing the predictor, and can deliver performance competitive with large predictors while still using very small predictors. This paper introduces profile-guided memory dependence prediction (PG-MDP), a software co-design to label consistently memory independent loads via their opcode and remove them from the MDP working set. These loads bypass querying the MDP when dispatched and always issue as soon as possible. Across SPEC2017 CPU intspeed, PG-MDP reduces the rate of MDP queries by 79%, false dependencies by 77%, and improves geomean IPC for a small simulated core by 1.47% (to within 0.5% of using 16x the predictor entries), with no area cost and no additional instruction bandwidth.

</details>

### [6] [IPEK: Intelligent Priority-Aware Event-Based Trust with Asymmetric Knowledge for Resilient Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07532)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出IPKE系统，通过事件感知和位置感知的不对称信任机制，结合Dempster-Shafer理论，有效检测VANET中智能攻击者，在15-35%攻击密度下保持0%误报率和75%以上召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VANET信任模型对交通事件进行同质化处理，无法检测智能攻击者策略：攻击者通过正确报告低优先级事件积累信誉，然后在安全关键情况下注入虚假数据。现有方法忽略事件严重性和位置关键性，导致无法识别此类攻击。

Method: 1) 定义事件感知和位置感知的智能攻击模型；2) 提出不对称局部信任机制：惩罚随事件和位置严重性缩放，奖励采用渐近模型，使信任难以恢复；3) 采用Dempster-Shafer理论进行全局信任融合，使用Yager组合规则，结合顺序源可靠性排序和不对称风险强调机制。

Result: 使用OMNeT++、Veins和SUMO仿真，在15-35%攻击密度下与MDT和TCEMD对比。IPKE在所有场景下保持0%误报率（无诚实车辆被错误撤销），召回率超过75%，F1分数超过0.86。

Conclusion: 将上下文感知集成到攻击建模和信任评估中，显著优于对称方法对抗战略对手。不对称信任机制和Dempster-Shafer融合能有效检测智能攻击，同时避免误报诚实车辆。

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) are vulnerable to intelligent attackers who exploit the homogeneous treatment of traffic events in existing trust models. These attackers accumulate reputation by reporting correctly on low-priority events and then inject false data during safety-critical situations - a strategy that current approaches cannot detect because they ignore event severity and location criticality in trust calculations. This paper addresses this gap through three contributions. First, it introduces event-aware and location-aware intelligent attack models, which have not been formally defined or simulated in prior work. Second, it proposes an asymmetric local trust mechanism where penalties scale with event and location severity while rewards follow an asymptotic model, making trust difficult to regain after misuse. Third, it adapts Dempster-Shafer Theory for global trust fusion using Yager's combination rule - assigning conflicting evidence to uncertainty rather than forcing premature decisions - combined with sequential source-reliability ordering and an asymmetric risk accentuation mechanism. Simulations using OMNeT++, Veins, and SUMO compare the proposed system (IPEK) against MDT and TCEMD under attacker densities of 15-35 percent. IPEK maintained 0 percent False Positive Rate across all scenarios, meaning no honest vehicle was wrongly revoked, while sustaining Recall above 75 percent and F1-scores exceeding 0.86. These results demonstrate that integrating context-awareness into both attack modeling and trust evaluation significantly outperforms symmetric approaches against strategic adversaries.

</details>


### [7] [LCMP: Distributed Long-Haul Cost-Aware Multi-Path Routing for Inter-Datacenter RDMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07836)
*Dong-Yang Yu,Yuchao Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Jun Wang,Wenfei Wu,Haipeng Yao,Wendong Wang,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LCMP是一个分布式长距离成本感知多路径路由框架，针对RDMA云服务在多路径数据中心间传输中的路径不对称、拥塞信号延迟和流路由碰撞问题，通过路径质量评分和交换机拥塞信号实现低成本、低延迟、拥塞敏感的传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMA云服务在多路径数据中心部署中面临路径不对称、拥塞信号延迟和同时流路由碰撞等新特性，导致现有路由方法失效，需要新的解决方案来优化传输性能。

Method: LCMP结合控制平面路径质量评分和紧凑的交换机拥塞信号：路径质量评分统一评估不对称路径质量；交换机拥塞信号实现对路径拥塞的快速响应。通过过滤高成本候选路径并在缩减集合内执行保持多样性的哈希，解决同时流决策碰撞问题。

Result: 在8个数据中心的测试平台上，LCMP相比最先进的DCN路由策略，将中位数和尾部FCT（流完成时间）减速分别降低了76%和64%。在2000公里数据中心间场景的大规模NS-3仿真中也确认了类似的改进。

Conclusion: LCMP通过创新的分布式多路径路由框架，有效解决了RDMA云服务在多路径数据中心间传输中的关键挑战，显著提升了传输性能，为长距离RDMA通信提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: RDMA-empowered cloud services are gradually deployed across datacenters (DCs) with multiple paths, which exhibit new properties of path asymmetry, delayed congestion signals, and simultaneous flow routing collisions, and further fail existing routing methods.
  We present LCMP, a distributed long-haul cost-aware multi-path routing framework that aims to place RDMA flows on multiple inter-DC paths, achieving low-cost, low-latency, and congestion-responsive transmission. LCMP combines a control-plane path-quality score with compact on-switch congestion signals, where the former unifies quality assessment for asymmetric paths and the latter enables responsive reaction to path congestion. LCMP further resolves the simultaneous flow decision collision problem by filtering high-cost candidates, and performing a diversity-preserving hash inside the reduced set. On an 8-DC testbed, LCMP reduces median and tail FCT slowdown by up to 76% and 64%, respectively compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) DCN routing strategies. And large-scale NS-3 simulations under the 2000 km inter-DC scenario confirm similar improvements.

</details>


### [8] [LITE: Lightweight Channel Gain Estimation with Reduced X-Haul CSI Signaling in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08458)
*David Goez,Marco Piazzola,Giulia Costa,Achiel Colpaert,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Esra Aycan Beyazit,Nina Slamnik-Krijestorac,Johann M. Marquez-Barja,Miguel Camelo Botero*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LITE是一个轻量级CSI压缩与预测框架，结合1-D卷积自编码器和SE增强BiLSTM，在O-RAN架构中实现高效信道状态信息处理，减少83.39%模型复杂度同时提升5%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CF-MaMIMO在O-RAN中面临频繁CSI交换导致的X-haul带宽压力问题，现有基于未压缩CSI或重型预测器的方法无法满足传输和处理预算限制。

Method: 提出LITE框架：在O-DU部署1-D卷积自编码器进行50% CSI压缩，在Near-RT-RIC部署SE增强BiLSTM预测器进行短时程轨迹无关预测，采用压缩感知训练和TensorRT优化。

Result: 实现83.39%模型复杂度降低和5%准确率提升；压缩感知训练仅导致6%准确率损失；TensorRT优化实现147k QPS和4.6倍吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: LITE提供了X-haul高效、低延迟、可部署的信道增益预测方案，兼容O-RAN分割架构，在严格传输和处理预算下优于独立和端到端策略。

Abstract: Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (CF-MaMIMO) in Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) promises high spectral efficiency but is limited by frequent Channel State Information (CSI) exchanges, which strain fronthaul/midhaul/backhaul (X-haul) bandwidth and exceed the capabilities of existing approaches relying on uncompressed CSI or heavy predictors. To overcome these constraints, we propose LITE, a lightweight pipeline combining a 1-D convolutional Autoencoder (AE) at the O-RAN Distributed Unit (O-DU) with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)-enhanced Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) predictor at the Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT-RIC), enabling short-horizon trajectory-unaware forecasting under strict transport and processing budgets. LITE applies 50% CSI compression and an asymmetric SE-BiLSTM, reducing model complexity by 83.39% while improving accuracy by 5% relative to a baseline BiLSTM. With compression-aware training, the Lightweight Intelligent Trajectory Estimator (LITE) incurs only 6% accuracy loss versus the BiLSTM baseline, outperforming independent and end-to-end strategies. A TensorRT-optimized implementation achieves 147k Queries per Second (QPS), a 4.6x throughput gain. These results demonstrate that LITE delivers X-haul-efficient, low-latency, and deployment-ready channel-gain prediction compatible with O-RAN splits.

</details>

### [9] [Open-Ended Video Game Glitch Detection with Agentic Reasoning and Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07818)
*Muyang Zheng,Tong Zhou,Geyang Wu,Zihao Lin,Haibo Wang,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了开放世界视频游戏故障检测任务，包含VideoGlitchBench基准和GliDe框架，用于从游戏视频中检测、描述并定位故障时间区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏故障理解任务主要局限于图像级识别或封闭式问答，无法处理连续游戏视频中需要理解游戏特定动态（如机制、物理、渲染、动画、状态转换）的开放世界故障检测问题，且难以区分真实故障与游戏中异常但有效的事件。

Method: 提出VideoGlitchBench基准（包含5,238个来自120款游戏的视频，带有详细故障描述和精确时间标注）和GliDe框架（包含三个关键组件：游戏感知上下文记忆用于知情推理、基于辩论的反射器用于多视角故障检测和验证、事件级定位模块从碎片化时间证据中恢复完整故障区间）。

Result: 实验表明该任务对当前多模态模型极具挑战性，而GliDe相比相应基线模型实现了显著更强的性能，通过特定任务评估协议（联合衡量语义保真度和时间准确性）验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 开放世界视频游戏故障检测是一个需要复杂推理的新任务，VideoGlitchBench为统一评估提供了首个基准，GliDe框架通过游戏感知记忆、辩论式验证和事件级定位等创新组件有效提升了故障检测和定位能力。

Abstract: Open-ended video game glitch detection aims to identify glitches in gameplay videos, describe them in natural language, and localize when they occur. Unlike conventional game glitch understanding tasks which have largely been framed as image-level recognition or closed-form question answering, this task requires reasoning about game-specific dynamics such as mechanics, physics, rendering, animation, and expected state transitions directly over continuous gameplay videos and distinguishing true glitches from unusual but valid in-game events. To support this task, we introduce VideoGlitchBench, the first benchmark for open-ended video game glitch detection with temporal localization. VideoGlitchBench contains 5,238 gameplay videos from 120 games, each annotated with detailed glitch descriptions and precise temporal spans, enabling unified evaluation of semantic understanding and temporal grounding. We further propose GliDe, an agentic framework with three key components: a game-aware contextual memory for informed reasoning, a debate-based reflector for multi-perspective glitch detection and verification, and an event-level grounding module that recovers complete glitch intervals from fragmented temporal evidence. We also design a task-specific evaluation protocol that jointly measures semantic fidelity and temporal accuracy. Experiments show that this task remains highly challenging for current multimodal models, while GliDe achieves substantially stronger performance than corresponding vanilla model baselines.

</details>


### [10] [Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping: Agent-Triggered Focus Sessions for Isolated Per-Agent Steering in Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07911)
*Nickson Patel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DACS（动态注意力上下文范围）是一种解决多智能体LLM编排系统中上下文污染问题的机制，通过两种不对称模式（注册模式和聚焦模式）实现智能体间上下文隔离，显著提升决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM编排系统存在上下文污染问题：当N个并发智能体竞争编排器的上下文窗口时，每个智能体的任务状态、部分输出和待处理问题会污染其他所有智能体的引导交互，导致决策质量下降。

Method: 提出动态注意力上下文范围（DACS）机制，编排器在两种不对称模式下运行：注册模式（仅保存轻量级智能体状态摘要，≤200词元）和聚焦模式（当智能体发出SteeringRequest时，注入该智能体的完整上下文，同时压缩其他智能体为注册条目）。上下文隔离是智能体触发的、不对称且确定性的。

Result: 在四个实验阶段共200次试验中，DACS在8个合成场景中达到90.0-98.4%的引导准确率，而扁平上下文基线仅为21.0-60.0%（p<0.0001）。错误智能体污染从28-57%降至0-14%，上下文效率比最高达3.53倍。准确率优势随N和D增长而增加，LLM-as-judge验证关键词匹配（平均kappa=0.909）。

Conclusion: DACS通过智能体触发的不对称上下文隔离机制，有效解决了多智能体LLM编排中的上下文污染问题，显著提升了决策准确性和上下文效率，且优势随智能体数量和决策密度增加而增强。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM orchestration systems suffer from context pollution: when N concurrent agents compete for the orchestrator's context window, each agent's task state, partial outputs, and pending questions contaminate the steering interactions of every other agent, degrading decision quality. We introduce Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping (DACS), a mechanism in which the orchestrator operates in two asymmetric modes. In Registry mode it holds only lightweight per-agent status summaries (<=200 tokens each), remaining responsive to all agents and the user. When an agent emits a SteeringRequest, the orchestrator enters Focus(a_i) mode, injecting the full context of agent a_i while compressing all other agents to their registry entries. Context isolation is agent-triggered, asymmetric, and deterministic: the context window contains exactly F(a_i) + R_{-i} during steering, eliminating cross-agent contamination without requiring context compression or retrieval. We evaluate DACS across four experimental phases totalling 200 trials: Phase 1 tests N in {3,5,10} (60 trials); Phase 2 tests agent heterogeneity and adversarial dependencies (60 trials); Phase 3 tests decision density up to D=15 (40 trials); Phase 4 uses autonomous LLM agents for free-form questions (40 trials, Claude Haiku 4.5). Across all 8 synthetic scenarios, DACS achieves 90.0--98.4% steering accuracy versus 21.0--60.0% for a flat-context baseline (p < 0.0001 throughout), with wrong-agent contamination falling from 28--57% to 0--14% and context efficiency ratios of up to 3.53x. The accuracy advantage grows with N and D; keyword matching is validated by LLM-as-judge across all phases (mean kappa=0.909). DACS outperforms the flat-context baseline by +17.2pp at N=3 (p=0.0023) and +20.4pp at N=5 (p=0.0008) in Phase 4, with the advantage growing with N confirmed by two independent judges.

</details>


### [11] ["Theater of Mind" for LLMs: A Cognitive Architecture Based on Global Workspace Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08206)
*Wenlong Shang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Global Workspace Agents (GWA)架构，将多智能体协调从被动数据结构转变为主动事件驱动的离散动力系统，通过熵基内在驱动机制和双层记忆分叉策略实现持续自主的LLM智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM本质上是BIBO系统，仅在明确提示时被动响应，缺乏内在时间连续性。现有多智能体框架依赖静态内存池和被动消息传递，导致认知停滞和同质化死锁，无法实现真正的自主人工智能。

Method: 1. 基于全局工作空间理论构建GWA认知架构；2. 中央广播枢纽与功能受限异质智能体群耦合，形成连续认知循环；3. 引入熵基内在驱动机制，数学量化语义多样性，动态调节生成温度以自主打破推理死锁；4. 采用双层记忆分叉策略确保长期认知连续性。

Result: GWA提供了一个稳健、可复现的工程框架，能够实现持续、自导向的LLM智能体，有效解决了现有多智能体系统中的认知停滞和同质化死锁问题。

Conclusion: GWA架构通过将多智能体协调转变为主动事件驱动系统，结合熵基驱动机制和记忆分叉策略，为构建真正自主的人工智能系统提供了理论基础和工程实现路径。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) operate fundamentally as Bounded-Input Bounded-Output (BIBO) systems. They remain in a passive state until explicitly prompted, computing localized responses without intrinsic temporal continuity. While effective for isolated tasks, this reactive paradigm presents a critical bottleneck for engineering autonomous artificial intelligence. Current multi-agent frameworks attempt to distribute cognitive load but frequently rely on static memory pools and passive message passing, which inevitably leads to cognitive stagnation and homogeneous deadlocks during extended execution. To address this structural limitation, we propose Global Workspace Agents (GWA), a cognitive architecture inspired by Global Workspace Theory. GWA transitions multi-agent coordination from a passive data structure to an active, event-driven discrete dynamical system. By coupling a central broadcast hub with a heterogeneous swarm of functionally constrained agents, the system maintains a continuous cognitive cycle. Furthermore, we introduce an entropy-based intrinsic drive mechanism that mathematically quantifies semantic diversity, dynamically regulating generation temperature to autonomously break reasoning deadlocks. Coupled with a dual-layer memory bifurcation strategy to ensure long-term cognitive continuity, GWA provides a robust, reproducible engineering framework for sustained, self-directed LLM agency.

</details>


### [12] [MemCoT: Test-Time Scaling through Memory-Driven Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08216)
*Haodong Lei,Junming Liu,Yirong Chen,Ding Wang,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MemCoT是一个测试时内存扩展框架，通过将长上下文推理转化为迭代式状态化信息搜索，解决LLM在因果推理中的幻觉和遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在长上下文因果推理中仍存在严重幻觉和灾难性遗忘问题，现有内存机制将检索视为静态、单步的被动匹配过程，导致语义稀释和上下文碎片化。

Method: 提出MemCoT框架：1）多视图长期记忆感知模块，支持Zoom-In证据定位和Zoom-Out上下文扩展；2）任务条件化双短期记忆系统，包含语义状态记忆和情节轨迹记忆，动态指导查询分解和剪枝。

Result: MemCoT实现了最先进的性能，多个开源和闭源模型在LoCoMo基准和LongMemEval-S基准上达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: MemCoT通过重新定义推理过程，将长上下文推理转化为迭代式状态化信息搜索，有效解决了LLM在长上下文因果推理中的根本瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still suffer from severe hallucinations and catastrophic forgetting during causal reasoning over massive, fragmented long contexts. Existing memory mechanisms typically treat retrieval as a static, single-step passive matching process, leading to severe semantic dilution and contextual fragmentation. To overcome these fundamental bottlenecks, we propose MemCoT, a test-time memory scaling framework that redefines the reasoning process by transforming long-context reasoning into an iterative, stateful information search. MemCoT introduces a multi-view long-term memory perception module that enables Zoom-In evidence localization and Zoom-Out contextual expansion, allowing the model to first identify where relevant evidence resides and then reconstruct the surrounding causal structure necessary for reasoning. In addition, MemCoT employs a task-conditioned dual short-term memory system composed of semantic state memory and episodic trajectory memory. This short-term memory records historical search decisions and dynamically guides query decomposition and pruning across iterations. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemCoT establishes a state-of-the-art performance. Empowered by MemCoT, several open- and closed-source models achieve SOTA performance on the LoCoMo benchmark and LongMemEval-S benchmark.

</details>

### [13] [SynQL: A Controllable and Scalable Rule-Based Framework for SQL Workload Synthesis for Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08021)
*Kahan Mehta,Amit Mankodi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SynQL是一个确定性SQL工作负载合成框架，通过遍历数据库外键图生成结构多样、可执行的SQL查询，解决了训练数据获取难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私法规限制，获取真实SQL工作负载日益困难，现有合成工具存在模式幻觉和拓扑塌陷问题，无法为学习型查询优化器提供足够的训练数据。

Method: 放弃概率文本生成，采用确定性方法：遍历数据库外键图填充抽象语法树，通过配置向量Θ控制连接拓扑、分析强度和谓词选择性，保证模式有效性。

Result: 在TPC-H和IMDb上实验显示，SynQL生成接近最大多样性的工作负载（拓扑熵H=1.53比特），基于树的成本模型在合成测试集上达到R²≥0.79，推理延迟亚毫秒。

Conclusion: SynQL为无法访问生产日志时生成训练数据提供了有效基础，特别适用于学习型查询优化器的训练数据合成。

Abstract: Database research and the development of learned query optimisers rely heavily on realistic SQL workloads. Acquiring real-world queries is increasingly difficult, however, due to strict privacy regulations, and publicly released anonymised traces typically strip out executable query text to preserve confidentiality. Existing synthesis tools fail to bridge this training data gap: traditional benchmarks offer too few fixed templates for statistical generalisation, while Large Language Model (LLM) approaches suffer from schema hallucination fabricating non-existent columns and topological collapse systematically defaulting to simplistic join patterns that fail to stress-test query optimisers. We propose SynQL, a deterministic workload synthesis framework that generates structurally diverse, execution-ready SQL workloads. As a foundational step toward bridging the training-data gap, SynQL targets the core SQL fragment -- multi-table joins with projections, aggregations, and range predicates -- which dominates analytical workloads. SynQL abandons probabilistic text generation in favour of traversing the live database's foreign-key graph to populate an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), guaranteeing schema and syntactic validity by construction. A configuration vector $Θ$ provides explicit, parametric control over join topology (Star, Chain, Fork), analytical intensity, and predicate selectivity. Experiments on TPC-H and IMDb show that SynQL produces near-maximally diverse workloads (Topological Entropy $H = 1.53$ bits) and that tree-based cost models trained on the synthetic corpus achieve $R^2 \ge 0.79$ on held-out synthetic test sets with sub-millisecond inference latency, establishing SynQL as an effective foundation for generating training data when production logs are inaccessible.

</details>

### [14] [Formally Guaranteed Control Adaptation for ODD-Resilient Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07414)
*Gricel Vázquez,Calum Imrie,Sepeedeh Shahbeigi,Nawshin Mannan Proma,Tian Gan,Victoria J Hodge,John Molloy,Simos Gerasimou*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应概率系统模型方法，用于处理超出设计域（ODD）的场景，同时提供定量保证，提高自主系统在意外情况下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保自主系统在超出操作设计域（ODD）的情况下仍能保持可靠性能是构建弹性自主系统的主要挑战。当前系统在面对未预见情况时缺乏有效的适应机制和形式化保证。

Method: 引入自适应概率系统模型方法，动态扩展现有系统情境处理能力的覆盖范围。该方法支持在未预见情境下对系统行为进行验证和适应，同时提供定量保证。

Result: 初步结果表明，该方法能有效提高系统可靠性，即使在未预见的超出ODD情境下，也能通过自适应行为调整和提供形式化保证来增强系统性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应概率系统模型方法为解决自主系统在超出设计域情况下的可靠性问题提供了有效途径，通过动态扩展情境处理能力和提供定量保证，增强了系统的弹性。

Abstract: Ensuring reliable performance in situations outside the Operational Design Domain (ODD) remains a primary challenge in devising resilient autonomous systems. We explore this challenge by introducing an approach for adapting probabilistic system models to handle out-of-ODD scenarios while, in parallel, providing quantitative guarantees. Our approach dynamically extends the coverage of existing system situation capabilities, supporting the verification and adaptation of the system's behaviour under unanticipated situations. Preliminary results demonstrate that our approach effectively increases system reliability by adapting its behaviour and providing formal guarantees even under unforeseen out-of-ODD situations.

</details>


### [15] [When Equality Fails as a Rewrite Principle: Provenance and Definedness for Measurement-Bearing Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07626)
*David B. Hulak,Arthur F. Ramos,Ruy J. G. B. de Queiroz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种跟踪来源和定义性的统一语义，用于处理带有测量的表达式，解决了代数等式在测量表达式中不成立的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通代数等式对于带有测量的表达式不是可靠的改写原则，因为相同观测的重用很重要，除法可能使代数相等的形式在定义域上产生差异。

Method: 提出令牌敏感的包围语义来跟踪来源和定义性，通过可容许域精化获得域安全的改写判断，支持相对变体连接局部和全局可容许性。

Result: 建立了单向改写和可互换性的判断，恢复定理内化了消去、背景减法和正区间自除法，严格性定理显示可达奇点使简化变为单向。

Conclusion: 令牌身份对于保持测量表达式的语义区分至关重要，所有定义和定理都在Lean 4中形式化验证，为测量计算提供了可靠的语义基础。

Abstract: Ordinary algebraic equality is not a sound rewrite principle for measurement-bearing expressions. Reuse of the same observation matters, and division can make algebraically equal forms differ on where they are defined. We present a unified semantics that tracks both provenance and definedness. Token-sensitive enclosure semantics yields judgments for one-way rewriting and interchangeability. An admissible-domain refinement yields a domain-safe rewrite judgment, and support-relative variants connect local and global admissibility. Reduction theorems recover the enclosure-based theory on universally admissible supports. Recovery theorems internalize cancellation, background subtraction, and positive-interval self-division. Strictness theorems show that reachable singularities make simplification one-way and make common-domain equality too weak for licensed replacement. An insufficiency theorem shows that erasing token identity collapses distinctions that definedness alone cannot recover. All definitions and theorems are formalized in sorry-free Lean 4.

</details>

### [16] [Towards Counterfactual Explanation and Assertion Inference for CPS Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07679)
*Zaid Ghazal,Hadiza Yusuf,Khouloud Gaaloul*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeCaF是一个用于CPS调试的反事实引导解释和断言表征框架，通过生成最小化的输入信号反事实修改来将失败测试转变为通过测试，并推断可解释的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS调试技术虽然能定位异常到特定模型组件，但无法揭示触发违规的输入信号值、时序条件，或能防止失败的最小精确时间修改。需要一种能提供更深入洞察的调试方法。

Method: DeCaF框架包含：1）给定失败测试输入，生成将测试从失败转变为通过的最小、必要且充分的反事实输入信号修改；2）推断输入上的逻辑谓词作为断言，以可解释形式泛化恢复条件；3）结合三种反事实生成器和两种因果模型，推断成功断言。

Result: 在三个CPS案例研究中，KD-Tree最近邻结合M5模型树获得最佳成功率，而遗传算法结合随机森林在成功率和因果精度之间提供最强平衡。

Conclusion: DeCaF为CPS调试提供了有效的反事实引导解释和断言表征框架，能够生成最小修改并推断可解释的恢复条件，帮助工程师理解失败原因而不需要访问内部模型细节。

Abstract: Verification and validation of cyber-physical systems (CPS) via large-scale simulation often surface failures that are hard to interpret, especially when triggered by interactions between continuous and discrete behaviors at specific events or times. Existing debugging techniques can localize anomalies to specific model components, but they provide little insight into the input-signal values and timing conditions that trigger violations, or the minimal, precisely timed changes that could have prevented the failure. In this article, we introduce DeCaF, a counterfactual-guided explanation and assertion-based characterization framework for CPS debugging. Given a failing test input, DeCaF generates counterfactual changes to the input signals that transform the test from failing to passing. These changes are designed to be minimal, necessary, and sufficient to precisely restore correctness. Then, it infers assertions as logical predicates over inputs that generalize recovery conditions in an interpretable form engineers can reason about, without requiring access to internal model details. Our approach combines three counterfactual generators with two causal models, and infers success assertions. Across three CPS case studies, DeCaF achieves its best success rate with KD-Tree Nearest Neighbors combined with M5 model tree, while Genetic Algorithm combined with Random Forest provides the strongest balance between success and causal precision.

</details>


### [17] [MVOS_HSI: A Python Library for Preprocessing Agricultural Crop Hyperspectral Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07656)
*Rishik Aggarwal,Krisha Joshi,Pappu Kumar Yadav,Jianwei Qin,Thomas F. Burks,Moon S. Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MVOS_HSI是一个开源Python库，为叶片级高光谱成像数据提供端到端处理流程，旨在解决植物表型研究中数据处理标准化和可重复性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像能够无损检测植物性状，但数据处理通常依赖实验室特定的MATLAB或Python脚本，导致工作流程难以共享、结果难以重复。需要标准化工具来提高植物表型研究的可重复性和一致性。

Method: 开发开源Python库MVOS_HSI，提供从原始ENVI文件校准到基于多种植被指数（NDVI、CIRedEdge和GCI）的叶片检测和裁剪的完整工作流程。包含数据增强工具用于机器学习训练，以及光谱剖面可视化工具。可作为Python库导入或命令行工具运行。

Result: MVOS_HSI成功整合了高光谱数据处理中的常见任务，提供了标准化的工作流程。代码和文档已在GitHub上公开可用，支持植物表型研究人员获得一致且可重复的结果。

Conclusion: MVOS_HSI通过将高光谱数据处理任务整合到单一软件包中，帮助研究人员在植物表型研究中产生一致且可重复的结果，解决了现有数据处理方法分散和不可重复的问题。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) allows researchers to study plant traits non-destructively. By capturing hundreds of narrow spectral bands per pixel, it reveals details about plant biochemistry and stress that standard cameras miss. However, processing this data is often challenging. Many labs still rely on loosely organized collections of lab-specific MATLAB or Python scripts, which makes workflows difficult to share and results difficult to reproduce. MVOS_HSI is an open-source Python library that provides an end-to-end workflow for processing leaf-level HSI data. The software handles everything from calibrating raw ENVI files to detecting and clipping individual leaves based on multiple vegetation indices (NDVI, CIRedEdge and GCI). It also includes tools for data augmentation to create training-time variations for machine learning and utilities to visualize spectral profiles. MVOS_HSI can be used as an importable Python library or run directly from the command line. The code and documentation are available on GitHub. By consolidating these common tasks into a single package, MVOS_HSI helps researchers produce consistent and reproducible results in plant phenotyping

</details>


### [18] [Externalization in LLM Agents: A Unified Review of Memory, Skills, Protocols and Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08224)
*Chenyu Zhou,Huacan Chai,Wenteng Chen,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Yuanyi Song,Tianyi Xu,Yingxuan Yang,Aofan Yu,Weiming Zhang,Congming Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Zeyu Zheng,Zhuosheng Zhang,Xingyu Lou,Changwang Zhang,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"外部化"框架，分析LLM智能体从权重依赖转向运行时基础设施重构的趋势，认为记忆、技能和协议是三种关键外部化形式，系统级协调对实际智能体进步至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体开发正从改变模型权重转向重构运行时基础设施，将原本期望模型内部实现的能力外部化为记忆存储、可重用技能、交互协议等模块。论文旨在通过"外部化"视角分析这一转变，解释为什么实际智能体进步越来越依赖外部认知基础设施而非仅更强模型。

Method: 采用认知人工物理论框架，提出外部化分析视角：1) 记忆外部化跨时间状态；2) 技能外部化程序性专业知识；3) 协议外部化交互结构；4) 协调层统一管理执行。通过历史分析（从权重到上下文到协调层）、三类外部化形式分析及其在智能体系统中的交互，探讨参数化能力与外部化能力权衡。

Result: 建立系统级框架，阐明记忆、技能和协议作为三种耦合但不同的外部化形式如何相互作用，识别自演化协调层和共享智能体基础设施等新兴方向，揭示模型与外部基础设施长期共同演化中的评估、治理等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 实际智能体进步不仅依赖更强模型，更依赖更好的外部认知基础设施。外部化框架将困难认知负担转化为模型可更可靠解决的形式，协调层作为统一层将记忆、技能和协议协调为受治理执行，这是理解现代LLM智能体系统设计的关键视角。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly built less by changing model weights than by reorganizing the runtime around them. Capabilities that earlier systems expected the model to recover internally are now externalized into memory stores, reusable skills, interaction protocols, and the surrounding harness that makes these modules reliable in practice. This paper reviews that shift through the lens of externalization. Drawing on the idea of cognitive artifacts, we argue that agent infrastructure matters not merely because it adds auxiliary components, but because it transforms hard cognitive burdens into forms that the model can solve more reliably. Under this view, memory externalizes state across time, skills externalize procedural expertise, protocols externalize interaction structure, and harness engineering serves as the unification layer that coordinates them into governed execution. We trace a historical progression from weights to context to harness, analyze memory, skills, and protocols as three distinct but coupled forms of externalization, and examine how they interact inside a larger agent system. We further discuss the trade-off between parametric and externalized capability, identify emerging directions such as self-evolving harnesses and shared agent infrastructure, and discuss open challenges in evaluation, governance, and the long-term co-evolution of models and external infrastructure. The result is a systems-level framework for explaining why practical agent progress increasingly depends not only on stronger models, but on better external cognitive infrastructure.

</details>


### [19] [MIMIC-Py: An Extensible Tool for Personality-Driven Automated Game Testing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07752)
*Yifei Chen,Sarra Habchi,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIMIC-Py是一个基于Python的自动化游戏测试工具，将基于人格的LLM智能体转化为可重用、可扩展的框架，支持通过API或合成代码与游戏交互，减少新游戏部署的工程工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频游戏是复杂、非确定性的系统，难以大规模自动测试。现有基于人格的LLM智能体工具大多是研究原型，缺乏跨游戏的可重用性，需要将研究原型转化为实用的自动化游戏测试工具。

Method: MIMIC-Py采用模块化架构，将规划、执行和记忆与游戏特定逻辑解耦，将人格特质作为可配置输入，支持多种交互机制（通过暴露的API或合成代码），实现跨游戏重用。

Result: MIMIC-Py能够以最小工程工作量部署到新游戏环境，弥合研究原型与实用自动化游戏测试之间的差距，提供可重用和可扩展的框架。

Conclusion: MIMIC-Py通过将基于人格的LLM智能体转化为可重用框架，解决了自动化游戏测试中跨游戏可重用性的挑战，为实际应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Modern video games are complex, non-deterministic systems that are difficult to test automatically at scale. Although prior work shows that personality-driven Large Language Model (LLM) agents can improve behavioural diversity and test coverage, existing tools largely remain research prototypes and lack cross-game reusability.
  This tool paper presents MIMIC-Py, a Python-based automated game-testing tool that transforms personality-driven LLM agents into a reusable and extensible framework. MIMIC-Py exposes personality traits as configurable inputs and adopts a modular architecture that decouples planning, execution, and memory from game-specific logic. It supports multiple interaction mechanisms, enabling agents to interact with games via exposed APIs or synthesized code. We describe the design of MIMIC-Py and show how it enables deployment to new game environments with minimal engineering effort, bridging the gap between research prototypes and practical automated game testing.
  The source code and a demo video are available on our project webpage: https://mimic-persona.github.io/MIMIC-Py-Home-Page/.

</details>


### [20] [An Empirical Study on Influence-Based Pretraining Data Selection for Code Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07769)
*Chengli Xing,Zhengran Zeng,Gexiang Fang,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探索了数据影响力分数过滤方法在编程数据集上的有效性，发现基于验证集损失的数据影响力分数过滤能提升代码大语言模型的编程性能，且不同下游编程任务对有益训练数据的标准存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据过滤研究主要关注通用数据集，对编程数据集的关注不足。研究者认识到预训练数据质量对提升LLM性能至关重要，因此探索通用数据过滤技术（数据影响力分数过滤）在编程相关数据集上的有效性。

Method: 1. 提出生成式编程任务的数据影响力分数计算方法：将多种下游编码任务转化为验证集，使用模型在这些集上的损失作为性能指标；2. 从头开始预训练一个10亿参数的Code-LLM，使用1000亿代码标记的数据集；3. 进行广泛的实证研究，评估数据影响力分数过滤方法的有效性。

Result: 1. 基于验证集损失的数据影响力分数过滤能提升模型的编程性能；2. 有益训练数据的标准在不同下游编程任务间存在显著差异；3. 基于预测的数据影响力分数过滤方法的可行性得到评估。

Conclusion: 数据影响力分数过滤方法在编程数据集上有效，但不同编程任务需要不同的数据筛选标准，这为Code-LLM的预训练数据优化提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Recent advancements in code large language models (Code-LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in resolving programming related tasks. Meanwhile, researchers have recognized that the quality of pre-training data is crucial for improving LLM performance. However, most of the existing research on pre-training data filtering has focused on general datasets, and little attention for programming datasets. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by exploring the effectiveness of a widely used general data filtering technique, i.e., data-influence-score filtering, within the context of programming-related datasets. To this end, we first introduce a method for calculating data-influence-score for generative programming tasks which involves transforming a variety of downstream coding tasks into validation sets and using the models loss on these sets as a performance metric. Next, we pre-train a Code-LLMs with 1 billion parameters from scratch on a dataset of 100 billion code tokens. Based on it, we conduct an extensive empirical study to evaluate the effectiveness of data-influence-score filtering methods. Specifically, we examine how well this technique improves model performance, investigate how the characteristics of beneficial training data vary across different training stages and programming tasks, and assess the feasibility of prediction-based data-influence-score filtering method. Our findings show that data-influence-score filtering based on validation-set-loss can enhance models programming performance. Moreover, we observe that the criteria of beneficial training data differ significantly across various downstream programming tasks.

</details>


### [21] [To Copilot and Beyond: 22 AI Systems Developers Want Built](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07830)
*Rudrajit Choudhuri,Christian Bird,Carmen Badea,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发者希望AI工具能处理编码之外的辅助工作，但保持对核心编程活动的控制，形成"有界委托"模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者仅将约十分之一的工作时间用于编写代码，但现有AI工具主要针对编码环节。本研究旨在探索开发者工作流程中其他部分需要什么样的AI支持，以及他们希望AI在哪些方面保持距离。

Method: 对860名微软开发者进行问卷调查，采用人机协同、多模型委员会主题分析方法，识别开发者希望构建的AI系统。

Result: 识别出5个任务类别中的22个AI系统需求，发现开发者希望AI能更早地在工作流程中嵌入质量信号以跟上加速的代码生成，同时在整个过程中实施明确的权限范围、来源追踪、不确定性信号和最小权限访问。

Conclusion: 开发者希望AI吸收围绕其技艺的组装工作，而非技艺本身，形成"有界委托"模式。这一边界反映了他们的专业身份认同，表明AI工具的价值不仅在于它能做什么，更在于它在何处以及如何精确地停止。

Abstract: Developers spend roughly one-tenth of their workday writing code, yet most AI tooling targets that fraction. This paper asks what should be built for the rest. We surveyed 860 Microsoft developers to understand where they want AI support, and where they want it to stay out. Using a human-in-the-loop, multi-model council-based thematic analysis, we identify 22 AI systems that developers want built across five task categories. For each, we describe the problem it solves, what makes it hard to build, and the constraints developers place on its behavior. Our findings point to a growing right-shift burden in AI-assisted development: developers wanted systems that embed quality signals earlier in their workflow to keep pace with accelerating code generation, while enforcing explicit authority scoping, provenance, uncertainty signaling, and least-privilege access throughout. This tension reveals a pattern we call "bounded delegation": developers wanted AI to absorb the assembly work surrounding their craft, never the craft itself. That boundary tracks where they locate professional identity, suggesting that the value of AI tooling may lie as much in where and how precisely it stops as in what it does.

</details>


### [22] [ZeroCoder: Can LLMs Improve Code Generation Without Ground-Truth Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07864)
*Lishui Fan,Mouxiang Chen,Tingwei Zhu,Kui Liu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ZeroCoder是一个完全无标签的协同进化框架，通过联合训练代码生成器和测试生成器，利用自生成代码-测试交互的执行反馈来改进代码生成，无需人工标注的测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法依赖人工标注的测试用例，这使得进展受到稀缺且昂贵监督的瓶颈限制。现有使用自生成测试的方法由于测试生成模型的次优性能而效果有限，需要一种无需真实监督就能改进代码生成的方法。

Method: 提出ZeroCoder框架：1）协同进化代码生成器（Coder）和测试生成器（Tester）；2）通过执行采样的解决方案对采样的测试形成通过矩阵；3）使用可插拔的选择算法识别可能正确的解决方案和一致测试的共识子集；4）通过基于排名的预过滤确保奖励质量；5）使用课程学习平衡测试的有效性和突变驱动的区分性；6）引入DyB4贝叶斯选择器动态重新校准先验，解决选择器漂移问题。

Result: 在三个模型和六个基准测试中，ZeroCoder持续改进代码生成和测试生成。在完全无标签设置下，在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上代码生成改进高达14.5%。使用DyB4时，增益达到21.6%，测试生成改进24.3%，接近有监督的性能。

Conclusion: ZeroCoder通过协同进化代码和测试生成，利用自生成的代码-测试交互提供渐进式信息监督，有效解决了无监督代码生成的瓶颈。DyB4选择器进一步解决了协同进化过程中的选择器漂移问题，使系统能够接近有监督的性能水平。

Abstract: Code generation is important in software engineering, and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a powerful paradigm to improve it through execution-based feedback. However, most RLVR pipelines rely on human-curated tests, making progress bottlenecked by scarce and costly supervision. Existing work tried to use self-generated tests to ground rewards, but the lack of discriminative tests constrains the effect due to the sub-optimal performance of the model on test generation. We aim to improve code generation without ground-truth supervision by co-evolving code and test generation, so that their interactions yield progressively more informative supervision. To this end, we present ZeroCoder, a fully label-free co-evolutionary framework that jointly trains a Coder and a Tester using execution feedback from self-generated code-test interactions. For each problem, ZeroCoder executes sampled solutions against sampled tests to form a passing matrix, identifies a consensus subset of likely-correct solutions and consistent tests via a pluggable selection algorithm, and derives role-specific rewards. To ensure reward quality, ZeroCoder filters low-information instances via rank-based pre-filtering and trains the Tester with a curriculum balancing validity and mutation-driven discriminativeness. We further identify selector drift, the progressive miscalibration of fixed selection rules during co-evolution, and introduce DyB4, a Bayesian selector that uses as few as 10 labeled instances to recalibrate its priors dynamically. Across three models and six benchmarks, ZeroCoder consistently improves code generation and test generation. In the fully label-free setting, it improves code generation by up to 14.5% over the base model on Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct. With DyB4, the gain reaches 21.6%, while test generation improves by 24.3%, approaching oracle-supervised performance.

</details>


### [23] [Investigating Code Reuse in Software Redesign: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07919)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Huaien Zhang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对跨仓库软件重构中代码和测试重用的挑战，提出基于克隆检测的语义对齐启发式方法，显著减少无关克隆并提高映射精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件重构在保持功能的同时提升质量属性，但跨仓库重构中手动重用代码和测试成本高且易出错，特别是在静态分析器等需要跨仓库重构的场景中。现有方法难以有效跟踪原始项目与重构项目间的代码和测试对应关系。

Method: 采用行动研究方法论，结合实证调查与开源贡献验证。通过改造克隆检测技术来推导原始项目与重构项目间的代码映射，基于研究中发现的语义重用模式提出语义对齐启发式方法和可扩展的分层检测策略。

Result: 在两个重构项目对（Soot/SootUp 和 FindBugs/SpotBugs）上的评估显示：在SAS阈值为0.5时，平均减少33-99%的可能无关克隆；在1,749个样本的基准测试中，精度提升至86%。同时向重构项目提交了5个问题和10个拉取请求，其中8个已被合并。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了跨仓库重构中的关键挑战（非线性迁移、双向重用需求、TODO延迟重用、测试移植被忽视、残留缺陷传播），并提出基于克隆检测的语义对齐方法能有效解决代码和测试跟踪问题，为软件重构实践提供了实用工具和方法。

Abstract: Software redesign preserves functionality while improving quality attributes, but manual reuse of code and tests is costly and error-prone, especially in crossrepository redesigns. Focusing on static analyzers where cross-repo redesign needs often arise, we conduct a bidirectional study of the ongoing Soot/SootUp redesign case using an action research methodology that combines empirical investigation with validated open-source contributions. Our study reveals: (1) non-linear migration which necessitates bidirectional reuse, (2) deferred reuse via TODOs, (3) neglected test porting, and (4) residual bug propagation during migrations. We identify tracking corresponding code and tests as the key challenge, and address it by retrofitting clone detection to derive code mappings between original and redesigned projects. Guided by semantic reuse patterns derived in our study, we propose Semantic Alignment Heuristics and a scalable hierarchical detection strategy. Evaluations on two redesigned project pairs (Soot/SootUp and FindBugs/SpotBugs) show that our approach achieves an average reduction of 33-99% in likely irrelevant clones at a SAS threshold of 0.5 across all tool results, and improves precision up to 86% on our benchmark of 1,749 samples. Moreover, we contribute to the redesigned projects by submitting five issues and 10 pull requests, of which eight have been merged.

</details>


### [24] [Log-based, Business-aware REST API Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08007)
*Ding Yang,Ruixiang Qian,Zhao Wei,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LoBREST是一种基于日志的业务感知REST API测试技术，利用历史请求日志有效测试业务敏感功能，相比现有规范驱动方法有显著改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于REST规范（如Swagger/OpenAPI）的测试技术主要针对简单的资源CRUD操作，但无法有效测试复杂的业务敏感功能，因为这些功能需要额外的业务约束信息，而这些信息通常不在REST规范中

Method: LoBREST采用三阶段方法：1) 使用局部切片策略将历史请求日志划分为较小切片以保留完整业务约束；2) 通过添加缺失操作切片和补全切片内缺失资源来增强切片；3) 使用增强切片作为初始种子进行业务感知模糊测试以提高测试充分性

Result: 在17个真实服务上评估，LoBREST优于8个现有工具（包括Arat-rl、Morest和Deeprest），在16个服务上获得最佳操作覆盖率，在15个服务上获得最佳行覆盖率，平均比第二名分别提高2.1倍和1.2倍，检测到108个5XX错误，其中38个是其他工具未发现的

Conclusion: LoBREST通过利用历史请求日志中的业务约束信息，有效解决了现有规范驱动REST API测试技术在业务敏感功能测试方面的不足，显著提高了测试覆盖率和错误检测能力

Abstract: REST APIs enable collaboration among microservices. A single fault in a REST API can bring down the entire microservice system and cause significant financial losses, underscoring the importance of REST API testing. Effectively testing REST APIs requires thoroughly exercising the functionalities behind them. To this end, existing techniques leverage REST specifications (e.g., Swagger or OpenAPI) to generate test cases. Using the resource constraints extracted from specifications, these techniques work well for testing simple, business-insensitive functionalities, such as resource creation, retrieval, update, and deletion. However, for complex, business-sensitive functionalities, these specification-based techniques often fall short, since exercising such functionalities requires additional business constraints that are typically absent from REST specifications. In this paper, we present LoBREST, a log-based, business-aware REST API testing technique that leverages historical request logs (HRLogs) to effectively exercise the business-sensitive functionalities behind REST APIs. To obtain compact operation sequences that preserve clean and complete business constraints, LoBREST first employs a locality-slicing strategy to partition HRLogs into smaller slices. Then, to ensure the effectiveness of the obtained slices, LoBREST enhances them in two steps: (1) adding slices for operations missing from HRLogs, and (2) completing missing resources within the slices. Finally, to improve test adequacy, LoBREST uses these enhanced slices as initial seeds to perform business-aware fuzzing. LoBREST outperformed eight tools (including Arat-rl, Morest, and Deeprest) across 17 real-world services. It achieved top operation coverage on 16 services and line coverage on 15, averaging 2.1x and 1.2x improvements over the runner-up. LoBREST detected 108 5XX bugs, including 38 found by no other tool.

</details>


### [25] [A Comparative Study of Semantic Log Representations for Software Log-based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08028)
*Yuqing Wang,Ying Song,Xiaozhou Li,Nana Reinikainen,Mika V. Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对日志异常检测中的语义表示方法进行基准测试，发现BERT方法效果更好但效率低，静态词嵌入方法效率高但效果差。作者提出QTyBERT方法，通过轻量级BERT变体和量化技术，在保持检测效果的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的日志异常检测方法通常使用语义表示方法将日志文本转换为向量嵌入，但这些方法通常作为端到端管道进行评估，不同语义表示方法的影响尚未得到充分理解。需要系统评估各种语义表示方法的性能，并解决现有方法在效果和效率之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1. 对广泛使用的语义日志表示方法进行基准测试，包括静态词嵌入方法（Word2Vec、GloVe、FastText）和基于BERT的上下文嵌入方法；2. 提出QTyBERT方法：使用SysBE（轻量级BERT变体）结合系统特定量化技术，在CPU上高效编码日志事件；3. 使用CroSysEh增强日志嵌入的语义表达能力，通过无监督训练从多个系统的未标记日志中学习BERT模型嵌入空间的底层语义结构。

Result: 在三个公开日志数据集（BGL、Thunderbird、Spirit）上的实验表明：1. BERT方法检测效果更好，但日志嵌入生成时间显著更长；2. 静态词嵌入方法效率高，但检测效果一般较差；3. QTyBERT生成的日志嵌入在深度学习模型中达到与BERT相当或更好的检测效果，同时将日志嵌入生成时间缩短到接近静态词嵌入方法的水平。

Conclusion: QTyBERT方法有效解决了日志异常检测中语义表示方法的效果-效率权衡问题，通过轻量级BERT变体、量化和语义增强技术，在保持高检测性能的同时大幅提升处理效率，为实际部署提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent deep learning (DL) methods for log anomaly detection increasingly rely on semantic log representation methods that convert the textual content of log events into vector embeddings as input to DL models. However, these DL methods are typically evaluated as end-to-end pipelines, while the impact of different semantic representation methods is not well understood. In this paper, we benchmark widely used semantic log representation methods, including static word embedding methods (Word2Vec, GloVe, and FastText) and the BERT-based contextual embedding method, across diverse DL models for log-event level anomaly detection on three publicly available log datasets: BGL, Thunderbird, and Spirit. We identify an effectiveness--efficiency trade off under CPU deployment settings: the BERT-based method is more effective, but incurs substantially longer log embedding generation time, limiting its practicality; static word embedding methods are efficient but are generally less effective and may yield insufficient detection performance. Motivated by this finding, we propose QTyBERT, a novel semantic log representation method that better balances this trade-off. QTyBERT uses SysBE, a lightweight BERT variant with system-specific quantization, to efficiently encode log events into vector embeddings on CPUs, and leverages CroSysEh to enhance the semantic expressiveness of these log embeddings. CroSysEh is trained unsupervisedly using unlabeled logs from multiple systems to capture the underlying semantic structure of the BERT model's embedding space. We evaluate QTyBERT against existing semantic log representation methods. Our results show that, for the DL models, using QTyBERT-generated log embeddings achieves detection effectiveness comparable to or better than BERT-generated log embeddings, while bringing log embedding generation time closer to that of static word embedding methods.

</details>


### [26] [Can LLMs Deobfuscate Binary Code? A Systematic Analysis of Large Language Models into Pseudocode Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08083)
*Li Hu,Xiuwei Shang,Jieke Shi,Shaoyin Cheng,Junqi Zhang,Gangyang Li,Zhou Yang,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BinDeObfBench是首个全面评估LLM在二进制去混淆任务中性能的基准测试，涵盖预编译、编译时和编译后多个阶段的混淆变换，研究发现推理能力和领域专业知识比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制代码去混淆是逆向工程中的基础挑战，混淆技术被广泛用于阻碍分析和隐藏程序逻辑。虽然大语言模型在从混淆二进制中恢复语义方面显示出潜力，但缺乏对其有效性的系统性评估。

Method: 提出了BinDeObfBench基准测试，用于评估LLM在二进制去混淆任务中的性能，涵盖预编译、编译时和编译后阶段的多种混淆变换。评估了不同模型在去混淆任务上的表现，包括标准模型和推理模型，并比较了任务特定监督微调与广泛领域预训练的效果。

Result: 去混淆性能更依赖于推理能力和领域专业知识，而非模型规模；任务特定的监督微调始终优于广泛领域预训练；推理模型在严重混淆下仍能保持鲁棒性，并能跨不同指令集架构和优化级别泛化；上下文学习对标准模型有益，但对推理模型的增益有限。

Conclusion: 研究强调了任务特定微调和推理驱动策略的重要性，BinDeObfBench为未来二进制去混淆研究奠定了基础，表明推理能力和领域专业知识是提升去混淆性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Deobfuscating binary code remains a fundamental challenge in reverse engineering, as obfuscation is widely used to hinder analysis and conceal program logic. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in recovering semantics from obfuscated binaries, a systematic evaluation of their effectiveness is still lacking. In this work, we present BinDeObfBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for assessing LLM-based binary deobfuscation across diverse transformations spanning pre-compilation, compile-time, and post-compilation stages. Our evaluation shows that deobfuscation performance depends more on reasoning capability and domain expertise than on model scale, and that task-specific supervised fine-tuning consistently outperforms broad domain pre-training. Reasoning models can maintain robustness under severe obfuscation, generalize across different instruction set architectures (ISAs) and optimization levels. In-context learning benefits standard models but yields limited gains for reasoning models. Overall, our study highlights the importance of task-specific fine-tuning and reasoning-driven strategies, and positions BinDeObfBench as a basis for future work in binary deobfuscation.

</details>


### [27] [GALA: Multimodal Graph Alignment for Bug Localization in Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08089)
*Zhuoyao Liu,Zhengran Zeng,Shu-Dong Huang,Yang Liu,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GALA是一个用于多模态程序修复的框架，通过图对齐方法将GUI截图中的视觉元素精确映射到代码组件，解决了现有方法因丢弃空间关系而导致的定位不准确问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多模态程序修复方法通常将图像转换为纯文本，丢弃了关键的空间关系信息，导致视觉观察与代码组件之间的严重脱节，使得定位退化为不精确的关键词匹配。

Method: GALA采用四阶段方法：1)构建图像UI图以捕获视觉元素及其结构关系；2)通过交叉引用UI图与仓库级结构进行文件级对齐；3)通过细粒度代码依赖推理进行函数级对齐；4)在已对齐的代码上下文中生成补丁。

Result: 在SWE-bench多模态基准测试中，GALA实现了最先进的性能，证明了层次化结构对齐方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GALA通过将多模态程序修复从隐式语义猜测转向显式结构推理，建立了高度准确的视觉到代码映射，显著提升了多模态场景下的程序修复能力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR) has shown strong potential on textual benchmarks, yet struggles in multimodal scenarios where bugs are reported with GUI screenshots. Existing methods typically convert images into plain text, which discards critical spatial relationships and causes a severe disconnect between visual observations and code components, leading localization to degrade into imprecise keyword matching. To bridge this gap, we propose GALA (Graph Alignment for Localization in APR), a framework that shifts multimodal APR from implicit semantic guessing to explicit structural reasoning. GALA operates in four stages: it first constructs an Image UI Graph to capture visual elements and their structural relationships; then performs file-level alignment by cross-referencing this UI graph with repository-level structures (e.g., file references) to locate candidate files; next conducts function-level alignment by reasoning over fine-grained code dependencies (e.g., call graphs) to precisely ground visual elements to corresponding code components; and finally performs patch generation within the grounded code context based on the aligned files and functions. By systematically enforcing both semantic and relational consistency across modalities, GALA establishes a highly accurate visual-to-code mapping. Evaluations on the SWE-bench Multimodal benchmark demonstrate that GALA achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical structural alignment.

</details>


### [28] [Test-Oriented Programming: rethinking coding for the GenAI era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08102)
*Jorge Melegati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出测试导向编程(TOP)新范式，开发者只需检查基于自然语言规范生成的测试代码，生产代码可委托给大语言模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代码生成工具主要分为改进自动补全的助手和多智能体系统，但作者认为LLM可以实现更高层次的抽象，让开发者从编写生产代码中解放出来。

Method: 开发概念验证工具，使用两种不同LLM生成小型命令行程序，通过自然语言规范生成测试代码，让LLM基于测试生成生产代码。

Result: 获得了有希望的结果，验证了TOP范式的可行性，但也识别出在实际项目中应用该范式面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 测试导向编程(TOP)是一种有前景的新编程范式，通过LLM将生产代码生成任务委托给模型，让开发者专注于测试验证，但实际应用仍需解决相关挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown astonishing capability of generating software code, leading to its use to support developers in programming. Proposed tools have relied either on assistants for improved auto-complete or multi-agents, in which different model instances are orchestrated to perform parts of a problem to reach a complete solution. We argue that LLMs can enable a higher-level of abstraction, a new paradigm we called Test-Oriented Programming (TOP). Within this paradigm, developers only have to check test code generated based on natural language specifications, rather than focusing on production code, which could be delegated to the LLMs. To evaluate the feasibility of this proposal, we developed a proof-of-concept tool and used it to generate a small command-line program employing two different LLMs. We obtained promising results and identified challenges for the use of this paradigm for real projects.

</details>


### [29] [Towards Improving the External Validity of Software Engineering Experiments with Transportability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08200)
*Julian Frattini,Richard Torkar,Robert Feldt,Carlo Furia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将可迁移性方法引入软件工程研究，通过结合实验数据和观察数据来提升控制实验的外部效度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中控制实验面临参与者代表性不足的问题，限制了实验结果的外部效度，而研究者通常拥有大量观察数据但未充分利用

Method: 引入可迁移性方法，结合实验和观察数据，通过模拟演示其潜力，并制定SE研究场景应用路线图和实用指南

Result: 展示了可迁移性方法在SE中的潜力，提出了具体应用场景（如学生替代开发者），制定了实施路线图

Conclusion: 采用可迁移性方法可以增强控制实验的外部效度，使SE研究结果更可靠且更具实践价值

Abstract: Controlled experiments are a core research method in software engineering (SE) for validating causal claims. However, recruiting a sample of participants that represents the intended target population is often difficult or expensive, which limits the external validity of experimental results. At the same time, SE researchers often have access to much larger amounts of observational than experimental data (e.g., from repositories, issue trackers, logs, surveys and industrial processes). Transportability methods combine these data from experimental and observational studies to "transport" results from the experimental sample to a broader, more representative sample of the target population. Although the ability to combine observational and experimental data in a principled way could substantially benefit empirical SE research, transportability methods have - to our knowledge - not been adopted in SE. In this vision, we aim to help make that adoption possible. To that end, we introduce transportability methods, their prerequisites, and demonstrate their potential through a simulation. We then outline several SE research scenarios in which these methods could apply, e.g., how to effectively use students as substitutes for developers. Finally, we outline a road map and practical guidelines to support SE researchers in applying them. Adopting transportability methods in SE research can strengthen the external validity of controlled experiments and help the field produce results that are both more reliable and more useful in practice.

</details>


### [30] [Empirical Evaluation of Taxonomic Trace Links: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08207)
*Waleed Abdeen,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Peter Löwenadler,Parisa Yousefi,Krzysztof Wnuk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TTL方法通过领域特定分类学连接源和目标工件，在工业环境中评估显示其在一个场景中有用，但在另一个场景中效果较差，需要改进分类器精度和解决异构工件结构挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可追溯性是知识密集型任务中工件的关键质量属性，但在实践中常被忽视，面临粒度、缺乏通用工件结构和责任不清等挑战。TTL方法旨在通过领域特定分类学解决这些常见可追溯性挑战。

Method: 在爱立信进行的混合方法研究，涉及一个软件产品。收集了两个可追溯性用例的定量和定性数据，建立了463个业务用例、64个测试用例和277个ISO标准需求之间的追溯链接，并举行了三个焦点小组会议。

Result: 识别出两个实际相关场景，TTL在一个场景中被认为是有用的解决方案，在另一个场景中效果较差。开发领域特定分类学和管理异构工件结构是主要挑战，分类器精度需要改进才能实用。

Conclusion: TTL是一种有前景的方法，可在实践中采用并支持可追溯性用例。但TTL不是传统追溯链接的替代品，而是补充它们以支持更多可追溯性用例并鼓励早期创建追溯链接。

Abstract: Context: Traceability is a key quality attribute of artifacts that are used in knowledge-intensive tasks and supports software engineers in producing higher-quality software. Despite its clear benefits, traceability is often neglected in practice due to challenges such as granularity of traces, lack of a common artifact structure, and unclear responsibility. The Taxonomic Trace Links (TTL) approach connects source and target artifacts through a domain-specific taxonomy, aiming to address these common traceability challenges. Objective: In this study, we empirically evaluate TTL in an industrial setting to identify its strengths and weaknesses for real-world adoption. Method: We conducted a mixed-methods study at Ericsson involving one of its software products. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected across two traceability use cases. We established trace links between 463 business use cases, 64 test cases, and 277 ISO-standard requirements. Additionally, we held three focus group sessions with practitioners. Results: We identified two practically relevant scenarios where traceability is required and evaluated TTL in each. Overall, practitioners found TTL to be a useful solution for one of the scenarios, while less useful for the other. However, developing a domain-specific taxonomy and managing heterogeneous artifact structures were noted as significant challenges. Moreover, the precision of the classifier that is used to create trace links needs to be improved to make the solution practical. Conclusion: TTL is a promising approach that can be adopted in practice and enables traceability use cases. However, TTL is not a replacement for traditional trace links, but rather complements them to enable more traceability use cases and encourage the early creation of trace links.

</details>


### [31] [Security Concerns in Generative AI Coding Assistants: Insights from Online Discussions on GitHub Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08352)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Monika Swetha Gurupathi,Zadia Codabux,Nalin Arachchilage,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Stack Overflow、Reddit和Hacker News等在线论坛的讨论，识别了开发者对基于生成式AI的编码助手（如GitHub Copilot）的四大安全关切领域：数据泄露风险、代码许可问题、对抗性攻击和不安全代码建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI已成为软件开发工具的核心组件，但现有研究主要关注其技术性能评估，而忽视了开发者在安全领域的新兴关切。本研究旨在探索开发者对GenAI编码助手的安全担忧，以弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 从Stack Overflow、Reddit和Hacker News三个流行平台收集关于GitHub Copilot安全问题的帖子、评论和讨论线程。使用BERTopic进行主题聚类，然后通过主题分析合成识别不同的安全关切类别。

Result: 识别出四大主要安全关切领域：1) 潜在数据泄露风险；2) 代码许可问题；3) 对抗性攻击（如提示注入）；4) 不安全代码建议。这些发现揭示了GenAI在软件工程中的局限性和权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于更广泛地理解开发者如何感知和参与基于GenAI的编码助手，同时突出了改进其内置安全功能的关键领域。这些发现对GenAI工具的安全设计和开发者教育具有重要意义。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) has become a central component of many development tools (e.g., GitHub Copilot) that support software practitioners across multiple programming tasks, including code completion, documentation, and bug detection. However, current research has identified significant limitations and open issues in GenAI, including reliability, non-determinism, bias, and copyright infringement. While prior work has primarily focused on assessing the technical performance of these technologies for code generation, less attention has been paid to emerging concerns of software developers, particularly in the security realm. OBJECTIVE: This work explores security concerns regarding the use of GenAI-based coding assistants by analyzing challenges voiced by developers and software enthusiasts in public online forums. METHOD: We retrieved posts, comments, and discussion threads addressing security issues in GitHub Copilot from three popular platforms, namely Stack Overflow, Reddit, and Hacker News. These discussions were clustered using BERTopic and then synthesized using thematic analysis to identify distinct categories of security concerns. RESULTS: Four major concern areas were identified, including potential data leakage, code licensing, adversarial attacks (e.g., prompt injection), and insecure code suggestions, underscoring critical reflections on the limitations and trade-offs of GenAI in software engineering. IMPLICATIONS: Our findings contribute to a broader understanding of how developers perceive and engage with GenAI-based coding assistants, while highlighting key areas for improving their built-in security features.

</details>


### [32] [CIAO - Code In Architecture Out - Automated Software Architecture Documentation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08293)
*Marco De Luca,Tiziano Santilli,Domenico Amalfitano,Anna Rita Fasolino,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CIAO是一个基于LLM的自动化流程，可从GitHub仓库生成符合标准的系统级架构文档，开发者评估认为其有价值且成本低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构文档对于系统理解至关重要，但常常缺失或不完整。现有的LLM技术主要处理局部代码片段，缺乏生成连贯系统级架构描述的能力。

Method: 提出CIAO（Code In Architecture Out）流程，基于LLM的工作流，从GitHub仓库输入，按照ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010、SEI Views & Beyond和C4模型模板生成系统级架构文档。

Result: 22名开发者评估显示，生成的文档被认为有价值、可理解且与源代码基本一致，但存在图表质量、高层上下文建模和部署视图方面的局限性。生成完整文档仅需几分钟且成本低廉。

Conclusion: 结构化、标准导向的方法能有效指导LLM生成既实用又经济高效的系统级架构文档，为自动化架构文档生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Software architecture documentation is essential for system comprehension, yet it is often unavailable or incomplete. While recent LLM-based techniques can generate documentation from code, they typically address local artifacts rather than producing coherent, system-level architectural descriptions. This paper presents a structured process for automatically generating system-level architectural documentation directly from GitHub repositories using Large Language Models. The process, called CIAO (Code In Architecture Out), defines an LLM-based workflow that takes a repository as input and produces system-level architectural documentation following a template derived from ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010, SEI Views \& Beyond, and the C4 model. The resulting documentation can be directly added to the target repository. We evaluated the process through a study with 22 developers, each reviewing the documentation generated for a repository they had contributed to. The evaluation shows that developers generally perceive the produced documentation as valuable, comprehensible, and broadly accurate with respect to the source code, while also highlighting limitations in diagram quality, high-level context modeling, and deployment views. We also assessed the operational cost of the process, finding that generating a complete architectural document requires only a few minutes and is inexpensive to run. Overall, the results indicate that a structured, standards-oriented approach can effectively guide LLMs in producing system-level architectural documentation that is both usable and cost-effective.

</details>


### [33] [Vulnerability Detection with Interprocedural Context in Multiple Languages: Assessing Effectiveness and Cost of Modern LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08417)
*Kevin Lira,Baldoino Fonseca,Davy Baía,Márcio Ribeiro,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究实证评估了四种现代大语言模型（Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4.1 Mini、GPT-5 Mini和Gemini 3 Flash）在检测跨函数依赖漏洞方面的效果、推理成本和解释质量，发现Gemini 3 Flash在C语言漏洞检测上具有最佳性价比，Claude Haiku 4.5在解释准确性方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的漏洞检测研究大多局限于单函数内部，忽略了跨函数的数据流和控制流依赖关系。这些跨过程依赖可能产生重要漏洞，因此需要研究如何利用调用者和被调用者的上下文信息来改进漏洞检测。

Method: 使用ReposVul数据集中的509个漏洞进行实证研究，系统性地改变跨过程上下文级别（仅目标函数代码、目标函数+调用者、目标函数+被调用者），在C、C++和Python三种编程语言上评估四种现代大语言模型（Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4.1 Mini、GPT-5 Mini、Gemini 3 Flash）。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash在C语言漏洞检测上表现出最佳成本效益平衡，F1分数≥0.978，每个配置的估计成本为$0.50-$0.58。Claude Haiku 4.5在93.6%的评估案例中正确识别并解释了漏洞。研究结果对设计能够跨多种编程语言代码库通用的AI辅助安全分析工具具有直接意义。

Conclusion: 跨过程上下文信息对基于大语言模型的漏洞检测至关重要，不同模型在不同语言和成本约束下表现各异。研究结果为构建实用的AI辅助安全分析工具提供了实证基础，强调了考虑跨函数依赖关系的重要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been a promising way for automated vulnerability detection. However, most prior studies have explored the use of LLMs to detect vulnerabilities only within single functions, disregarding those related to interprocedural dependencies. These studies overlook vulnerabilities that arise from data and control flows that span multiple functions. Thus, leveraging the context provided by callers and callees may help identify vulnerabilities. This study empirically investigates the effectiveness of detection, the inference cost, and the quality of explanations of four modern LLMs (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4.1 Mini, GPT-5 Mini, and Gemini 3 Flash) in detecting vulnerabilities related to interprocedural dependencies. To do that, we conducted an empirical study on 509 vulnerabilities from the ReposVul dataset, systematically varying the level of interprocedural context (target function code-only, target function + callers, and target function + callees) and evaluating the four modern LLMs across C, C++, and Python. The results show that Gemini 3 Flash offers the best cost-effectiveness trade-off for C vulnerabilities, achieving F1 >= 0.978 at an estimated cost of $0.50-$0.58 per configuration, and Claude Haiku 4.5 correctly identified and explained the vulnerability in 93.6% of the evaluated cases. Overall, the findings have direct implications for the design of AI-assisted security analysis tools that can generalize across codebases in multiple programming languages.

</details>


### [34] [ReCodeAgent: A Multi-Agent Workflow for Language-agnostic Translation and Validation of Large-scale Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07341)
*Ali Reza Ibrahimzada,Brandon Paulsen,Daniel Kroening,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReCodeAgent：首个完全自主的多智能体方法，用于语言无关的仓库级代码翻译与验证，支持多种编程语言对，显著提升翻译正确性和测试通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码翻译和验证技术通常只针对单一编程语言对，扩展新语言对需要大量工程努力。编程智能体可以实现语言无关性，但现有技术尚未提供完全自主的智能体方法用于大规模程序的仓库级代码翻译和验证。

Method: 提出ReCodeAgent，一种自主的多智能体方法，用户只需提供源语言项目和目标语言，系统即可自动翻译和验证整个仓库。该方法利用智能体合成多种编程语言的代码，并自主使用特定于每种编程语言的分析工具。

Result: 在118个真实项目（平均每个项目1975行代码，43个翻译单元）上评估，涵盖6种编程语言和4种语言对。ReCodeAgent在翻译正确性上持续优于先前技术，在真实测试上将测试通过率提高了60.8%，平均成本为15.3美元。多智能体架构相比单智能体架构，测试通过率下降40.4%，轨迹长度增加28%且效率持续低下。

Conclusion: ReCodeAgent是首个实现跨多种编程语言高翻译成功率的自主多智能体方法，为仓库级代码翻译和验证提供了语言无关的解决方案，多智能体架构设计对性能有显著影响。

Abstract: Most repository-level code translation and validation techniques have been evaluated on a single source-target programming language (PL) pair, owing to the complex engineering effort required to adapt new PL pairs. Programming agents can enable PL-agnosticism in repository-level code translation and validation: they can synthesize code across many PLs and autonomously use existing tools specific to each PL's analysis. However, state-of-the-art has yet to offer a fully autonomous agentic approach for repository-level code translation and validation of large-scale programs. This paper proposes ReCodeAgent, an autonomous multi-agent approach for language-agnostic repository-level code translation and validation. Users only need to provide the project in the source PL and specify the target PL for ReCodeAgent to automatically translate and validate the entire repository. ReCodeAgent is the first technique to achieve high translation success rates across many PLs.
  We compare the effectiveness of ReCodeAgent with four alternative neuro-symbolic and agentic approaches to translate 118 real-world projects, with 1,975 LoC and 43 translation units for each project, on average. The projects cover 6 PLs (C, Go, Java, JavaScript, Python, and Rust) and 4 PL pairs (C-Rust, Go-Rust, Java-Python, Python-JavaScript). Our results demonstrate that ReCodeAgent consistently outperforms prior techniques on translation correctness, improving test pass rate by 60.8% on ground-truth tests, with an average cost of $15.3. We also perform process-centric analysis of ReCodeAgent trajectories to confirm its procedural efficiency. Finally, we investigate how the design choices (a multi-agent vs. single-agent architecture) influence ReCodeAgent performance: on average, the test pass rate drops by 40.4%, and trajectories become 28% longer and persistently inefficient.

</details>
