### [35] [When Switching Algorithms Helps: A Theoretical Study of Online Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07473)
*Denis Antipov,Carola Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 首次证明在线算法选择能在OneMax问题上实现渐进加速：通过切换(1+λ) EA和(1+(λ,λ)) GA算法，达到O(n log log n)期望时间，优于任一单独算法的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在线算法选择（OAS）在实证研究中表现出色，但缺乏理论证明其能实现渐进加速。现有理论指导不足，需要建立理论框架来验证算法切换的实际优势。

Method: 提出在OneMax问题上切换(1+λ) EA和(1+(λ,λ)) GA的策略。首先分析理想切换规则（在最优时间点切换），然后设计实际可行的切换策略。结合固定起点和固定目标视角分析不同算法在不同优化阶段的优势。

Result: 证明通过适当选择两种算法的种群规模，切换策略能在O(n log log n)期望时间内找到最优解，优于单独使用任一算法的最优性能Θ(n√(log n log log log n / log log n))。实际切换策略能达到与理想切换相同的性能。

Conclusion: 首次为在线算法选择提供了理论证明，展示了算法切换能实现渐进加速。该方法结合不同分析视角，为深入理解OAS提供了有前景的理论路径。

Abstract: Online algorithm selection (OAS) aims to adapt the optimization process to changes in the fitness landscape and is expected to outperform any single algorithm from a given portfolio. Although this expectation is supported by numerous empirical studies, there are currently no theoretical results proving that OAS can yield asymptotic speedups (apart from some artificial examples for hyper-heuristics). Moreover, theory-based guidelines for when and how to switch between algorithms are largely missing.
  In this paper, we present the first theoretical example in which switching between two algorithms -- the $(1+λ)$ EA and the $(1+(λ,λ))$ GA -- solves the OneMax problem asymptotically faster than either algorithm used in isolation. We show that an appropriate choice of population sizes for the two algorithms allows the optimum to be reached in $O(n\log\log n)$ expected time, faster than the $Θ(n\sqrt{\frac{\log n \log\log\log n}{\log\log n}})$ runtime of the best of these two algorithms with optimally tuned parameters.
  We first establish this bound under an idealized switching rule that changes from the $(1+λ)$ to the $(1+(λ,λ))$ GA at the optimal time. We then propose a realistic switching strategy that achieves the same performance. Our analysis combines fixed-start and fixed-target perspectives, illustrating how different algorithms dominate at different stages of the optimization process. This approach offers a promising path toward a deeper theoretical understanding of OAS.

</details>


### [36] [Analysis of Search Heuristics in the Multi-Armed Bandit Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08109)
*Jasmin Brandt,Barbara Hammer,Timo Kötzing,Jurek Sander*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文分析了进化算法和分布估计算法在识别Dueling Bandits中Condorcet winner的能力差异，发现(1+1) EA表现较差，而基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA表现更好，并提出通过重复对决来改进(1+1) EA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解不同搜索启发式算法（特别是进化算法）在探索/利用权衡中的表现，特别是在Dueling Bandits设置下识别Condorcet winner的能力。由于许多搜索启发式通过比较不同选项工作，这与Dueling Bandits设置相似，因此可以分析进化算法在此类问题上的性能。

Method: 采用Dueling Bandits设置，每个臂有固定的获胜概率。分析(1+1) EA和基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA（分布估计算法）在识别Condorcet winner方面的性能。通过理论分析比较两种算法在平稳分布中选择Condorcet winner的概率。对于(1+1) EA，提出使用重复对决来提升性能。

Result: 结果显示：(1+1) EA在识别Condorcet winner方面表现较差，即使Condorcet winner以1-p的概率击败其他臂，当p=Ω(1/n)时，(1+1) EA在平稳分布中仅以常数概率选择Condorcet winner。相比之下，基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA在维持的分布中以1-Θ(p)的概率选择Condorcet winner。通过重复对决可以显著提升(1+1) EA在平稳分布中选择Condorcet winner的概率。

Conclusion: 结论是进化算法（特别是(1+1) EA）在识别Dueling Bandits中的Condorcet winner方面表现不佳，而分布估计算法（如基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA）表现更好。对于(1+1) EA，可以通过重复对决来改进其性能。这为理解进化算法在探索/利用权衡中的局限性提供了理论见解，并为改进此类算法提供了方向。

Abstract: We consider the classic Multi-Armed Bandit setting to understand the exploration/exploitation tradeoffs made by different search heuristics. Since many search heuristics work by comparing different options (in evolutionary algorithms called "individuals"; in the Bandit literature called "arms"), we work with the "Dueling Bandits" setting. In each iteration, a comparison between different arms can be made; in the binary stochastic setting, each arm has a fixed winning probability against any other arm. A Condorcet winner is any arm that beats every other arm with a probability strictly higher than $1/2$.
  We show that evolutionary algorithms are rather bad at identifying the Condorcet winner: Even if the Condorcet winner beats every other arm with a probability $1-p$, the (1+1) EA, in its stationary distribution, chooses the Condorcet winner only with constant probability if $p=Ω(1/n)$. By contrast, we show that a simple EDA (based on the Max-Min Ant System with iteration-best update) will choose the Condorcet winner in its maintained distribution with probability $1-Θ(p)$. As a remedy for the (1+1) EA, we show how repeated duels can significantly boost the probability of the Condorcet winner in the stationary distribution.

</details>


### [37] [Exploration of Pareto-preserving Search Space Transformations in Multi-objective Test Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08173)
*Diedeerick Vermetten,Jeroen Rook*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文强调在多目标优化基准问题中引入搜索空间变换的重要性，通过参数化双射变换创建流行基准问题的不同实例，并分析这些变换对各种多目标优化算法性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化问题的设计主要关注目标空间结构，而搜索空间结构相对被忽视。单目标优化中已广泛采用搜索空间变换来防止算法利用意外结构，但在多目标优化中，如何在保持目标空间结构不变的前提下为边界约束问题添加变换仍面临挑战。

Method: 使用两种参数化的双射变换来创建流行多目标基准问题的不同实例。这些变换旨在改变搜索空间结构而不影响目标空间结构。同时，还将参数化变换应用于目标空间，并比较两种变换对算法性能的影响。

Result: 研究表明搜索空间变换显著影响多目标优化算法的性能。通过创建不同的基准问题实例，揭示了算法可能利用的意外结构，并展示了参数化变换在目标空间中的应用效果及其性能影响。

Conclusion: 搜索空间变换对于创建无偏的多目标优化基准问题至关重要。参数化双射变换能够在不改变目标空间结构的前提下改变搜索空间结构，有助于更全面地评估算法性能，防止算法利用基准设计中的意外偏差。

Abstract: Benchmark problems are an important tool for gaining understanding of optimization algorithms. Since algorithms often aim to perform well on benchmarks, biases in benchmark design provide misleading insights. In single-objective optimization, for example, many problems used to have their optimum in the center of the search domain. To remedy these issues, search space transformations have been widely adopted by benchmark suites, preventing algorithms from exploiting unintended structure.
  In multi-objective optimization, problem design has focused primarily on the objective space structure. While this focus addresses important aspects of the multi-objective nature of the problems, the search space structures of these problems have received comparatively limited attention. In this work, we re-emphasize the importance of transformations in the search space, and address the challenges inherent in adding transformations to boundary constraints problems without impacting the structure of the objective space. We utilized two parameterized, bijective transformations to create different instantiations of popular benchmark problems, and show how these changes impact the performance of various multi-objective optimization algorithms. In addition to the search space transformations, we show that such parameterized transformations can also be applied to the objective space, and compare their respective performance impacts.

</details>


### [38] [Robust Multi-Objective Optimization for Bicycle Rebalancing in Shared Mobility Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08296)
*Diego Daniel Pedroza-Perez,Gabriel Luque,Sergio Nesmachnow,Jamal Toutouh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 研究基于站点自行车共享系统的静态夜间再平衡问题，考虑需求不确定性，构建三目标优化模型，使用NSGA-II算法求解，在巴塞罗那Bicing系统上验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于站点的自行车共享系统存在自行车供应与用户需求之间的空间不平衡问题，通常通过夜间卡车再平衡来解决。本研究关注在需求不确定性下的静态夜间再平衡问题，旨在同时优化多个目标

Method: 将问题建模为三目标优化问题：最小化总行驶距离、期望未满足需求、以及在高需求场景下的鲁棒性未满足需求度量。使用NSGA-II算法近似求解帕累托前沿，采用排列-分区编码和领域特定的重定位算子，包括用于站点重定位的偏置最佳改进移动算子

Result: 在巴塞罗那Bicing系统（460个站点）上的实验显示，算法能生成分布良好的帕累托解集，对参考非支配集有显著贡献。贪婪构造基线主要产生极端解且通常被支配

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理需求不确定性下的自行车共享系统再平衡问题，提供多目标权衡解，鲁棒性目标有助于选择减少高峰需求服务退化的方案

Abstract: Dock-based bike-sharing systems exhibit spatial imbalances between bicycle supply and user demand, often addressed through overnight truck-based rebalancing. This work studies static overnight rebalancing under demand uncertainty modeled as a tri-objective optimization problem. The objectives minimize total travel distance, expected unmet demand, and a robustness-oriented unmet demand measure over high-demand scenarios.
  Route plans are evaluated via a recourse simulation that enforces truck loads and station capacity constraints across multiple demand realizations. The robustness objective supports selecting plans that reduce peak-demand service degradation. Trade-off solutions are approximated with Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II using a permutation--partition encoding and domain-specific relocation operators, including a biased best-improvement move for station relocation.
  Experiments on the real Barcelona Bicing system with 460 stations show well-distributed Pareto sets and substantial contributions to the reference non-dominated set. Greedy constructive baselines mainly yield extreme solutions and are often dominated.

</details>


### [39] [Multi-Modal Learning meets Genetic Programming: Analyzing Alignment in Latent Space Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08324)
*Benjamin Léger,Kazem Meidani,Christian Gagné*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: SNIP模型通过多模态对齐实现符号回归的潜在空间优化，但研究发现其跨模态对齐过于粗糙，无法有效指导符号空间搜索，揭示了细粒度对齐是实现有效优化的重要方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归传统上使用遗传编程进行组合搜索，潜在空间优化方法通过神经编码器将符号表达式映射到连续空间。SNIP模型引入多模态方法，在共享潜在空间中对齐符号和数值编码器，旨在通过数值空间优化隐式指导符号搜索。然而，类似CLIP的模型通常只实现粗粒度对齐，本文旨在验证SNIP是否真正实现了有效的双模态优化。

Method: 通过实验分析SNIP模型在符号回归任务中的表现，重点考察两个关键方面：1）优化过程中跨模态对齐是否改善；2）SNIP学习的对齐是否足够精细以支持符号空间的原则性搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）即使适应度提高，跨模态对齐在优化过程中并未改善；2）SNIP学习的对齐过于粗糙，无法有效指导符号空间的原则性搜索。这意味着虽然多模态潜在空间优化在符号回归中具有潜力，但实际中尚未实现有效的对齐引导优化。

Conclusion: SNIP模型虽然引入了有前景的多模态潜在空间优化方法，但其实现的跨模态对齐过于粗糙，无法有效指导符号回归搜索。研究揭示了细粒度对齐是实现有效对齐引导优化的关键方向，为未来符号回归的多模态方法发展提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) aims to discover mathematical expressions from data, a task traditionally tackled using Genetic Programming (GP) through combinatorial search over symbolic structures. Latent Space Optimization (LSO) methods use neural encoders to map symbolic expressions into continuous spaces, transforming the combinatorial search into continuous optimization. SNIP (Meidani et al., 2024), a contrastive pre-training model inspired by CLIP, advances LSO by introducing a multi-modal approach: aligning symbolic and numeric encoders in a shared latent space to learn the phenotype-genotype mapping, enabling optimization in the numeric space to implicitly guide symbolic search. However, this relies on fine-grained cross-modal alignment, whereas literature on similar models like CLIP reveals that such an alignment is typically coarse-grained. In this paper, we investigate whether SNIP delivers on its promise of effective bi-modal optimization for SR. Our experiments show that: (1) cross-modal alignment does not improve during optimization, even as fitness increases, and (2) the alignment learned by SNIP is too coarse to efficiently conduct principled search in the symbolic space. These findings reveal that while multi-modal LSO holds significant potential for SR, effective alignment-guided optimization remains unrealized in practice, highlighting fine-grained alignment as a critical direction for future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [40] [Label Leakage Attacks in Machine Unlearning: A Parameter and Inversion-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07386)
*Weidong Zheng,Kongyang Chen,Yao Huang,Yuanwei Guo,Yatie Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对类别遗忘场景，提出了四种从模型参数和模型反演角度攻击的方法，能够有效推断目标模型遗忘的类别，揭示了现有遗忘方法可能泄露被遗忘数据类别的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术在面部识别等领域的广泛应用，数据隐私安全问题受到广泛关注，特别是众多隐私保护法律强调的"被遗忘权"。现有技术提出了各种遗忘方法，但这些方法可能无意中泄露被遗忘数据的类别信息。本文聚焦于类别遗忘场景，分析多个场景中被遗忘数据类别泄露的潜在问题。

Method: 提出了四种攻击方法：1）模型参数层面：通过计算目标模型参数与在保留数据子集和不相关数据上训练的辅助模型参数之间的点积或向量差，构建判别特征，然后通过k-means聚类、Youden指数和决策树算法实现遗忘类别的准确识别；2）模型反演领域：设计了基于梯度优化的白盒攻击和基于遗传算法的黑盒攻击来重构类别原型样本，然后使用阈值准则和信息熵准则分析这些合成样本的预测分布来推断遗忘类别。

Result: 在四个标准数据集上对五种最先进的遗忘算法进行了评估。实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够有效推断目标模型遗忘的类别，并对每种方法的优势和局限性进行了详细分析。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了现有遗忘方法在类别遗忘场景下可能泄露被遗忘数据类别的隐私风险，提出的攻击方法能够有效识别遗忘类别，为设计更安全的遗忘算法提供了重要参考。

Abstract: With the widespread application of artificial intelligence technologies in face recognition and other fields, data privacy security issues have received extensive attention, especially the \textit{right to be forgotten} emphasized by numerous privacy protection laws. Existing technologies have proposed various unlearning methods, but they may inadvertently leak the categories of unlearned data. This paper focuses on the category unlearning scenario, analyzes the potential problems of category leakage of unlearned data in multiple scenarios, and proposes four attack methods from the perspectives of model parameters and model inversion based on attackers with different knowledge backgrounds. At the level of model parameters, we construct discriminative features by computing either dot products or vector differences between the parameters of the target model and those of auxiliary models trained on subsets of retained data and unrelated data, respectively. These features are then processed via k-means clustering, Youden's Index, and decision tree algorithms to achieve accurate identification of the forgotten class. In the model inversion domain, we design a gradient optimization-based white-box attack and a genetic algorithm-based black-box attack to reconstruct class-prototypical samples. The prediction profiles of these synthesized samples are subsequently analyzed using a threshold criterion and an information entropy criterion to infer the forgotten class. We evaluate the proposed attacks on four standard datasets against five state-of-the-art unlearning algorithms, providing a detailed analysis of the strengths and limitations of each method. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach can effectively infer the classes forgotten by the target model.

</details>


### [41] [Interpreting the Error of Differentially Private Median Queries through Randomization Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07581)
*Thomas Humphries,Tim Li,Shufan Zhang,Karl Knopf,Xi He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PostRI方法，在差分隐私中位数估计后计算随机化区间，相比现有方法提升14%-850%的效用，同时保持较窄的置信区间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私统计中，添加的噪声使得从业者难以解释统计质量。现有方法（如随机化区间RI）需要为获得高质量RI而降低中位数本身的质量，这限制了实际应用效果

Method: 提出PostRI方法，先估计中位数，再计算随机化区间。该方法不依赖输入数据的噪声分布，能够在中位数估计完成后独立计算置信区间

Result: PostRI方法相比相关工作，中位数估计效用提升14%-850%，同时能够保持较窄的随机化区间，提高了差分隐私统计的可解释性和实用性

Conclusion: PostRI方法解决了差分隐私中位数估计中质量与置信区间权衡的问题，通过后处理计算随机化区间，显著提升了统计效用，增强了差分隐私统计的可解释性

Abstract: It can be difficult for practitioners to interpret the quality of differentially private (DP) statistics due to the added noise. One method to help analysts understand the amount of error introduced by DP is to return a Randomization Interval (RI), along with the statistic. A RI is a type of confidence interval that bounds the error introduced by DP. For queries where the noise distribution depends on the input, such as the median, prior work degrades the quality of the median itself to obtain a high-quality RI. In this work, we propose PostRI, a solution to compute a RI after the median has been estimated. PostRI enables a median estimation with 14%-850% higher utility than related work, while maintaining a narrow RI.

</details>


### [42] [Private Seeds, Public LLMs: Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07486)
*Qian Ma,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RPSG是一种结合差分隐私和私有种子生成逼真合成文本数据的方法，在隐私保护和数据保真度之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在合成数据生成方面表现出强大能力，特别是在生成私有文本的合成副本方面。然而，这需要在隐私保护和数据实用性之间进行仔细权衡，现有方法难以同时满足高保真度和强隐私保护的需求。

Method: 提出RPSG方法，结合隐私保护机制（包括形式化差分隐私）和私有种子（特别是包含个人信息的文本），生成逼真的合成数据。该方法利用大语言模型的能力，在隐私约束下生成高质量合成文本。

Result: 与最先进的私有合成数据生成方法相比，RPSG在保持对私有数据高保真度的同时，提供了强大的隐私保护。综合实验证明了该方法在隐私-效用权衡方面的优越性能。

Conclusion: RPSG为生成逼真且隐私保护的合成文本数据提供了一种有效解决方案，在隐私保护和数据实用性之间实现了良好平衡，为私有文本数据的合成生成开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for synthetic data generation. A particularly important use case is producing synthetic replicas of private text, which requires carefully balancing privacy and utility. We propose Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation (RPSG), which leverages privacy-preserving mechanisms, including formal differential privacy (DP); and private seeds, in particular text containing personal information, to generate realistic synthetic data. Comprehensive experiments against state-of-the-art private synthetic data generation methods demonstrate that RPSG achieves high fidelity to private data while providing strong privacy protection.

</details>


### [43] [TRUSTDESC: Preventing Tool Poisoning in LLM Applications via Trusted Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07536)
*Hengkai Ye,Zhechang Zhang,Jinyuan Jia,Hong Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRUSTDESC是一个防止工具中毒攻击的框架，通过从工具实现自动生成可信的工具描述，解决现有防御对隐式TPA无效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）依赖外部工具执行任务，但工具集成引入了新的提示注入攻击面：工具中毒攻击（TPAs）。攻击者通过嵌入恶意指令（显式TPA）或误导性声明（隐式TPA）来操纵工具描述，影响模型行为和工具选择。现有防御主要检测异常指令，对隐式TPA无效。

Method: TRUSTDESC采用三阶段流水线：1) SliceMin：通过可达性感知的静态分析和LLM引导的去冗余，提取最小工具相关代码片段；2) DescGen：从这些片段合成描述，同时减轻误导或对抗性代码伪影；3) DynVer：通过执行合成任务和验证行为声明，进行动态验证以精炼描述。

Result: 在52个跨多个工具生态系统的真实世界工具上评估TRUSTDESC，结果显示它能够生成准确工具描述，提高任务完成率，同时从根源上缓解隐式TPA，且时间和货币开销最小。

Conclusion: TRUSTDESC是首个通过从实现自动生成可信工具描述来防止工具中毒的框架，有效解决了现有防御对隐式TPA无效的问题，为LLM工具集成提供了安全保障。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to perform time-sensitive tasks and real-world actions. While tool integration expands LLM capabilities, it also introduces a new prompt-injection attack surface: tool poisoning attacks (TPAs). Attackers manipulate tool descriptions by embedding malicious instructions (explicit TPAs) or misleading claims (implicit TPAs) to influence model behavior and tool selection. Existing defenses mainly detect anomalous instructions and remain ineffective against implicit TPAs. In this paper, we present TRUSTDESC, the first framework for preventing tool poisoning by automatically generating trusted tool descriptions from implementations. TRUSTDESC derives implementation-faithful descriptions through a three-stage pipeline. SliceMin performs reachability-aware static analysis and LLM-guided debloating to extract minimal tool-relevant code slices. DescGen synthesizes descriptions from these slices while mitigating misleading or adversarial code artifacts. DynVer refines descriptions through dynamic verification by executing synthesized tasks and validating behavioral claims. We evaluate TRUSTDESC on 52 real-world tools across multiple tool ecosystems. Results show that TRUSTDESC produces accurate tool descriptions that improve task completion rates while mitigating implicit TPAs at their root, with minimal time and monetary overhead.

</details>


### [44] [MCP-DPT: A Defense-Placement Taxonomy and Coverage Analysis for Model Context Protocol Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07551)
*Mehrdad Rostamzadeh,Sidhant Narula,Nahom Birhan,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCP（模型上下文协议）的防御导向安全分析框架，通过分层架构将攻击分类并识别主要和次要防御点，揭示了当前防御措施主要集中在工具层而存在架构性安全缺陷的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP使LLM能够动态发现和调用第三方工具，显著扩展了智能体能力，但引入了独特的安全挑战。现有研究多为攻击导向或基准测试驱动，缺乏对MCP多参与方设计和分布式信任边界下防御责任分配的指导。

Method: 提出了防御导向的MCP安全分析方法，引入分层对齐的分类法，将攻击按负责执行的架构组件组织。威胁被映射到六个MCP层，识别主要和次要防御点，支持在对手控制工具、服务器或生态系统组件情况下的深度防御推理。

Result: 将现有学术和行业防御措施映射到该框架，揭示了保护措施不均衡且主要集中于工具层，在主机编排、传输和供应链层存在持续的安全缺口。这些发现表明许多MCP安全弱点源于架构错位而非孤立的实现缺陷。

Conclusion: MCP的安全问题主要是架构性的，需要系统性的防御责任分配和深度防御策略。当前防御集中在工具层，需要在主机编排、传输和供应链层加强保护，以应对多参与方环境中的安全挑战。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables large language models (LLMs) to dynamically discover and invoke third-party tools, significantly expanding agent capabilities while introducing a distinct security landscape. Unlike prompt-only interactions, MCP exposes pre-execution artifacts, shared context, multi-turn workflows, and third-party supply chains to adversarial influence across independently operated components. While recent work has identified MCP-specific attacks and evaluated defenses, existing studies are largely attack-centric or benchmark-driven, providing limited guidance on where mitigation responsibility should reside within the MCP architecture. This is problematic given MCP's multi-party design and distributed trust boundaries. We present a defense-placement-oriented security analysis of MCP, introducing a layer-aligned taxonomy that organizes attacks by the architectural component responsible for enforcement. Threats are mapped across six MCP layers, and primary and secondary defense points are identified to support principled defense-in-depth reasoning under adversaries controlling tools, servers, or ecosystem components. A structured mapping of existing academic and industry defenses onto this framework reveals uneven and predominantly tool-centric protection, with persistent gaps at the host orchestration, transport, and supply-chain layers. These findings suggest that many MCP security weaknesses stem from architectural misalignment rather than isolated implementation flaws.

</details>


### [45] [MEV-ACE: Identity-Authenticated Fair Ordering for Proposer-Controlled MEV Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07568)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MEV ACE是一种公平排序协议，通过注册经济身份、认证提交/打开消息和可验证延迟随机性，消除区块生产者对交易排序的单方面控制，防止前置交易、三明治攻击和审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取价值）对区块链公平性构成结构性威胁，因为区块生产者可以观察待处理交易并单方面决定其排序或包含。现有缓解措施存在两个未解决的缺陷：承诺未通过可惩罚身份进行认证，包含义务缺乏其他验证者可验证的可转移证据。

Method: MEV ACE结合三种机制：1) 使用注册经济身份，其认证密钥从ACE GF框架确定性派生并在链上质押；2) 使用带验证者接收阈值的认证提交和打开消息，使可接受性和包含义务可独立审计；3) 使用基于可验证延迟的随机性，仅在可接受承诺集固定后确定交易顺序。

Result: 在拜占庭容错验证者模型中形式化协议，在标准假设下证明三个属性：可接受集锁定后的顺序不可预测性、签名不可伪造性下的承诺真实性，以及获得阈值提交和打开收据的交易的可问责包含性。当生产者和用户质押超过单时隙无效执行或选择性不打开的收益时，MEV ACE消除了对前置交易、三明治攻击和已接受交易审查的单方面提议者裁量权。

Conclusion: MEV ACE协议在结构上保持单时隙，无需阈值解密委员会，兼容后量子签名方案如ML-DSA 44，有效解决了MEV带来的公平性问题。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value, or MEV, remains a structural threat to blockchain fairness because a block producer can often observe pending transactions and unilaterally decide their ordering or inclusion. Existing mitigations hide transaction contents or outsource ordering, but they often leave two gaps unresolved. First, commitments are not authenticated by slashable identities. Second, inclusion obligations are not backed by transferable evidence that other validators can verify. This paper presents MEV ACE, a fair ordering protocol for proposer controlled ordering MEV. MEV ACE combines three mechanisms. First, it uses registered economic identities whose authentication keys are deterministically derived from the ACE GF framework and bonded on chain. Second, it uses authenticated commit and open messages with validator receipt thresholds, which make admissibility and inclusion obligations independently auditable. Third, it uses verifiable delay based randomness to determine transaction order only after the admissible commitment set is fixed. We formalize the protocol in a Byzantine fault tolerant validator model with threshold receipts and show three properties under standard assumptions: order unpredictability after the admissible set is locked, commitment authenticity under signature unforgeability, and accountable inclusion for transactions that obtain threshold commit and open receipts. Under these conditions, and when producer and user bonds exceed the one slot gain from invalid execution or selective non opening, MEV ACE removes unilateral proposer discretion over front running, sandwich attacks, and censorship against admitted transactions. The protocol remains single slot in structure, requires no threshold decryption committee, and is compatible with post quantum signature schemes such as ML DSA 44.

</details>


### [46] [TrajGuard: Streaming Hidden-state Trajectory Detection for Decoding-time Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07727)
*Cheng Liu,Xiaolei Liu,Xingyu Li,Bangzhou Xin,Kangyi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrajGuard：基于解码过程中隐藏状态轨迹的实时越狱防御框架，无需训练即可实现95%的平均防御率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱防御方法主要依赖静态检测，忽略了解码过程中风险的动态演变，导致解码轨迹中的风险信号未被充分利用，构成当前防御系统的关键盲点

Method: 提出TrajGuard框架，通过滑动窗口聚合解码过程中的隐藏状态轨迹来实时量化风险，仅当局部窗口内风险持续超过阈值时触发轻量级语义裁决，实现即时中断或约束后续解码

Result: 在12种越狱攻击和多种开源LLM上的实验表明，TrajGuard平均防御率达到95%，检测延迟降至5.2毫秒/令牌，误报率低于1.5%

Conclusion: 解码过程中的隐藏状态轨迹能有效支持实时越狱检测，为无需模型修改的防御提供了有前景的方向

Abstract: Existing jailbreak defense paradigms primarily rely on static detection of prompts, outputs, or internal states, often neglecting the dynamic evolution of risk during decoding. This oversight leaves risk signals embedded in decoding trajectories underutilized, constituting a critical blind spot in current defense systems. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that hidden states in critical layers during the decoding phase carry stronger and more stable risk signals than input jailbreak prompts. Specifically, the hidden representations of tokens generated during jailbreak attempts progressively approach high-risk regions in the latent space. Based on this observation, we propose TrajGuard, a training-free, decoding-time defense framework. TrajGuard aggregates hidden-state trajectories via a sliding window to quantify risk in real time, triggering a lightweight semantic adjudication only when risk within a local window persistently exceeds a threshold. This mechanism enables the immediate interruption or constraint of subsequent decoding. Extensive experiments across 12 jailbreak attacks and various open-source LLMs show that TrajGuard achieves an average defense rate of 95%. Furthermore, it reduces detection latency to 5.2 ms/token while maintaining a false positive rate below 1.5%. These results confirm that hidden-state trajectories during decoding can effectively support real-time jailbreak detection, highlighting a promising direction for defenses without model modification.

</details>


### [47] [The Art of (Mis)alignment: How Fine-Tuning Methods Effectively Misalign and Realign LLMs in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07754)
*Rui Zhang,Hongwei Li,Yun Shen,Xinyue Shen,Wenbo Jiang,Guowen Xu,Yang Liu,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）中的错位攻击与再对齐防御机制，发现攻击与防御之间存在机制不对称性，ORPO在错位攻击中最有效，而DPO在再对齐防御中表现最佳但会牺牲模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的部署引发重大伦理和安全担忧。虽然采用对齐技术来提高模型安全性和可信度，但攻击者可能利用这些技术破坏安全性以实现恶意目的，导致"错位"。错位的LLMs可能在开放平台上发布以放大危害，因此在部署不受信任的第三方LLMs之前需要进行额外的安全对齐（再对齐）。

Method: 通过评估四种监督微调（SFT）和两种偏好微调（PFT）方法，在四个流行的安全对齐LLMs上进行实验。研究探讨了错位、再对齐及其相互作用的效应，重点关注攻击与防御机制的不对称性。

Result: 发现攻击与防御之间存在机制不对称性：ORPO在错位攻击中最有效，而DPO在再对齐防御中表现最佳但会牺牲模型效用。还识别出模型特定的抵抗性、多轮对抗动态的残留效应等值得注意的发现。

Conclusion: 这些发现强调了需要强大的安全防护措施和定制化的安全对齐策略，以减轻LLMs部署中的潜在风险。攻击与防御机制的不对称性表明需要针对性的安全策略。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) raises significant ethical and safety concerns. While LLM alignment techniques are adopted to improve model safety and trustworthiness, adversaries can exploit these techniques to undermine safety for malicious purposes, resulting in \emph{misalignment}. Misaligned LLMs may be published on open platforms to magnify harm. To address this, additional safety alignment, referred to as \emph{realignment}, is necessary before deploying untrusted third-party LLMs. This study explores the efficacy of fine-tuning methods in terms of misalignment, realignment, and the effects of their interplay. By evaluating four Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and two Preference Fine-Tuning (PFT) methods across four popular safety-aligned LLMs, we reveal a mechanism asymmetry between attack and defense. While Odds Ratio Preference Optimization (ORPO) is most effective for misalignment, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) excels in realignment, albeit at the expense of model utility. Additionally, we identify model-specific resistance, residual effects of multi-round adversarial dynamics, and other noteworthy findings. These findings highlight the need for robust safeguards and customized safety alignment strategies to mitigate potential risks in the deployment of LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangrui4041/The-Art-of-Mis-alignment.

</details>


### [48] [Anamorphic Encryption with CCA Security: A Standard Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07771)
*Shujun Wang,Jianting Ning,Qinyi Li,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用的CCA安全变形加密方案，通过定义变形密钥封装机制(AKEM)及其公钥和对称密钥变体，在标准模型中实现了强IND-CCA安全，解决了现有研究依赖CPA安全的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变形加密研究主要依赖CPA安全性，缺乏在标准模型中构建通用CCA安全变形方案的解决方案。特别是在接收者变形设置中，当用户被迫交出密钥时，需要保护隐藏消息的安全性，但现有方案无法抵抗CCA攻击。

Method: 形式化定义了变形密钥封装机制(AKEM)，包括公钥变形密钥封装机制(PKAKEM)和对称密钥变形密钥封装机制(SKAKEM)。提出通用构造方法，可利用任何支持随机数恢复的KEM进行实例化。通过KEM-DEM范式集成变形特性，基于基础KEM的单射属性确保密文与随机数之间的唯一映射。

Result: 在标准模型中实现了强IND-CCA(sIND-CCA)安全性，能够抵抗控制解封装密钥的"独裁者"攻击。通过形式化证明验证了方案的安全性，显著增强了隐蔽通道在现代密码基础设施中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了CCA安全变形加密方案的空白，提供了一种通用构造框架，将变形特性集成到KEM-DEM范式中，为隐蔽通信提供了更强的安全保障，特别是在后妥协场景下的密钥泄露风险中。

Abstract: Anamorphic encryption serves as a vital tool for covert communication, maintaining secrecy even during post-compromise scenarios. Particularly in the receiver-anamorphic setting, a user can shield hidden messages even when coerced into surrendering their secret keys. However, a major bottleneck in existing research is the reliance on CPA-security, leaving the construction of a generic, CCA-secure anamorphic scheme in the standard model as a persistent open challenge. To bridge this gap, we formalize the Anamorphic Key Encapsulation Mechanism (AKEM), encompassing both Public-Key (PKAKEM) and Symmetric-Key (SKAKEM) variants. We propose generic constructions for these primitives, which can be instantiated using any KEM that facilitates randomness recovery. Notably, our framework achieves strong IND-CCA (sIND-CCA) security for the covert channel. We provide a rigorous formal proof in the standard model, demonstrating resilience against a "dictator" who controls the decapsulation key. The security of our approach is anchored in the injective property of the base KEM, which ensures a unique mapping between ciphertexts and randomness. By integrating anamorphism into the KEM-DEM paradigm, our work significantly enhances the practical utility of covert channels within modern cryptographic infrastructures.

</details>


### [49] [BRASP: Boolean Range Queries over Encrypted Spatial Data with Access and Search Pattern Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07797)
*Jing Zhang,Ganxuan Yang,Yifei Yang,Siqi Wen,Zhengyang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BRASP是一种支持加密空间数据布尔范围查询的可搜索加密方案，通过希尔伯特曲线编码和双服务器架构隐藏搜索模式和访问模式，同时支持动态更新和前向安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可搜索加密方案存在搜索模式和访问模式泄露问题，可能导致云服务器推断查询内容和用户兴趣。现有模式隐藏方案主要针对关键词查询，无法自然支持加密空间数据的布尔范围查询。

Method: BRASP结合希尔伯特曲线前缀编码与加密的前缀-ID和关键词-ID倒排索引，支持高效空间范围过滤和合取关键词匹配。采用双服务器设置，通过索引洗牌和ID字段重分布隐藏搜索和访问模式，支持动态更新并实现前向安全性。

Result: 实验评估表明BRASP能有效保护查询隐私，同时保持相对较低的计算和通信开销。方案通过机密性、洗牌不可区分性、查询不可伪造性和前向安全性分析形式化验证了安全性。

Conclusion: BRASP为加密空间数据的布尔范围查询提供了一种有效的隐私保护解决方案，在保护查询隐私的同时保持了良好的性能，源代码已公开以促进可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Searchable Encryption (SE) enables users to query outsourced encrypted data while preserving data confidentiality. However, most efficient schemes still leak the search pattern and access pattern, which may allow an honest-but-curious cloud server to infer query contents, user interests, or returned records from repeated searches and observed results. Existing pattern-hiding solutions mainly target keyword queries and do not naturally support Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. This paper presents BRASP, a searchable encryption scheme for Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. BRASP combines Hilbert-curve-based prefix encoding with encrypted prefix--ID and keyword--ID inverted indexes to support efficient spatial range filtering and conjunctive keyword matching. To hide the search pattern and access pattern under a dual-server setting, BRASP integrates index shuffling for encrypted keyword and prefix entries with ID-field redistribution across two non-colluding cloud servers. BRASP also supports dynamic updates and achieves forward security. We formalize the security of BRASP through confidentiality, shuffle indistinguishability, query unforgeability, and forward-security analyses, and we evaluate its performance experimentally on a real-world dataset. The results show that BRASP effectively protects query privacy while incurring relatively low computation and communication overhead. To facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of BRASP is publicly available at https://github.com/Egbert-Lannister/BRASP

</details>


### [50] [A Hardware-Anchored Privacy Middleware for PII Sharing Across Heterogeneous Embedded Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07839)
*Aditya Sabbineni,Pravin Nagare,Devendra Dahiphale,Preetam Dedu,Willison Lopes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: UDSS是一个平台无关的用户数据共享框架，通过上下文范围执行机制减少IoT设备上的PII过度暴露风险，降低用户注册延迟65%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和智能家居生态系统快速扩张导致用户数据管理碎片化，当前设备注册流程存在高摩擦（手动数据输入）和不透明的数据共享实践

Method: 提出用户数据共享系统（UDSS），包含上下文范围执行（CSE）机制，根据用户意图（登录与注册）编程式限制数据暴露；采用分层访问模型平衡开发者需求与法规合规；在ARMv8 Linux中间件上实现概念验证

Result: UDSS将用户注册延迟降低65%，通过协议强制执行的数据最小化显著减少PII过度暴露风险

Conclusion: UDSS为异构消费电子市场提供了标准化的身份管理方法，适用于共享、设备中心的环境，其中持久用户-设备绑定无法假设

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and smart home ecosystems has led to a fragmented landscape of user data management across consumer electronics (CE) such as Smart TVs, gaming consoles, and set-top boxes. Current onboarding processes on these devices are characterized by high friction due to manual data entry and opaque data-sharing practices. This paper introduces the User Data Sharing System (UDSS), a platform-agnostic framework designed to facilitate secure, privacy-first PII (Personally Identifiable Information) exchange between device platforms and third-party applications. Our system implements a Contextual Scope Enforcement (CSE) mechanism that programmatically restricts data exposure based on user intent - specifically distinguishing between Sign-In and Sign-Up workflows. Unlike cloud-anchored identity standards such as FIDO2/WebAuthn, UDSS is designed for shared, device-centric CE environments where persistent user-to-device binding cannot be assumed. We further propose a tiered access model that balances developer needs with regulatory compliance (GDPR/CCPA). A proof-of-concept implementation on a reference ARMv8 Linux-based middleware demonstrates that UDSS reduces user onboarding latency by 65% and measurably reduces PII over-exposure risk through protocol-enforced data minimization. This framework provides a standardized approach to identity management in the heterogeneous CE market.

</details>


### [51] [xDup: Privacy-Preserving Deduplication for Humanitarian Organizations using Fuzzy PSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08019)
*Tim Rausch,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: xDup是一个用于人道主义组织跨组织去重的隐私友好系统，比现有方案快两个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义组织在危机情况下需要确保有限的预算能帮助尽可能多的人，当前跨组织去重方法存在隐私风险，可能危害脆弱援助对象的隐私

Method: 基于Fuzzy PSI构建xDup系统，并提出otFPSI协议——一种针对汉明空间的无输入假设的具体高效Fuzzy PSI协议

Result: xDup满足人道主义组织的实际需求，比现有解决方案快两个数量级；otFPSI协议比现有Fuzzy PSI协议更高效

Conclusion: xDup提供了一个实用的隐私友好跨组织去重系统，适用于真实的人道主义任务，显著提高了效率

Abstract: Humanitarian organizations help to ensure people's livelihoods in crisis situations. Typically, multiple organizations operate in the same region. To ensure that the limited budget of these organizations can help as many people as possible, organizations perform cross-organizational deduplication to detect duplicate registrations and ensure recipients receive aid from at most one organization. Current deduplication approaches risk privacy harm to vulnerable aid recipients by sharing their data with other organizations. We analyzed the needs of humanitarian organizations to identify the requirements for privacy-friendly cross-organizational deduplication fit for real-life humanitarian missions. We present xDup, a new practical deduplication system that meets the requirements of humanitarian organizations and is two orders of magnitude faster than current solutions. xDup builds on Fuzzy PSI, and we present otFPSI, a concretely efficient Fuzzy PSI protocol for Hamming Space without input assumptions. We show that it is more efficient than existing Fuzzy PSI protocols.

</details>


### [52] [TADP-RME: A Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy Framework for Enhancing Reliability of Data-Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08113)
*Labani Halder,Payel Sadhukhan,Sarbani Palit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TADP-RME框架通过自适应隐私预算和流形嵌入技术，在对抗环境中提升数据驱动系统的可靠性，改善隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方案采用固定隐私预算，导致刚性的隐私-效用权衡，无法适应异构用户信任水平。同时，仅添加噪声的差分隐私保留了数据的几何结构，易被推理攻击利用导致隐私泄露。

Method: 提出TADP-RME框架：1) 引入[0,1]范围的逆信任分数来自适应调节隐私预算；2) 使用反向流形嵌入进行非线性变换，破坏局部几何关系，同时通过后处理保持形式化差分隐私保证。

Result: 理论分析和实证结果表明，该框架改善了隐私-效用权衡，将攻击成功率降低高达3.1%，且未显著降低效用。在对抗推理攻击时持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TADP-RME为对抗环境中的可靠学习提供了统一框架，通过信任自适应隐私预算和几何结构破坏，在保持差分隐私保证的同时增强了系统可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in adversarial settings necessitates treating privacy as a foundational component of data-driven systems. While differential privacy and cryptographic protocols offer strong guarantees, existing schemes rely on a fixed privacy budget, leading to a rigid utility-privacy trade-off that fails under heterogeneous user trust. Moreover, noise-only differential privacy preserves geometric structure, which inference attacks exploit, causing privacy leakage.
  We propose TADP-RME (Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy with Reverse Manifold Embedding), a framework that enhances reliability under varying levels of user trust. It introduces an inverse trust score in the range [0,1] to adaptively modulate the privacy budget, enabling smooth transitions between utility and privacy. Additionally, Reverse Manifold Embedding applies a nonlinear transformation to disrupt local geometric relationships while preserving formal differential privacy guarantees through post-processing.
  Theoretical and empirical results demonstrate improved privacy-utility trade-offs, reducing attack success rates by up to 3.1 percent without significant utility degradation. The framework consistently outperforms existing methods against inference attacks, providing a unified approach for reliable learning in adversarial environments.

</details>


### [53] [Towards Identification and Intervention of Safety-Critical Parameters in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08297)
*Weiwei Qi,Zefeng Wu,Tianhang Zheng,Zikang Zhang,Xiaojun Jia,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ESI框架量化LLM安全机制，发现不同架构的安全关键参数分布模式，并基于此开发SET和SPA两种针对性干预方法，分别用于增强不安全模型的安全性和保护已对齐模型在能力调优时的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对LLM安全机制的清晰理解，阻碍了开发精确可靠的安全干预方法。需要更好的理解和控制LLM安全性，以应对多样化任务中的安全挑战。

Method: 提出预期安全影响（ESI）框架来量化不同参数对LLM安全的影响。基于ESI分析发现：密集LLM的安全关键参数主要位于中间层的值矩阵（V）和MLP中，而MoE模型则转移到后层MLP。基于此开发两种干预范式：安全增强调优（SET）通过更新少量安全关键参数来对齐不安全LLM；安全保持适应（SPA）在能力导向干预时防止安全关键权重被破坏。

Result: SET仅更新1%模型权重、100次迭代即可将未对齐LLM的攻击成功率降低50%以上；SPA在1000次迭代指令微调后能将已对齐LLM的安全退化控制在1%以内。两种方法都能在保持原始性能的同时实现安全目标。

Conclusion: ESI框架为理解和控制LLM安全机制提供了量化工具，揭示了不同架构的安全关键参数分布规律。基于ESI的SET和SPA方法实现了高效、精准的安全干预，为LLM安全增强和保持提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring Large Language Model (LLM) safety is crucial, yet the lack of a clear understanding about safety mechanisms hinders the development of precise and reliable methodologies for safety intervention across diverse tasks. To better understand and control LLM safety, we propose the Expected Safety Impact (ESI) framework for quantifying how different parameters affect LLM safety. Based on ESI, we reveal distinct safety-critical patterns across different LLM architectures: In dense LLMs, many safety-critical parameters are located in value matrices (V) and MLPs in middle layers, whereas in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, they shift to the late-layer MLPs. Leveraging ESI, we further introduce two targeted intervention paradigms for safety enhancement and preservation, i.e., Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET) and Safety Preserving Adaptation (SPA). SET can align unsafe LLMs by updating only a few safety-critical parameters, effectively enhancing safety while preserving original performance. SPA safeguards well-aligned LLMs during capability-oriented intervention (e.g., instruction tuning) by preventing disruption of safety-critical weights, allowing the LLM to acquire new abilities and maintain safety capabilities. Extensive evaluations on different LLMs demonstrate that SET can reduce the attack success rates of unaligned LLMs by over 50% with only a 100-iteration update on 1% of model weights. SPA can limit the safety degradation of aligned LLMs within 1% after a 1,000-iteration instruction fine-tuning on different tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ZJU-LLM-Safety/SafeWeights-ACL.

</details>


### [54] [Securing Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Taxonomy of Attacks, Defenses, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08304)
*Yuming Xu,Mingtao Zhang,Zhuohan Ge,Haoyang Li,Nicole Hu,Jason Chen Zhang,Qing Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出RAG安全的核心在于外部知识访问管道的安全性，通过建立操作边界区分LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁，系统化分析了RAG工作流程中的安全表面、攻击防御机制及评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM固有风险与RAG引入的风险混为一谈，缺乏对RAG特有安全威胁的系统分析。论文旨在明确RAG安全的核心问题在于外部知识访问管道的安全性，为构建更安全的RAG系统提供理论框架。

Method: 建立操作边界区分LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁；将RAG工作流程抽象为六个阶段；围绕三个信任边界和四个主要安全表面组织文献分析，包括检索前知识污染、检索时访问操纵、下游上下文利用和知识泄露。

Result: 系统化梳理了RAG安全领域的攻击、防御、修复机制和评估基准，发现当前防御措施大多是被动和碎片化的，缺乏整体性保护策略。

Conclusion: RAG安全需要关注整个知识访问生命周期的分层、边界感知保护，未来研究方向应填补当前防御机制的空白，构建更系统化的安全框架。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) significantly enhances large language models (LLMs) but introduces novel security risks through external knowledge access. While existing studies cover various RAG vulnerabilities, they often conflate inherent LLM risks with those specifically introduced by RAG. In this paper, we propose that secure RAG is fundamentally about the security of the external knowledge-access pipeline. We establish an operational boundary to separate inherent LLM flaws from RAG-introduced or RAG-amplified threats. Guided by this perspective, we abstract the RAG workflow into six stages and organize the literature around three trust boundaries and four primary security surfaces, including pre-retrieval knowledge corruption, retrieval-time access manipulation, downstream context exploitation, and knowledge exfiltration. By systematically reviewing the corresponding attacks, defenses, remediation mechanisms, and evaluation benchmarks, we reveal that current defenses remain largely reactive and fragmented. Finally, we discuss these gaps and highlight future directions toward layered, boundary-aware protection across the entire knowledge-access lifecycle.

</details>


### [55] [PIArena: A Platform for Prompt Injection Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08499)
*Runpeng Geng,Chenlong Yin,Yanting Wang,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIArena是一个统一的提示注入评估平台，解决了现有评估方法分散、难以比较防御效果的问题，通过动态策略攻击揭示了现有防御方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入攻击对实际应用构成严重安全威胁，但社区缺乏统一的评估平台，导致难以可靠比较防御方法、理解其真实鲁棒性以及评估跨任务泛化能力。

Method: 提出PIArena统一可扩展平台，支持集成最先进的攻击和防御方法，并设计动态策略攻击，根据防御反馈自适应优化注入提示。

Result: 通过PIArena全面评估，揭示了最先进防御方法的三个关键局限性：跨任务泛化能力有限、对自适应攻击的脆弱性、以及当注入任务与目标任务一致时的根本性挑战。

Conclusion: PIArena填补了提示注入评估的空白，为系统评估防御方法提供了统一平台，揭示了现有防御方法的不足，并开源了代码和数据集供社区使用。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications. While receiving increasing attention, the community faces a critical gap: the lack of a unified platform for prompt injection evaluation. This makes it challenging to reliably compare defenses, understand their true robustness under diverse attacks, or assess how well they generalize across tasks and benchmarks. For instance, many defenses initially reported as effective were later found to exhibit limited robustness on diverse datasets and attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce PIArena, a unified and extensible platform for prompt injection evaluation that enables users to easily integrate state-of-the-art attacks and defenses and evaluate them across a variety of existing and new benchmarks. We also design a dynamic strategy-based attack that adaptively optimizes injected prompts based on defense feedback. Through comprehensive evaluation using PIArena, we uncover critical limitations of state-of-the-art defenses: limited generalizability across tasks, vulnerability to adaptive attacks, and fundamental challenges when an injected task aligns with the target task. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena.

</details>

### [56] [Learning Markov Processes as Sum-of-Square Forms for Analytical Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07525)
*Peter Amorese,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏平方和形式的函数建模框架，用于条件密度估计并保持解析信念传播能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多马尔可夫过程模型的信念传播在解析上不可行，需要近似或采样方法，限制了预测能力

Method: 使用稀疏平方和形式进行条件密度估计，提出新的函数形式克服限制，同时学习基函数和系数，保持解析信念传播，训练方法确保归一化和非负性约束

Result: 在低维空间达到与SOTA相当的精度且内存需求显著减少，可扩展到12D系统而现有方法在2D以上失效

Conclusion: 提出的函数建模框架在保持解析信念传播的同时，实现了高效的条件密度估计，在可扩展性和内存效率方面具有优势

Abstract: Harnessing the predictive capability of Markov process models requires propagating probability density functions (beliefs) through the model. For many existing models however, belief propagation is analytically infeasible, requiring approximation or sampling to generate predictions. This paper proposes a functional modeling framework leveraging sparse Sum-of-Squares (SoS) forms for valid (conditional) density estimation. We study the theoretical restrictions of modeling conditional densities using the SoS form, and propose a novel functional form for addressing such limitations. The proposed architecture enables generalized simultaneous learning of basis functions and coefficients, while preserving analytical belief propagation. In addition, we propose a training method that allows for exact adherence to the normalization and non-negativity constraints. Our results show that the proposed method achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art approaches while requiring significantly less memory in low-dimensional spaces, and it further scales to 12D systems when existing methods fail beyond 2D.

</details>


### [57] [LLM-Generated Fault Scenarios for Evaluating Perception-Driven Lane Following in Autonomous Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07362)
*Faezeh Pasandideh,Achim Rettberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架，使用LLM生成结构化故障场景和LDM合成传感器退化，通过预计算查找表实现边缘设备实时故障感知推理，显著暴露了自动驾驶视觉系统在真实环境中的鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备部署自动驾驶视觉系统面临资源限制，无法实时执行全面的安全测试。现有验证方法依赖静态数据集或手动故障注入，无法捕捉真实世界部署中遇到的各种环境危害。

Method: 引入解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架：离线阶段使用LLM语义生成结构化故障场景，LDM合成高保真传感器退化，将复杂故障动态蒸馏到预计算查找表中；在线阶段边缘设备通过查找表实现实时故障感知推理，无需本地运行重型AI模型。

Result: 在ResNet18车道跟随模型的460个故障场景中验证：模型在干净数据上R^2约0.85，但生成的故障暴露显著鲁棒性退化，RMSE增加高达99%，在雾条件下0.10米内定位精度降至31.0%，表明正常数据评估对真实边缘AI部署不足。

Conclusion: 提出的离线-在线故障注入框架有效暴露了边缘AI系统在真实环境中的脆弱性，为资源受限设备提供了实用的安全验证方法，强调了传统评估方法在边缘部署中的局限性。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous vision systems on edge devices faces a critical challenge: resource constraints prevent real-time and predictable execution of comprehensive safety tests. Existing validation methods depend on static datasets or manual fault injection, failing to capture the diverse environmental hazards encountered in real-world deployment. To address this, we introduce a decoupled offline-online fault injection framework. This architecture separates the validation process into two distinct phases: a computationally intensive Offline Phase and a lightweight Online Phase. In the offline phase, we employ Large Language Models (LLMs) to semantically generate structured fault scenarios and Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) to synthesize high-fidelity sensor degradations. These complex fault dynamics are distilled into a pre-computed lookup table, enabling the edge device to perform real-time fault-aware inference without running heavy AI models locally. We extensively validated this framework on a ResNet18 lane-following model across 460 fault scenarios. Results show that while the model achieves a baseline R^2 of approximately 0.85 on clean data, our generated faults expose significant robustness degradation, with RMSE increasing by up to 99% and within-0.10 localization accuracy dropping to as low as 31.0% under fog conditions, demonstrating the inadequacy of normal-data evaluation for real-world edge AI deployment.

</details>


### [58] [Benchmark Shadows: Data Alignment, Parameter Footprints, and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07363)
*Hongjian Zou,Yidan Wang,Qi Ding,Yixuan Liao,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现基准对齐数据提升特定评估指标但限制泛化能力，而覆盖扩展数据促进参数适应和更好泛化，基准性能不足以全面表征模型能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在基准测试中表现提升往往不伴随整体能力改善，作者假设这种差异源于数据分布引起的训练机制差异，需要研究数据分布如何影响模型学习动态

Method: 设计受控数据干预实验，在固定训练设置下隔离分布效应；引入基于谱分析和秩分析的参数空间诊断方法；在多样化开源模型家族（包括多模态模型）中进行验证；通过提示重复案例研究检验数据伪影影响

Result: 基准对齐数据改善狭窄评估指标但限制更广泛的表征发展；覆盖扩展数据导致更分布式的参数适应和更好泛化；参数空间诊断揭示不同训练机制的结构特征；类似模式在不同模型家族中普遍存在；并非所有数据伪影都会引起机制转变

Conclusion: 基准性能本身不足以全面表征模型能力，数据分布在塑造学习动态中起关键作用，需要超越基准测试来评估模型的实际泛化能力

Abstract: Large language models often achieve strong benchmark gains without corresponding improvements in broader capability. We hypothesize that this discrepancy arises from differences in training regimes induced by data distribution. To investigate this, we design controlled data interventions that isolate distributional effects under fixed training settings. We find that benchmark-aligned data improves narrow evaluation metrics while limiting broader representational development, whereas coverage-expanding data leads to more distributed parameter adaptation and better generalization. We further introduce parameter-space diagnostics based on spectral and rank analyses, which reveal distinct structural signatures of these regimes. Similar patterns are observed across diverse open-source model families, including multimodal models as a key case study, suggesting that these effects extend beyond controlled settings. A case study on prompt repetition shows that not all data artifacts induce regime shifts. These results indicate that benchmark performance alone is insufficient to characterize model capability, and highlight the importance of data distribution in shaping learning dynamics.

</details>


### [59] [The Role of Emotional Stimuli and Intensity in Shaping Large Language Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07369)
*Ameen Patel,Felix Lee,Kyle Liang,Joseph Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索了四种不同情感（喜悦、鼓励、愤怒、不安全感）在提示工程中对大语言模型性能的影响，发现积极情感刺激能提高准确性、降低毒性，但会增加谄媚行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感提示研究仅限于单一类型的积极情感刺激，且未考虑情感强度的变化。本研究旨在探索四种不同情感及其强度变化对LLM性能的影响。

Method: 使用GPT-4o mini构建提示生成管道，创建包含四种情感（喜悦、鼓励、愤怒、不安全感）不同强度的LLM和人类生成提示集。编译"黄金数据集"，其中人类和模型标签保持一致。在准确性、谄媚性和毒性三个维度评估LLM行为。

Result: 积极情感刺激（喜悦、鼓励）能产生更准确、毒性更低的结果，但会增加谄媚行为。不同情感强度对模型性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: 情感提示对LLM性能有显著影响，积极情感能改善准确性和安全性，但需注意可能增加的谄媚行为。情感强度是重要调节因素。

Abstract: Emotional prompting - the use of specific emotional diction in prompt engineering - has shown increasing promise in improving large language model (LLM) performance, truthfulness, and responsibility. However these studies have been limited to single types of positive emotional stimuli and have not considered varying degrees of emotion intensity in their analyses. In this paper, we explore the effects of four distinct emotions - joy, encouragement, anger, and insecurity - in emotional prompting and evaluate them on accuracy, sycophancy, and toxicity. We develop a prompt-generation pipeline with GPT-4o mini to create a suite of LLM and human-generated prompts with varying intensities across the four emotions. Then, we compile a "Gold Dataset" of prompts where human and model labels align. Our empirical evaluation on LLM behavior suggests that positive emotional stimuli lead to more accurate and less toxic results, but also increase sycophantic behavior.

</details>


### [60] [Latent Structure of Affective Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07382)
*Benjamin J. Choi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs学习的情感表征具有与心理学中效价-唤醒模型一致的几何结构，该结构非线性但可线性近似，支持线性表征假说，并可用于量化情感处理的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLMs潜在表征的几何结构对模型透明度和AI安全具有重要意义。现有研究主要关注学习表征的一般几何和拓扑特性，但由于缺乏真实潜在几何结构，验证这些方法的发现具有挑战性。情感处理提供了一个有趣的测试平台，因为情感既具有分类组织又具有连续的情感维度，这在心理学文献中已有充分研究。

Method: 使用几何数据分析工具研究LLMs中情感表征的潜在结构。通过将LLMs学习的情感表征与心理学中广泛使用的效价-唤醒模型进行比较，分析其几何特性。

Result: 1. LLMs学习的情感表征与心理学中的效价-唤醒模型一致；2. 这些表征呈现非线性几何结构，但可以很好地线性近似，为模型透明度方法中常用的线性表征假说提供了实证支持；3. 学习的潜在表征空间可用于量化情感处理任务中的不确定性。

Conclusion: LLMs获得的情感表征具有与人类情感模型相似的几何结构，这对模型可解释性和安全性具有实际意义。研究结果为理解LLMs如何表示情感提供了几何视角，并支持在模型透明度方法中使用线性近似。

Abstract: The geometric structure of latent representations in large language models (LLMs) is an active area of research, driven in part by its implications for model transparency and AI safety. Existing literature has focused mainly on general geometric and topological properties of the learnt representations, but due to a lack of ground-truth latent geometry, validating the findings of such approaches is challenging. Emotion processing provides an intriguing testbed for probing representational geometry, as emotions exhibit both categorical organization and continuous affective dimensions, which are well-established in the psychology literature. Moreover, understanding such representations carries safety relevance. In this work, we investigate the latent structure of affective representations in LLMs using geometric data analysis tools. We present three main findings. First, we show that LLMs learn coherent latent representations of affective emotions that align with widely used valence--arousal models from psychology. Second, we find that these representations exhibit nonlinear geometric structure that can nonetheless be well-approximated linearly, providing empirical support for the linear representation hypothesis commonly assumed in model transparency methods. Third, we demonstrate that the learned latent representation space can be leveraged to quantify uncertainty in emotion processing tasks. Our findings suggest that LLMs acquire affective representations with geometric structure paralleling established models of human emotion, with practical implications for model interpretability and safety.

</details>


### [61] [SCOT: Multi-Source Cross-City Transfer with Optimal-Transport Soft-Correspondence Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07383)
*Yuyao Wang,Min Yang,Meng Chen,Weiming Huang,Yongshun Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOT：基于Sinkhorn熵最优传输的跨城市表示学习框架，通过软对应学习解决区域划分不兼容问题，提高标签稀缺城市的预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市迁移学习面临城市间区域划分不兼容且无真实对应关系的挑战，现有方法要么依赖启发式区域匹配（对锚点选择敏感），要么进行分布级对齐（对应关系隐含且异构性强时不稳定）

Method: 提出SCOT框架：1）使用Sinkhorn熵最优传输学习不等区域集间的显式软对应；2）通过OT加权对比目标锐化可迁移结构；3）采用循环式重构正则化稳定优化；4）对于多源迁移，将每个源和目标对齐到共享原型中心，使用目标诱导的原型先验引导的平衡熵传输

Result: 在真实世界城市和任务中，SCOT持续提高迁移准确性和鲁棒性，同时学习到的传输耦合和中心分配为对齐质量提供可解释的诊断

Conclusion: SCOT通过显式软对应学习和结构化优化，有效解决了跨城市迁移中区域划分不兼容的挑战，提供了准确、鲁棒且可解释的迁移学习方案

Abstract: Cross-city transfer improves prediction in label-scarce cities by leveraging labeled data from other cities, but it becomes challenging when cities adopt incompatible partitions and no ground-truth region correspondences exist. Existing approaches either rely on heuristic region matching, which is often sensitive to anchor choices, or perform distribution-level alignment that leaves correspondences implicit and can be unstable under strong heterogeneity. We propose SCOT, a cross-city representation learning framework that learns explicit soft correspondences between unequal region sets via Sinkhorn-based entropic optimal transport. SCOT further sharpens transferable structure with an OT-weighted contrastive objective and stabilizes optimization through a cycle-style reconstruction regularizer. For multi-source transfer, SCOT aligns each source and the target to a shared prototype hub using balanced entropic transport guided by a target-induced prototype prior. Across real-world cities and tasks, SCOT consistently improves transfer accuracy and robustness, while the learned transport couplings and hub assignments provide interpretable diagnostics of alignment quality.

</details>


### [62] [Decisions and Deployment: The Five-Year SAHELI Project (2020-2025) on Restless Multi-Armed Bandits for Improving Maternal and Child Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07384)
*Shresth Verma,Arpan Dasgupta,Neha Madhiwalla,Aparna Taneja,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAHELI项目使用决策聚焦学习的RMAB框架优化印度母婴健康项目中稀缺资源的分配，相比传统两阶段方法将累计参与度下降减少31%，并显著改善真实健康行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在母婴健康项目中，有限的医疗工作者资源无法为弱势受益者提供持续、个性化的服务，需要优化稀缺资源的调度以最大化长期参与度

Method: 采用决策聚焦学习的Restless Multi-Armed Bandit框架，直接从学习目标（最大化参与度）出发，替代传统的"预测-优化"两阶段方法

Result: 大规模随机对照试验显示，DFL策略相比当前标准护理将累计参与度下降减少31%，显著优于两阶段模型，并转化为真实健康行为的显著改善（如铁和钙补充剂的持续摄入）

Conclusion: SAHELI项目为在健康项目中应用序列决策AI优化资源分配提供了可扩展的蓝图，展示了AI驱动的资源分配能有效改善健康结果

Abstract: Maternal and child health is a critical concern around the world. In many global health programs disseminating preventive care and health information, limited healthcare worker resources prevent continuous, personalised engagement with vulnerable beneficiaries. In such scenarios, it becomes crucial to optimally schedule limited live-service resources to maximise long-term engagement. To address this fundamental challenge, the multi-year SAHELI project (2020-2025), in collaboration with partner NGO ARMMAN, leverages AI to allocate scarce resources in a maternal and child health program in India. The SAHELI system solves this sequential resource allocation problem using a Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (RMAB) framework. A key methodological innovation is the transition from a traditional Two-Stage "predict-then-optimize" approach to Decision-Focused Learning (DFL), which directly aligns the framework's learning method with the ultimate goal of maximizing beneficiary engagement. Empirical evaluation through large-scale randomized controlled trials demonstrates that the DFL policy reduced cumulative engagement drops by 31% relative to the current standard of care, significantly outperforming the Two-Stage model. Crucially, the studies also confirmed that this increased program engagement translates directly into statistically significant improvements in real-world health behaviors, notably the continued consumption of vital iron and calcium supplements by new mothers. Ultimately, the SAHELI project provides a scalable blueprint for applying sequential decision-making AI to optimize resource allocation in health programs.

</details>


### [63] [Fast Heterogeneous Serving: Scalable Mixed-Scale LLM Allocation for SLO-Constrained Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07472)
*Jiaming Cheng,Duong Tung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种约束感知启发式算法（GH和AGH）用于大规模LLM推理部署优化，在保证SLO约束下显著提升求解效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM推理部署面临模型选择、异构GPU配置、并行策略和工作负载分配等多维度优化问题，现有MILP方法虽能保证最优性但扩展性差，需要高效启发式算法

Method: 提出两种约束感知启发式算法：1) 贪婪启发式(GH)用于单次分配；2) 自适应贪婪启发式(AGH)通过多起点构造、重定位局部搜索和GPU整合增强GH。包含三种约束感知机制：TP感知可行性选择、成本-有效覆盖率排名、TP升级

Result: 在Azure LLM推理轨迹校准的工作负载上，两种启发式算法在1秒内生成可行解，AGH接近最优成本且在大规模实例上实现260倍加速。在参数膨胀1.5倍的样本外压力测试中，AGH保持可控的SLO违规和稳定成本，而精确求解器性能急剧下降

Conclusion: AGH算法在大规模LLM推理部署优化中实现了效率与质量的良好平衡，能够处理紧密耦合的约束条件并在动态环境下保持鲁棒性

Abstract: Deploying large language model (LLM) inference at scale requires jointly selecting base models, provisioning heterogeneous GPUs, configuring parallelism, and distributing workloads under tight latency, accuracy, and budget constraints. Exact mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) approaches guarantee optimality but scale poorly. We propose two constraint-aware heuristics: a Greedy Heuristic (GH) for single-pass allocation, and an Adaptive Greedy Heuristic (AGH) that enhances GH via multi-start construction, relocate-based local search, and GPU consolidation. Three constraint-aware mechanisms -- TP-aware feasibility selection, cost-per-effective-coverage ranking, and TP upgrade -- ensure feasibility under tightly coupled memory, delay, error, and budget constraints. On workloads calibrated with the Azure LLM Inference Trace (2025), both heuristics produce feasible solutions in under one second, with AGH closely approaching optimal cost while achieving over 260x speedup on large-scale instances. Under out-of-sample stress tests with up to 1.5x parameter inflation, AGH maintains controlled SLO violations and stable cost, whereas the exact solver's placement degrades sharply.

</details>


### [64] [Event-Centric World Modeling with Memory-Augmented Retrieval for Embodied Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07392)
*Fan Zhaowen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于事件中心世界建模与记忆增强检索的具身决策框架，通过语义事件表示和案例检索实现可解释、物理一致的实时控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端学习方法在动态安全关键环境中缺乏可解释性，且难以保证物理约束一致性，需要更透明、物理基础的决策框架

Method: 构建事件中心世界模型，将环境表示为结构化语义事件集，编码为置换不变潜在表示；通过记忆增强检索从经验知识库中检索关联事件与机动方案，加权组合生成最终动作

Result: 在无人机飞行场景中验证，框架满足实时控制约束，同时保持可解释性和行为一致性，通过物理知识增强检索确保系统动力学一致性

Conclusion: 事件中心世界建模与记忆增强检索框架为动态安全关键环境提供可解释、物理一致且计算高效的决策方案，支持基于案例推理的透明决策过程

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in dynamic and safety-critical environments require decision-making frameworks that are both computationally efficient and physically grounded. However, many existing approaches rely on end-to-end learning, which often lacks interpretability and explicit mechanisms for ensuring consistency with physical constraints. In this work, we propose an event-centric world modeling framework with memory-augmented retrieval for embodied decision-making. The framework represents the environment as a structured set of semantic events, which are encoded into a permutation-invariant latent representation. Decision-making is performed via retrieval over a knowledge bank of prior experiences, where each entry associates an event representation with a corresponding maneuver. The final action is computed as a weighted combination of retrieved solutions, providing a transparent link between decision and stored experiences. The proposed design enables structured abstraction of dynamic environments and supports interpretable decision-making through case-based reasoning. In addition, incorporating physics-informed knowledge into the retrieval process encourages the selection of maneuvers that are consistent with observed system dynamics. Experimental evaluation in UAV flight scenarios demonstrates that the framework operates within real-time control constraints while maintaining interpretable and consistent behavior.

</details>


### [65] [Flux Attention: Context-Aware Hybrid Attention for Efficient LLMs Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07394)
*Quantong Qiu,Zhiyi Hong,Yi Yang,Haitian Wang,Kebin Liu,Qingqing Dang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux Attention：一种上下文感知的层级动态注意力框架，通过轻量级层路由器在FA和SA之间自适应路由，解决长上下文场景中标准注意力的二次复杂度瓶颈，实现性能与推理速度的优越权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的二次计算复杂度限制了LLM在长上下文场景的可扩展性。现有混合注意力方法通常采用静态分配比例，无法适应不同任务的动态检索需求，且头级动态稀疏性常导致计算负载不平衡和同步长尾问题，阻碍自回归解码的硬件加速。

Method: 提出Flux Attention框架，在冻结的预训练LLM中集成轻量级层路由器，根据输入上下文自适应地将每层路由到全注意力（FA）或稀疏注意力（SA）。这种层级路由保持了高保真信息检索，同时确保连续内存访问，将理论计算减少转化为实际时钟加速。

Result: 在多个长上下文和数学推理基准测试中，Flux Attention相比基线模型实现了性能与推理速度的优越权衡，在预填充和解码阶段分别达到最高2.8倍和2.0倍的加速。该参数高效方法仅需在8×A800 GPU上进行12小时训练。

Conclusion: Flux Attention通过上下文感知的层级动态注意力路由，有效解决了长上下文LLM的可扩展性瓶颈，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理速度，为实际部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic computational complexity of standard attention mechanisms presents a severe scalability bottleneck for LLMs in long-context scenarios. While hybrid attention mechanisms combining Full Attention (FA) and Sparse Attention (SA) offer a potential solution, existing methods typically rely on static allocation ratios that fail to accommodate the variable retrieval demands of different tasks. Furthermore, head-level dynamic sparsity often introduces severe computational load imbalance and synchronization long-tails, which hinder hardware acceleration during autoregressive decoding. To bridge this gap, we introduce Flux Attention, a context-aware framework that dynamically optimizes attention computation at the layer level. By integrating a lightweight Layer Router into frozen pretrained LLMs, the proposed method adaptively routes each layer to FA or SA based on the input context. This layer-wise routing preserves high-fidelity information retrieval while ensuring contiguous memory access, translating theoretical computational reductions into practical wall-clock speedups. As a parameter-efficient approach, our framework requires only 12 hours of training on 8$\times$A800 GPUs. Extensive experiments across multiple long-context and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Flux Attention achieves a superior trade-off between performance and inference speed compared with baseline models, with speed improvements of up to $2.8\times$ and $2.0\times$ in the prefill and decode stages.

</details>


### [66] [Critical Patch-Aware Sparse Prompting with Decoupled Training for Continual Learning on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07399)
*Wonseon Lim,Jaesung Lee,Dae-Won Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CPS-Prompt：一种面向边缘设备持续学习的临界感知稀疏提示框架，通过关键补丁采样和分离式提示与分类器训练，显著降低训练时内存占用和计算成本，同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的持续学习不仅需要高精度，还需要训练时的高效性以适应严格的内存和计算约束。现有的基于提示的持续学习方法主要关注精度或推理性能，往往忽视了设备上训练的内存和计算成本。

Method: 提出CPS-Prompt框架，包含两个核心技术：1）关键补丁采样（CPS）进行任务感知的令牌缩减，减少计算量；2）分离式提示与分类器训练（DPCT）降低反向传播开销。

Result: 在三个公共基准测试和真实边缘硬件上的实验表明，CPS-Prompt相比平衡的CODA-Prompt基线，峰值内存、训练时间和能效提升约1.6倍，同时平均精度保持在最先进的C-Prompt的2%以内，与CODA-Prompt精度相当。

Conclusion: CPS-Prompt在保持高精度的同时，显著提升了边缘设备持续学习的训练效率，为资源受限环境下的设备上自适应提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) on edge devices requires not only high accuracy but also training-time efficiency to support on-device adaptation under strict memory and computational constraints. While prompt-based continual learning (PCL) is parameter-efficient and achieves competitive accuracy, prior work has focused mainly on accuracy or inference-time performance, often overlooking the memory and computational costs of on-device training. In this paper, we propose CPS-Prompt, a critical patch-aware sparse prompting framework that explicitly targets training-time memory usage and computational cost by integrating critical patch sampling (CPS) for task-aware token reduction and decoupled prompt and classifier training (DPCT) to reduce backpropagation overhead. Experiments on three public benchmarks and real edge hardware show that CPS-Prompt improves peak memory, training time, and energy efficiency by about 1.6x over the balanced CODA-Prompt baseline, while maintaining accuracy within 2% of the state-of-the-art C-Prompt on average and remaining competitive with CODA-Prompt in accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/laymond1/cps-prompt.

</details>


### [67] [Accelerating Training of Autoregressive Video Generation Models via Local Optimization with Representation Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07402)
*Yucheng Zhou,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Local Opt.和ReCo方法加速自回归视频生成训练，在保持质量的同时将训练成本减半


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在图像生成中表现出色，但在视频生成中面临高计算成本和长训练时间的限制，需要探索加速训练的方法

Method: 提出两种方法：1) Local Optimization (Local Opt.) - 在局部窗口内优化token，利用上下文信息减少误差传播；2) Representation Continuity (ReCo) - 受Lipschitz连续性启发，使用连续性损失约束表示变化，提高模型鲁棒性

Result: 在类别和文本到视频数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在保持质量的同时实现了优于基线的性能，并将训练成本减半

Conclusion: 提出的Local Opt.和ReCo策略有效解决了自回归视频生成训练中的误差累积和一致性问题，显著加速了训练过程

Abstract: Autoregressive models have shown superior performance and efficiency in image generation, but remain constrained by high computational costs and prolonged training times in video generation. In this study, we explore methods to accelerate training for autoregressive video generation models through empirical analyses. Our results reveal that while training on fewer video frames significantly reduces training time, it also exacerbates error accumulation and introduces inconsistencies in the generated videos. To address these issues, we propose a Local Optimization (Local Opt.) method, which optimizes tokens within localized windows while leveraging contextual information to reduce error propagation. Inspired by Lipschitz continuity, we propose a Representation Continuity (ReCo) strategy to improve the consistency of generated videos. ReCo utilizes continuity loss to constrain representation changes, improving model robustness and reducing error accumulation. Extensive experiments on class- and text-to-video datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance to the baseline while halving the training cost without sacrificing quality.

</details>


### [68] [GAN-based Domain Adaptation for Image-aware Layout Generation in Advertising Poster Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07409)
*Chenchen Xu,Min Zhou,Tiezheng Ge,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于GAN的广告海报布局生成方法，通过构建CGL数据集并设计PDA-GAN模型，结合像素级判别器实现图像感知的布局生成，同时提出了三个内容感知评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布局在平面设计和海报生成中至关重要，现有基于深度学习的布局生成方法需要成对的产品图像和布局数据集。本文旨在解决因修复伪影导致的修复海报与干净图像之间的域差距问题，实现图像感知的布局生成。

Method: 1) 构建CGL数据集（60,548对修复海报和121,000张干净产品图像）；2) 设计CGL-GAN模型，在修复区域使用高斯模糊生成布局；3) 提出PDA-GAN模型，结合无监督域适应，通过像素级判别器生成图像感知布局；4) 提出三个内容感知评估指标。

Result: 定量和定性评估表明，PDA-GAN实现了最先进的性能，能够生成高质量的图像感知布局。提出的内容感知指标有效评估了模型捕捉图形元素与图像内容之间复杂关系的能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了修复海报与干净图像之间的域差距问题，提出的PDA-GAN模型能够生成高质量的图像感知布局，为广告海报的自动化设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Layout plays a crucial role in graphic design and poster generation. Recently, the application of deep learning models for layout generation has gained significant attention. This paper focuses on using a GAN-based model conditioned on images to generate advertising poster graphic layouts, requiring a dataset of paired product images and layouts. To address this task, we introduce the Content-aware Graphic Layout Dataset (CGL-Dataset), consisting of 60,548 paired inpainted posters with annotations and 121,000 clean product images. The inpainting artifacts introduce a domain gap between the inpainted posters and clean images. To bridge this gap, we design two GAN-based models. The first model, CGL-GAN, uses Gaussian blur on the inpainted regions to generate layouts. The second model combines unsupervised domain adaptation by introducing a GAN with a pixel-level discriminator (PD), abbreviated as PDA-GAN, to generate image-aware layouts based on the visual texture of input images. The PD is connected to shallow-level feature maps and computes the GAN loss for each input-image pixel. Additionally, we propose three novel content-aware metrics to assess the model's ability to capture the intricate relationships between graphic elements and image content. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that PDA-GAN achieves state-of-the-art performance and generates high-quality image-aware layouts.

</details>


### [69] [Multimodal Large Language Models for Multi-Subject In-Context Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07422)
*Yucheng Zhou,Dubing Chen,Huan Zheng,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUSIC是首个专门为多主体上下文图像生成设计的MLLM，通过自动数据生成管道、视觉思维链机制和语义驱动的空间布局规划，解决了多主体图像生成中的主体缺失和语义漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成方法在处理多个给定主体时存在主体缺失和语义漂移问题，随着参考身份数量的增加，这些问题更加严重，需要专门的多主体上下文图像生成解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出自动可扩展的数据生成管道，无需人工标注；2) 引入视觉思维链机制，通过逐步推理从主体图像到语义再到生成；3) 开发语义驱动的空间布局规划方法，管理视觉复杂性；4) 训练时加入复杂主体图像以增强链式推理能力；5) 构建MSIC基准测试集。

Result: 实验结果表明，MUSIC在多主体和单主体场景下均显著优于其他方法，在MSIC新基准测试中表现出色。

Conclusion: MUSIC通过创新的数据生成、视觉思维链和空间布局规划，有效解决了多主体图像生成的挑战，为多主体上下文图像生成提供了首个专门的MLLM解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have enabled visually coherent image synthesis from descriptions, but generating images containing multiple given subjects remains challenging. As the number of reference identities increases, existing methods often suffer from subject missing and semantic drift. To address this problem, we propose MUSIC, the first MLLM specifically designed for \textbf{MU}lti-\textbf{S}ubject \textbf{I}n-\textbf{C}ontext image generation. To overcome the data scarcity, we introduce an automatic and scalable data generation pipeline that eliminates the need for manual annotation. Furthermore, we enhance the model's understanding of multi-subject semantic relationships through a vision chain-of-thought (CoT) mechanism, guiding step-by-step reasoning from subject images to semantics and generation. To mitigate identity entanglement and manage visual complexity, we develop a novel semantics-driven spatial layout planning method and demonstrate its test-time scalability. By incorporating complex subject images during training, we improve the model's capacity for chained reasoning. In addition, we curate MSIC, a new benchmark tailored for multi-subject in-context generation. Experimental results demonstrate that MUSIC significantly surpasses other methods in both multi- and single-subject scenarios.

</details>


### [70] [GIRL: Generative Imagination Reinforcement Learning via Information-Theoretic Hallucination Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07426)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GIRL提出了一种基于潜在世界模型的强化学习框架，通过跨模态基础信号和不确定性自适应信任区域瓶颈来解决模型误差累积和想象轨迹漂移问题，显著提高了长时程规划的样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）通过想象轨迹优化策略来提高样本效率，但长时程规划中模型误差会累积，导致想象轨迹偏离训练流形，从而降低规划质量。现有方法难以有效控制这种漂移现象。

Method: GIRL包含两个核心组件：1）使用冻结基础模型（DINOv2）的跨模态基础信号，将潜在转移先验锚定到语义一致的嵌入空间，惩罚不一致或不可信的预测；2）不确定性自适应信任区域瓶颈，将KL正则化器解释为约束优化问题的拉格朗日乘子，通过期望信息增益和相对性能损失信号校准的学习区域限制想象漂移。

Result: 在DeepMind Control、Adroit Hand Manipulation和带视觉干扰的Meta-World三个基准测试中，GIRL相对于DreamerV3将潜在轨迹漂移减少了38%到61%，提高了渐近回报，并在长时程任务中需要更少的环境交互。在稀疏奖励和高接触设置下，GIRL也优于TD-MPC2。蒸馏先验变体减少了推理开销并提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: GIRL通过跨模态基础化和自适应信任区域机制有效解决了模型误差累积导致的想象轨迹漂移问题，显著提高了基于模型强化学习在长时程规划中的样本效率和性能，为复杂环境中的强化学习提供了更可靠的规划框架。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) improves sample efficiency by optimizing policies inside imagined rollouts, but long-horizon planning degrades when model errors compound and imagined trajectories drift off the training manifold. We introduce GIRL (Generative Imagination Reinforcement Learning), a latent world-model framework that addresses this failure mode with two key components. First, a cross-modal grounding signal derived from a frozen foundation model (DINOv2) anchors the latent transition prior to a semantically consistent embedding space, penalizing inconsistent or implausible predictions. Second, an uncertainty-adaptive trust-region bottleneck interprets the KL regularizer as the Lagrange multiplier of a constrained optimization problem, restricting imagination drift within a learned region calibrated by Expected Information Gain and a Relative Performance Loss signal.
  We re-derive a value-gap bound using the Performance Difference Lemma and Integral Probability Metrics, yielding a bound that remains informative as the discount factor approaches one and connects the objective to real-environment regret. Experiments across three benchmark suites, including DeepMind Control, Adroit Hand Manipulation, and Meta-World with visual distractors, show that GIRL reduces latent rollout drift by 38 to 61 percent across tasks relative to DreamerV3, improves asymptotic return, and requires fewer environment interactions on long-horizon tasks. GIRL also outperforms TD-MPC2 on sparse-reward and high-contact settings under standard evaluation metrics. A distilled-prior variant reduces inference overhead and improves computational efficiency relative to the full model.

</details>


### [71] [Regret-Aware Policy Optimization: Environment-Level Memory for Replay Suppression under Delayed Harm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07428)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对强化学习中延迟危害导致的"重放"问题，提出了一种诊断方法和解决方案。在可观测状态-动作对保持平稳的情况下，延迟危害可能导致有害级联反应的重现。作者提出了重放抑制诊断和基于遗憾的策略优化方法，通过在环境中添加持久性危害痕迹和疤痕场来减少历史有害区域的可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通过目标塑造来保证安全性，同时保持环境动态在可观测状态-动作对上的平稳性。但在延迟危害场景下，这种方法可能导致"重放"问题：经过一段冲刷期后，在相同可观测条件下重新引入相同刺激会重现类似的有害级联反应。这种重放现象在平台介导系统中尤为突出，需要新的方法来抑制重放而不显著影响任务性能。

Method: 1. 提出重放抑制诊断(RSD)：一种控制暴露-衰减-重放协议，用于在冻结策略评估下隔离这种失效模式。2. 证明在平稳可观测转移核下，重放无法被结构性地抑制而不导致重放时动作分布的持久性偏移。3. 提出遗憾感知策略优化(RAPO)：通过向环境添加持久性危害痕迹和疤痕场，应用有界、质量保持的转移重新加权来减少历史有害区域的可达性。

Result: 在图扩散任务(50-1000个节点)上，RAPO有效抑制了重放，将重放放大增益(RAG)从0.98降低到0.33(250节点图)，同时保留了82%的任务回报。通过仅在重放期间禁用转移变形，重放放大增益恢复到0.91，这隔离了环境级变形作为因果机制的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在平稳可观测转移核下，重放无法被完全抑制而不导致动作分布的持久性偏移。提出的RAPO方法通过在环境中引入持久性痕迹和疤痕场，结合有界转移重新加权，能够有效抑制重放现象，同时保持大部分任务性能。这为处理强化学习中的延迟危害问题提供了一种新的环境级干预方法。

Abstract: Safety in reinforcement learning (RL) is typically enforced through objective shaping while keeping environment dynamics stationary with respect to observable state-action pairs. Under delayed harm, this can lead to replay: after a washout period, reintroducing the same stimulus under matched observable conditions reproduces a similar harmful cascade.
  We introduce the Replay Suppression Diagnostic (RSD), a controlled exposure-decay-replay protocol that isolates this failure mode under frozen-policy evaluation. We show that, under stationary observable transition kernels, replay cannot be structurally suppressed without inducing a persistent shift in replay-time action distributions.
  Motivated by platform-mediated systems, we propose Regret-Aware Policy Optimization (RAPO), which augments the environment with persistent harm-trace and scar fields and applies a bounded, mass-preserving transition reweighting to reduce reachability of historically harmful regions.
  On graph diffusion tasks (50-1000 nodes), RAPO suppresses replay, reducing re-amplification gain (RAG) from 0.98 to 0.33 on 250-node graphs while retaining 82\% of task return. Disabling transition deformation only during replay restores re-amplification (RAG 0.91), isolating environment-level deformation as the causal mechanism.

</details>


### [72] [SYN-DIGITS: A Synthetic Control Framework for Calibrated Digital Twin Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07513)
*Grace Jiarui Fan,Chengpiao Huang,Tianyi Peng,Kaizheng Wang,Yuhang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SYN-DIGITS是一个用于校准基于LLM的数字孪生模拟的轻量级框架，通过潜在因子模型将数字孪生响应与人类真实数据对齐，显著提升模拟准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在数字孪生模拟中具有灵活性，但存在系统性偏差和校准不足的问题，限制了其可靠性，需要一种模型无关的校准方法来提升模拟准确性。

Method: 提出SYN-DIGITS框架，受因果推断中合成控制方法启发，作为LLM模拟器的后处理层。开发潜在因子模型，通过潜在空间对齐条件实现校准，支持个体层面和分布层面的模拟。

Result: 在13种角色构建、3个LLM和2个数据集上评估10种校准方法。SYN-DIGITS相比未校准基线，个体层面相关性相对提升达50%，分布差异相对减少50-90%，且具有可证明的误差保证。

Conclusion: SYN-DIGITS提供了一个原则性、轻量级且模型无关的校准框架，能够显著提升数字孪生模拟的准确性，支持未见问题和未观测人群的模拟，具有理论保证和实际效果。

Abstract: AI-based persona simulation -- often referred to as digital twin simulation -- is increasingly used for market research, recommender systems, and social sciences. Despite their flexibility, large language models (LLMs) often exhibit systematic bias and miscalibration relative to real human behavior, limiting their reliability. Inspired by synthetic control methods from causal inference, we propose SYN-DIGITS (SYNthetic Control Framework for Calibrated DIGItal Twin Simulation), a principled and lightweight calibration framework that learns latent structure from digital-twin responses and transfers it to align predictions with human ground truth. SYN-DIGITS operates as a post-processing layer on top of any LLM-based simulator and thus is model-agnostic.
