We propose SOLAR (Subspace-Oriented Latent Adapter Reparameterization), a post-training compression framework that substantially reduces the communication cost (i.e., the number of parameters to transmit or store) of PEFT adapters. SOLAR expresses each PEFT update as a linear combination of basis vectors formed from the foundation model's singular vectors with controlled random perturbations. By exploiting the subspace similarity (the alignment of principal directions) between the foundation model and task-specific fine-tuned updates, SOLAR decouples the adapter size from PEFT structure and ensures compact yet expressive representations. It is model-agnostic and compatible with existing PEFT methods, including LoRA, AdaLoRA, and other adapter modules. We theoretically establish a bound on the reconstruction error. Experiments on language and vision tasks using LLaMA, GPT, and ViT models demonstrate that SOLAR preserves task performance while significantly reducing model representation sizes, offering an effective and communication-efficient solution for deployment in distributed systems and edge devices.

</details>


### [111] [ADAPTive Input Training for Many-to-One Pre-Training on Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08398)
*Paul Quinlan,Qingguo Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT提出了一种新的时间序列预训练范式，能够有效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，支持在输入尺寸和通道维度差异极大的数据集上进行混合批次预训练，为构建时间序列基础模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列自监督预训练方法在单数据集预训练、多数据集微调的场景中表现良好，但在预训练阶段加入多个数据集时难以泛化到新数据集。这限制了利用多样数据集构建时间序列基础模型的能力。

Method: 提出ADAPT预训练范式，能够高效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，解决预训练数据输入尺寸和通道维度极端差异的问题，支持在162个时间序列分类数据集上进行混合批次预训练。

Result: 在162个时间序列分类数据集上训练，在分类基准测试中取得了新的最先进性能，成功实现了在时间序列领域同时训练多个数据集。

Conclusion: ADAPT是构建时间序列领域通用基础模型的重要构建模块，解决了多数据集预训练中的泛化挑战，为时间序列基础模型的发展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent work on time-series models has leveraged self-supervised training to learn meaningful features and patterns in order to improve performance on downstream tasks and generalize to unseen modalities. While these pretraining methods have shown great promise in one-to-many scenarios, where a model is pre-trained on one dataset and fine-tuned on a downstream dataset, they have struggled to generalize to new datasets when more datasets are added during pre-training. This is a fundamental challenge in building foundation models for time-series data, as it limits the ability to develop models that can learn from a large variety of diverse datasets available. To address this challenge, we present a new pre-training paradigm for time-series data called ADAPT, which can efficiently align the physical properties of data in the time-series domain, enabling mixed-batch pre-training despite the extreme discrepancies in the input sizes and channel dimensions of pre-training data. We trained on 162 time-series classification datasets and set new state-of-the-art performance for classification benchmarks. We successfully train a model within the time-series domain on a wide range of datasets simultaneously, which is a major building block for building generalist foundation models in time-series domains.

</details>


### [112] [Adversarial Label Invariant Graph Data Augmentations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08404)
*Simon Zhang,Ryan P. DeMilt,Kun Jin,Cathy H. Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIA方法，通过对抗性训练实现协变量偏移下的OoD泛化，利用对抗性标签不变数据增强探索训练数据环境


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中，训练和测试数据常来自不同环境，导致分布偏移。协变量偏移是仅输入数据分布变化而概念分布不变的分布偏移类型，需要有效方法处理此类OoD泛化问题

Method: 提出RIA方法，受Q学习启发，通过对抗性探索训练数据环境。使用对抗性标签不变数据增强诱导新环境，防止模型坍缩为仅适应训练分布。将现有OoD泛化方法重新表述为约束优化问题，开发交替梯度下降-上升算法求解

Result: 在OoD图分类任务上进行了广泛实验，涵盖多种合成和自然分布偏移。实验表明RIA方法相比OoD基线能达到更高准确率

Conclusion: RIA方法能有效处理协变量偏移下的OoD泛化问题，通过对抗性训练和数据增强提升模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OoD) generalization occurs when representation learning encounters a distribution shift. This occurs frequently in practice when training and testing data come from different environments. Covariate shift is a type of distribution shift that occurs only in the input data, while the concept distribution stays invariant. We propose RIA - Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training, a new method for OoD generalization under convariate shift. Motivated by an analogy to $Q$-learning, it performs an adversarial exploration for training data environments. These new environments are induced by adversarial label invariant data augmentations that prevent a collapse to an in-distribution trained learner. It works with many existing OoD generalization methods for covariate shift that can be formulated as constrained optimization problems. We develop an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve the problem, and perform extensive experiments on OoD graph classification for various kinds of synthetic and natural distribution shifts. We demonstrate that our method can achieve high accuracy compared with OoD baselines.

</details>


### [113] [Provably Adaptive Linear Approximation for the Shapley Value and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08438)
*Weida Li,Yaoliang Yu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种在Θ(n)空间约束下高效逼近半值（包括Shapley值）的理论框架和线性空间算法Adalina，显著降低了查询复杂度，并建立了现有方法的统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值及其半值家族在归因问题中应用广泛，但精确计算需要指数级查询复杂度。大规模应用需要高效近似方法，特别是在线性空间约束下探索逼近极限。

Method: 基于向量集中不等式建立理论框架，系统开发线性空间算法，统一OFA、unbiased kernelSHAP、SHAP-IQ和回归调整方法，引入自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法Adalina。

Result: 理论框架将查询复杂度降至O(n/ε² log(1/δ))，Adalina算法在理论上实现改进的均方误差，所有理论结果均通过实验验证。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了半值高效近似的统一理论框架，提出了首个自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法，为大规模归因问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems. A fundamental and long-standing challenge is their efficient approximation, since exact computation generally requires an exponential number of utility queries in the number of players $n$. To meet the challenges of large-scale applications, we explore the limits of efficiently approximating semi-values under a $Θ(n)$ space constraint. Building upon a vector concentration inequality, we establish a theoretical framework that enables sharper query complexities for existing unbiased randomized algorithms. Within this framework, we systematically develop a linear-space algorithm that requires $O(\frac{n}{ε^{2}}\log\frac{1}δ)$ utility queries to ensure $P(\|\hat{\boldsymbolφ}-\boldsymbolφ\|_{2}\geqε)\leq δ$ for all commonly used semi-values. In particular, our framework naturally bridges OFA, unbiased kernelSHAP, SHAP-IQ and the regression-adjusted approach, and definitively characterizes when paired sampling is beneficial. Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.

</details>


### [114] [Less Approximates More: Harmonizing Performance and Confidence Faithfulness via Hybrid Post-Training for High-Stakes Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08454)
*Haokai Ma,Lee Yan Zhen,Gang Yang,Yunshan Ma,Ee-Chien Chang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyTuning：一种混合后训练框架，通过渐进推理增益度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏和内部反馈强化学习，在有限监督下提升准确性和置信度忠实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高风险任务中部署时，错误但自信的推断可能导致严重现实危害，需要解决置信度忠实性问题。现有联合优化方法面临高质量训练语料稀缺、事实无根据的过度自信和错误更新放大等挑战。

Method: 提出渐进推理增益度量推理步骤是否逐步增强对最终答案的支持；引入HyTuning混合后训练框架，通过PRG风格度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏和内部反馈强化学习，使用稀缺监督推理轨迹作为稳定锚点，同时利用大量未标记查询实现可扩展性。

Result: 在多个领域特定和通用基准测试中，HyTuning在有限监督下提高了准确性并实现了置信度忠实性，支持"少近似多"的实际效果。

Conclusion: HyTuning通过渐进推理增益度量和自适应混合优化，有效解决了大型语言模型置信度忠实性问题，在有限监督下实现了准确性和置信度校准的平衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes tasks, where confident yet incorrect inferences may cause severe real-world harm, bringing the previously overlooked issue of confidence faithfulness back to the forefront. A promising solution is to jointly optimize unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) with reasoning-trace-guided Reasoning Distillation (RD), which may face three persistent challenges: scarcity of high-quality training corpora, factually unwarranted overconfidence and indiscriminate fusion that amplifies erroneous updates. Inspired by the human confidence accumulation from uncertainty to certainty, we propose Progressive Reasoning Gain (PRG) to measure whether reasoning steps progressively strengthen support for the final answer. Furthermore, we introduce HyTuning, a hybrid post-training framework that adaptively reweights RD and RLIF via a PRG-style metric, using scarce supervised reasoning traces as a stable anchor while exploiting abundant unlabeled queries for scalability. Experiments on several domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that HyTuning improves accuracy while achieving confidence faithfulness under limited supervision, supporting a practical "Less Approximates More" effect.

</details>


### [115] [A Machine Learning Framework for Turbofan Health Estimation via Inverse Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08460)
*Milad Leyli-Abadi,Lucas Thil,Sebastien Razakarivony,Guillaume Doquet,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了从涡轮风扇发动机传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标的逆问题，引入了包含维护事件和使用变化的新数据集，比较了稳态/非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器和自监督学习方法，发现传统滤波器仍是强基线，自监督方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题，受限于稀疏传感和复杂的非线性热力学。该领域研究分散，现有比较基于不现实的数据集，且对时间信息利用不足。本研究旨在从实际运行传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标，考虑真实的退化模式和维护事件。

Method: 1) 引入包含行业导向复杂性（维护事件、使用变化）的新数据集；2) 建立基准比较稳态和非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器；3) 提出自监督学习方法，在无法获取真实健康标签的情况下学习潜在表示；4) 通过比较无监督表示与直接预测基线的下游估计性能，建立解决该逆问题的实际难度下界。

Result: 1) 传统滤波器（贝叶斯滤波器）仍是强基线；2) 自监督学习方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性；3) 结果表明需要更先进和可解释的推理策略；4) 为可重复性提供了生成的数据集和实现代码。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入更现实的数据集和系统比较方法，为涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计建立了新的基准。结果表明传统方法仍有竞争力，而自监督学习揭示了问题的复杂性，强调了开发更先进、可解释推理策略的必要性。数据集和代码的公开促进了该领域的可重复研究。

Abstract: Estimating the health state of turbofan engines is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem, hindered by sparse sensing and complex nonlinear thermodynamics. Research in this area remains fragmented, with comparisons limited by the use of unrealistic datasets and insufficient exploration of the exploitation of temporal information. This work investigates how to recover component-level health indicators from operational sensor data under realistic degradation and maintenance patterns. To support this study, we introduce a new dataset that incorporates industry-oriented complexities such as maintenance events and usage changes. Using this dataset, we establish an initial benchmark that compares steady-state and nonstationary data-driven models, and Bayesian filters, classic families of methods used to solve this problem. In addition to this benchmark, we introduce self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches that learn latent representations without access to true health labels, a scenario reflective of real-world operational constraints. By comparing the downstream estimation performance of these unsupervised representations against the direct prediction baselines, we establish a practical lower bound on the difficulty of solving this inverse problem. Our results reveal that traditional filters remain strong baselines, while SSL methods reveal the intrinsic complexity of health estimation and highlight the need for more advanced and interpretable inference strategies. For reproducibility, both the generated dataset and the implementation used in this work are made accessible.

</details>


### [116] [TTVS: Boosting Self-Exploring Reinforcement Learning via Test-time Variational Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08468)
*Sikai Bai,Haoxi Li,Jie Zhang,Yongjiang Liu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTVS框架通过动态合成测试查询的语义等价变体，使大型推理模型能够在无标签测试数据上进行自我进化，克服了传统测试时方法依赖静态查询集和RLVR范式需要昂贵监督的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型推理模型主要依赖带有可验证奖励的强化学习，但在专业或新兴领域获取这种监督成本过高或不可得。现有测试时方法又受限于静态查询集，容易过拟合文本模式，无法实现真正的测试时适应。

Method: 提出测试时变分合成框架，包含两个协同模块：1) 在线变分合成模块，将静态测试查询转化为动态多样的语义等价变体流，迫使模型学习底层问题逻辑而非表面模式；2) 测试时混合探索模块，在合成变体间平衡准确性驱动的利用和一致性驱动的探索。

Result: 在八种模型架构上的广泛实验表明，TTVS仅使用无标签测试数据就超越了其他测试时适应方法，甚至优于使用大量高质量标签数据训练的最先进监督RL技术。

Conclusion: TTVS为大型推理模型在缺乏监督的领域提供了有效的测试时自适应解决方案，通过动态合成语义等价变体使模型能够自我进化，学习底层逻辑而非表面模式，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) driven by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), this paradigm is fundamentally limited in specialized or novel domains where such supervision is prohibitively expensive or unavailable, posing a key challenge for test-time adaptation. While existing test-time methods offer a potential solution, they are constrained by learning from static query sets, risking overfitting to textual patterns. To address this gap, we introduce Test-Time Variational Synthesis (TTVS), a novel framework that enables LRMs to self-evolve by dynamically augmenting the training stream from unlabeled test queries. TTVS comprises two synergistic modules: (1) Online Variational Synthesis, which transforms static test queries into a dynamic stream of diverse, semantically-equivalent variations, enforcing the model to learn underlying problem logic rather than superficial patterns; (2) Test-time Hybrid Exploration, which balances accuracy-driven exploitation with consistency-driven exploration across synthetic variants. Extensive experiments show TTVS yields superior performance across eight model architectures. Notably, using only unlabeled test-time data, TTVS not only surpasses other test-time adaptation methods but also outperforms state-of-the-art supervised RL-based techniques trained on vast, high-quality labeled data.

</details>


### [117] [Quantization Impact on the Accuracy and Communication Efficiency Trade-off in Federated Learning for Aerospace Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08474)
*Abdelkarim Loukili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在航空航天联邦学习中对称均匀量化对精度-效率权衡的影响，发现INT4量化在保持与FP32统计无差异精度的同时，将梯度通信成本降低8倍，而INT2量化则因不稳定而不适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习(FL)能够在分布式航空航天机队中实现隐私保护的预测性维护，但梯度通信开销限制了其在带宽有限的物联网节点上的部署。需要研究量化技术来平衡精度和效率。

Method: 使用对称均匀量化(b∈{32,8,4,2}比特)研究对定制设计的轻量级1-D卷积模型(AeroConv1D, 9,697参数)在NASA C-MAPSS基准测试上的影响。采用严格的多次评估(N=10种子)，在现实的非独立同分布客户端分区下进行联邦学习训练，并与IID分区进行直接比较。

Result: INT4量化在FD001(p=0.341)和FD002(p=0.264 MAE, p=0.534 NASA评分)上达到与FP32统计无差异的精度，同时将每轮梯度通信成本从37.88 KiB降低到4.73 KiB(8倍减少)。INT2量化虽然在某些指标上表现较好，但表现出灾难性的不稳定性(变异系数45.8% vs FP32的22.3%)。Xilinx ZCU102 FPGA资源分析显示INT4满足硬件约束(85.5% DSP利用率)。

Conclusion: INT4量化是航空航天联邦学习的可行解决方案，在保持精度的同时显著降低通信成本。研究强调了正确评估非独立同分布客户端分区的重要性，因为IID分区会人为抑制方差，掩盖极端量化的真实不稳定性。INT2量化由于不可重现性而不适合实际部署。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving predictive maintenance across distributed aerospace fleets, but gradient communication overhead constrains deployment on bandwidth-limited IoT nodes. This paper investigates the impact of symmetric uniform quantization ($b \in \{32,8,4,2\}$ bits) on the accuracy--efficiency trade-off of a custom-designed lightweight 1-D convolutional model (AeroConv1D, 9\,697 parameters) trained via FL on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark under a realistic Non-IID client partition. Using a rigorous multi-seed evaluation ($N=10$ seeds), we show that INT4 achieves accuracy \emph{statistically indistinguishable} from FP32 on both FD001 ($p=0.341$) and FD002 ($p=0.264$ MAE, $p=0.534$ NASA score) while delivering an $8\times$ reduction in gradient communication cost (37.88~KiB $\to$ 4.73~KiB per round). A key methodological finding is that naïve IID client partitioning artificially suppresses variance; correct Non-IID evaluation reveals the true operational instability of extreme quantization, demonstrated via a direct empirical IID vs.\ Non-IID comparison. INT2 is empirically characterized as unsuitable: while it achieves lower MAE on FD002 through extreme quantization-induced over-regularization, this apparent gain is accompanied by catastrophic NASA score instability (CV\,=\,45.8\% vs.\ 22.3\% for FP32), confirming non-reproducibility under heterogeneous operating conditions. Analytical FPGA resource projections on the Xilinx ZCU102 confirm that INT4 fits within hardware constraints (85.5\% DSP utilization), potentially enabling a complete FL pipeline on a single SoC. The full simulation codebase and FPGA estimation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/therealdeadbeef/aerospace-fl-quantization.

</details>


### [118] [What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08524)
*Stephen Cheng,Sarah Wiegreffe,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多令牌激活修补框架研究LLM中转向向量的因果机制，发现不同转向方法在相同层使用功能可互换的电路，转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，可稀疏化90-99%仍保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然转向向量是大型语言模型对齐的高效技术，但缺乏对其工作原理的可解释性理解——具体来说，转向向量影响哪些内部机制以及如何导致不同的模型输出。本文旨在研究转向向量有效性的因果机制。

Method: 提出多令牌激活修补框架，对拒绝行为进行综合案例研究。通过数学分解转向的OV电路，分析不同转向方法在相同层的功能可互换电路，并研究转向向量与注意力机制（特别是OV电路和QK电路）的交互方式。

Result: 发现转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而基本忽略QK电路——冻结所有注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%。转向的OV电路可分解为语义可解释的概念。转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持大部分性能，不同转向方法在重要维度子集上具有一致性。

Conclusion: 转向向量通过影响注意力机制的OV电路而非QK电路来改变模型行为，这些电路具有功能可互换性且可稀疏化。转向的OV电路包含语义可解释的概念，为理解转向向量工作机制提供了机械性解释。

Abstract: Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.GL'></div>

# cs.GL [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Jean-Raymond Abrial: A Scientific Biography of a Formal Methods Pioneer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07353)
*Jonathan P. Bowen,Henri Habrias*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 关于Jean-Raymond Abrial学术生涯与贡献的学术传记研究，追溯其从实时语言、数据库到形式化方法（Z、B方法、Event-B）的发展历程及其工业应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Abrial是软件与系统工程形式化方法发展的核心人物，其职业生涯跨越五十多年，在Z规范表示法、B方法和Event-B的创建以及工业应用方面发挥了决定性作用。本文旨在对其生平与工作进行学术传记式梳理，评估其对软件工程和程序形式化推理的持久影响。

Method: 采用学术传记研究方法，追溯Abrial思想从早期实时语言和数据库工作，到形式化规范、精化和证明的基础性贡献，再到工业级工具支持（Atelier B和Rodin平台）的发展历程。将Abrial的贡献置于历史、学术和工业背景中进行定位分析。

Result: 系统梳理了Abrial在形式化方法领域的完整发展轨迹：从早期实时语言和数据库研究，到创建Z规范表示法、B方法和Event-B，再到开发工业级工具支持。展示了这些方法在大规模工业系统中的应用可行性，并评估了其对软件工程和程序形式化推理的持久影响。

Conclusion: Abrial的贡献构成了形式化方法发展的重要里程碑，其工作不仅推动了理论发展，还通过工业级工具支持实现了实际应用，对软件工程和程序形式化推理产生了深远而持久的影响。

Abstract: Jean-Raymond Abrial is one of the central figures in the development of formal methods for software and systems engineering. Over a career spanning more than five decades, he has played a decisive role in the creation of the Z specification notation, the B-Method, and Event-B, and in demonstrating their applicability to large-scale industrial systems. This paper presents a scholarly biographical account of Abrial's life and work, tracing the evolution of his ideas from early work on real-time languages and databases, through foundational contributions to formal specification, refinement, and proof, to the development of industrial-strength tool support such as the Atelier~B and the Rodin platform. The paper situates Abrial's contributions within their historical, intellectual, and industrial contexts, and assesses their lasting impact on software engineering and formal reasoning about programs.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [To Layer or Not to Layer? Evaluating the Effects and Mechanisms of LLM-Generated Feedback on learning performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07469)
*Jie Cao,Chloe Qianhui Zhao,Christian Schunn,Elizabeth A. McLaughlin,Jionghao Lin,Kenneth R. Koedinger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分层反馈（先鼓励和提示再揭示答案）虽然能提高行为参与度和积极感知，但会增加认知负荷，最终导致学习效果比非分层反馈更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能高效生成丰富的形成性反馈，但分层反馈（先提供鼓励和提示再揭示答案）是否能真正提高学习参与度和效果尚不明确，需要实证研究验证。

Method: 将199名参与者随机分配到分层或非分层LLM生成反馈组，评估对学习表现、行为与认知参与度、情感感知的影响，并通过中介分析探究这些因素如何调节学习表现。

Result: 分层反馈略微提高了行为参与度，被认为更鼓励和支持独立性，但同时也引发了更大的心理努力。中介分析显示积极情感路径（感知鼓励）被负面行为路径（需要≥3次提交的任务数量）抵消，认知路径不显著，最终分层反馈的学习效果显著更差。

Conclusion: 分层反馈在增强参与度和积极感知方面存在权衡，但实际会损害学习效果。研究为设计自动化LLM反馈系统提供了整合结果、感知和机制层面的细致见解。

Abstract: Feedback is vital for learning, yet its effectiveness depends not only on its content but also on how it engages students in the learning process. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer novel opportunities to efficiently generate rich, formative feedback, ranging from direct explanations to incrementally layered scaffolding designed to foster learner autonomy. Despite these affordances, it remains unclear whether layered feedback (which sequences encouragement and prompts prior to revealing the correct answer) actually improves engagement and learning outcomes. To address this, we randomly assigned 199 participants to receive either layered or non-layered LLM-generated feedback. We assessed its impact on learning performance, behavioral and cognitive engagement, and affective perceptions, to determine how these factors mediate learning performance. Results indicate that layered feedback elicited slightly higher behavioral engagement and, as anticipated, was perceived as more encouraging and supportive of independence. However, it concurrently induced greater mental effort. Mediation analyses revealed a positive affective pathway driven by perceived encouragement, which was counteracted by a negative behavioral pathway linked to the average number of tasks requiring $\geq 3$ submissions; the cognitive pathway (mental effort) was non-significant. Taken together, layered feedback resulted in significantly poorer learning outcomes compared to non-layered feedback. These findings illuminate a critical trade-off: while layered scaffolding enhances engagement and positive perceptions, it can detrimentally impact actual learning performance. This study contributes nuanced insights for the design of automated, LLM-driven feedback systems by integrating outcome, perception, and mechanism-level analyses.

</details>


### [121] [The Day My Chatbot Changed: Characterizing the Mental Health Impacts of Social AI App Updates via Negative User Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07548)
*Sirajam Munira,Lydia Manikonda*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分析Character AI聊天机器人应用在Google Play上的210,840条用户评论，研究版本更新如何影响负面评价趋势和用户反馈模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI聊天机器人越来越多地用于情感、创意和社交支持，用户与这些系统形成了持续、常规的互动。应用通过频繁版本更新不断演进，功能或行为的变化可能影响用户评价。然而，在真实部署环境中，关于公开表达的用户反馈如何随应用版本变化的研究有限。

Method: 分析Character AI在Google Play上的210,840条用户评论，将每条评论与发布时的应用版本关联。特别关注负面评论，研究版本级别的评分趋势和语言模式如何反映用户体验。采用主题分析方法识别用户不满的集中领域。

Result: 用户评分在连续版本间波动，某些版本与更强的负面评价相关。主题分析显示不满主要集中在技术故障和错误等反复出现的问题上。部分评论还将这些担忧与潜在的心理或成瘾相关影响联系起来。

Conclusion: 研究发现聚合用户评价和表达的关注点随软件迭代而变化，为更新周期如何与用户反馈模式相关提供了实证见解，强调了在AI系统演进过程中稳定性和透明沟通的重要性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) chatbots are increasingly used for emotional, creative, and social support, leading to sustained and routine user interaction with these systems. As these applications evolve through frequent version updates, changes in functionality or behavior may influence how users evaluate them. However, work on how publicly expressed user feedback varies across app versions in real-world deployment contexts is limited. This study analyzes 210,840 Google Play reviews of the chatbot application Character AI, linking each review to the app version active at the time of posting. We specifically examine negative reviews to study how version-level rating trends, and linguistic patterns reflect user experiences. Our results show that user ratings fluctuate across successive versions, with certain releases associated with stronger negative evaluations. Thematic analysis indicates that dissatisfaction is concentrated around recurring issues related to technical malfunctions and errors. A subset of reviews additionally frames these concerns in terms of potential psychological or addiction-related effects. The findings highlight how aggregate user evaluations and expressed concerns vary across software iterations and provide empirical insight into how update cycles relate to user feedback patterns and underscore the importance of stability and transparent communication in evolving AI systems.

</details>


### [122] [Generative Experiences for Digital Mental Health Interventions: Evidence from a Randomized Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07558)
*Ananya Bhattacharjee,Michael Liut,Matthew Jörke,Diyi Yang,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GUIDE系统通过运行时生成个性化干预内容和多模态交互结构，在数字心理健康工具中引入"生成式体验"范式，显著降低用户压力并改善体验


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字心理健康工具主要关注提供何种支持内容，而忽略了如何让用户体验这些内容。即使内容匹配，如果交互形式与用户参与方式不匹配，干预也会失败。需要一种能够动态塑造支持体验和交互方式的新方法。

Method: 提出"生成式体验"范式，开发GUIDE系统，通过基于规则的模块化组件生成，在运行时动态生成个性化干预内容和多模态交互结构。系统使用评分标准指导生成过程。

Result: 在237名参与者的预注册研究中，GUIDE相比基于LLM的认知重构对照组，显著降低了压力水平(p=0.02)并改善了用户体验(p=0.04)。系统支持了多样化的反思和行动形式，同时揭示了交互序列个性化中的张力。

Conclusion: 生成式体验范式为数字心理健康干预开辟了新方向，使系统能够动态塑造支持体验和交互方式，为数字环境中如何体验和实施支持奠定了基础。

Abstract: Digital mental health (DMH) tools have extensively explored personalization of interventions to users' needs and contexts. However, this personalization often targets what support is provided, not how it is experienced. Even well-matched content can fail when the interaction format misaligns with how someone can engage. We introduce generative experience as a paradigm for DMH support, where the intervention experience is composed at runtime. We instantiate this in GUIDE, a system that generates personalized intervention content and multimodal interaction structure through rubric-guided generation of modular components. In a preregistered study with N = 237 participants, GUIDE significantly reduced stress (p = .02) and improved the user experience (p = .04) compared to an LLM-based cognitive restructuring control. GUIDE also supported diverse forms of reflection and action through varied interaction flows, while revealing tensions around personalization across the interaction sequence. This work lays the foundation for interventions that dynamically shape how support is experienced and enacted in digital settings.

</details>


### [123] [Smells Like Fire: Exploring the Impact of Olfactory Cues in VR Wildfire Evacuation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07699)
*Alison Crosby,MJ Johns,Eunsol Sol Choi,Tejas Polu,Katherine Isbister,Sri Kurniawan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索嗅觉刺激（烟雾）对虚拟现实野火疏散准备游戏影响的初步研究，发现烟雾条件显著提升沉浸感，且参与者普遍报告疏散准备意识增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索在虚拟现实疏散准备训练中加入嗅觉刺激（烟雾）是否能增强沉浸感和训练效果，以提升野火疏散准备的真实性和有效性。

Method: 采用对照实验设计，将18名参与者随机分为烟雾组和对照组，两组均完成相同的虚拟现实野火疏散任务，烟雾组额外接受嗅觉刺激。通过任务后问卷调查评估参与者的感知准备度和整体体验。

Result: 烟雾组参与者报告显著高于对照组的沉浸感；两组参与者均表示体验后对真实世界野火疏散的准备意识有所增强。

Conclusion: 在虚拟现实疏散训练中加入嗅觉刺激能有效提升沉浸感，且此类训练能增强参与者对野火疏散的准备意识，为未来更真实的应急训练设计提供了初步证据。

Abstract: This paper presents a pilot study exploring the effects of an olfactory stimulus (smoke) for a Virtual Reality game designed to support wildfire evacuation preparedness. Participants (N=18) were split evenly into either a smoke or a control condition, and both completed the same evacuation task. Post-task surveys assessed the participants' perceived preparedness and overall experience. Initial findings suggest participants in the smoke condition reported significantly higher immersion compared to those in the control condition. Across both groups, participants expressed an increased sense of preparedness for real-world wildfire evacuations following the experience.

</details>


### [124] [From Uncertainty to Possibility: Early Computing Experiences for Rural Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07638)
*Poornima Meegammana,Niranjan Meegammana,Chathurika Jayalath,Chethya Munasinghe,Kunal Gupta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 农村地区少女编程自我效能提升与职业兴趣转变研究：通过本地化课程设计（数字基础→无屏编程→积木编程）结合家长意识和教师性别响应培训，有效提升编程自信并拓宽科技职业志向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村地区少女在计算领域面临多重障碍（资源、语言、性别规范），现有研究多基于资源丰富、英语主导环境，缺乏对本地化路径如何提升编程自我效能和拓宽职业兴趣的理解。

Method: 设计递进式课程：从数字基础和无屏问题解决开始，逐步过渡到积木编程；配套实施家长意识提升和教师性别响应实践培训；采用前后测问卷调查编程自我效能变化，辅以定性数据分析关键影响因素。

Result: 编程自我效能显著可靠提升；职业志向向科技领域转移；定性数据显示掌握经验、同伴协作和个人项目创作是自信提升的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 本地化、渐进式课程结合家长教师支持能有效提升农村少女编程自信和科技职业兴趣；掌握经验、协作和个性化项目是核心设计要素，为低资源社区可扩展项目提供设计优先级，有助于改变"谁属于计算领域"的认知。

Abstract: Girls remain underrepresented in computing, and rural contexts often compound barriers of access, language, and gender norms. Prior work in computing education highlights that confidence and belonging can shape participation, yet most evidence comes from well-resourced, English-dominant settings. Less is known about how locally grounded pathways can build programming self-efficacy and broaden career interest for adolescent girls. We addressed this gap by delivering a curriculum that began with digital foundations and unplugged problem-solving, then progressed to block-based programming activities, supported by parent awareness and teacher training in gender-responsive practices. Pre and post-surveys showed a reliable increase in programming self-efficacy, and career aspirations shifted toward technology. Complementary qualitative data indicate that mastery experiences, peer collaboration, and the creation of personal projects were key drivers of confidence, suggesting design priorities for scalable, locally relevant programmes in low-resource communities that can shift perceptions of who belongs in computing.

</details>


### [125] [Narrix: Remixing Narrative Strategies from Examples for Story Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07643)
*Chao Zhang,Shunan Guo,Abe Davis,Eunyee Koh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Narix是一个帮助新手作家识别和复用叙事策略的写作工具，通过分析示例故事、可视化策略和提供可控生成来提升写作能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验丰富的讲故事者能够将故事分解为局部叙事策略并理解这些策略如何塑造高层次故事弧线，这种分解有助于作家识别他人作品中的模式并适应这些模式来讲述新故事。然而，新手作家难以识别这些策略或有效地复用它们。

Method: 开发Narix写作工具，通过分析示例故事中的叙事策略，使用颜色编码的词汇线索和解释来高亮显示这些策略，并将它们置于交互式故事弧线上，通过情感转变和转折点进行探索。作家可以将策略拖放到多维轨道上，并通过指定策略引导的可控生成，应用块范围编辑来修改或继续他们的草稿。

Result: 通过一项受试者内研究（N=12），与基于聊天的基线写作界面相比，Narix显著提高了参与者对叙事策略的保留、信心和创造性适应能力。

Conclusion: Narix是一种有效的写作工具，能够帮助新手作家识别、理解和复用叙事策略，从而提升他们的写作能力和创造性表达。

Abstract: Experienced storytellers decompose stories into local narrative strategies and how these strategies shape higher-level arcs. This decomposition helps writers recognize patterns in others' work and adapt those patterns to tell new stories. Novices, however, struggle to identify these strategies or to reuse them effectively. We present Narrix, a novel writing tool that helps novice writers recognize narrative strategies in example stories and repurpose these strategies in their own writing. Narrix analyzes strategies in example stories, highlights them with color-coded lexical cues and explanations, and situates them on an interactive story arc for exploration by emotional shifts and turning points. Writers then drag strategies onto multi-dimensional tracks and apply block-scoped edits to revise or continue their drafts through controlled generation steered by specified strategies. Through a within-subjects study (N=12), Narrix showed improved participants' retention, confidence, and creative adaptation of narrative strategies compared to a baseline chat-based writing interface.

</details>


### [126] [Designing Annotations in Visualization: Considerations from Visualization Practitioners and Educators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07691)
*Md Dilshadur Rahman,Devin Lange,Ghulam Jilani Quadri,Paul Rosen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过质性访谈探究可视化标注的设计实践与决策过程，揭示专业人员的启发式方法和教育者的教学视角


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注标注的视觉形式，但对标注设计背后的决策过程关注不足。研究者希望探究实践中标注是如何设计的，以及教育者如何反思这些实践，从而理解标注作为设计活动的专业知识

Method: 采用两阶段质性研究方法：第一阶段访谈10位来自不同背景的实践者，了解他们创建标注时使用的启发式方法；第二阶段访谈7位可视化教育者，获取关于清晰度、指导和观看者能动性等更广泛问题的补充视角

Result: 研究系统性地描述了专业环境中的标注设计知识，突出了影响标注使用的考虑因素、权衡取舍和情境判断。通过使这些隐性专业知识显性化，揭示了标注设计的复杂决策过程

Conclusion: 该研究补充了先前以形式为中心的研究，加强了对标注作为设计活动的理解，并指出了改进工具和指南支持的机会。通过揭示专业实践中的决策过程，为标注设计提供了更全面的理论框架

Abstract: Annotation is a central mechanism in visualization design that enables people to communicate key insights. Prior research has provided essential accounts of the visual forms annotations take, but less attention has been paid to the decisions behind them. This paper examines how annotations are designed in practice and how educators reflect on those practices. We conducted a two-phase qualitative study: interviews with ten practitioners from diverse backgrounds revealed the heuristics they draw on when creating annotations, and interviews with seven visualization educators offered complementary perspectives situated within broader concerns of clarity, guidance, and viewer agency. These studies provide a systematic account of annotation design knowledge in professional settings, highlighting the considerations, trade-offs, and contextual judgments that shape the use of annotations. By making this tacit expertise explicit, our work complements prior form-focused studies, strengthens understanding of annotation as a design activity, and points to opportunities for improved tool and guideline support.

</details>


### [127] [Twitch Third-Party Developers' Support Seeking and Provision Practices on Discord](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07732)
*Jie Cai,He Zhang,Yueyan Liu,John M. Carroll,Chun Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 第三方开发者在无法从平台获得即时支持时转向Discord社区寻求帮助，研究发现这种支持实践构成了平台劳动，且需要在Discord和Twitch之间切换加剧了这种劳动负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索第三方开发者（TPDs）在无法从Twitch平台获得即时支持时，如何通过Discord在线社区进行支持寻求和提供的实践，以及这些实践如何影响开发者的工作负担和平台生态。

Method: 采用混合方法：首先通过主题建模识别Discord社区中与支持寻求和提供相关的主题，然后对这些主题进行深入的定性分析。

Result: 研究发现：(1) TPDs在社会、技术和政策方面的支持寻求实践高度依赖Twitch平台，这种依赖构成了一种平台劳动形式；(2) TPDs需要在Discord和Twitch之间切换进行支持寻求和提供，这加剧了平台劳动负担；(3) TPDs灵活的角色实践反映了Discord社区的繁荣，但需要角色在两个平台之间架起桥梁，将非正式支持寻求转化为可能的Twitch正式支持。

Conclusion: 研究提出了有效管理正式和非正式空间之间支持寻求和提供的建议，以改善TPDs的发展。该研究对社区支持实践和CSCW领域的平台生态研究做出了贡献。

Abstract: Third-party developers (TPDs) often turn to online communities for support when they can't get immediate responses from the platform. Twitch, as a leading live streaming platform, attracted many TPDs and formed an online support community on Discord. This study explores TPDs' support practices via mixed method (a topic modeling to identify topics related to support seeking and provision first and a follow-up in-depth qualitative analysis with these topics) and found that: (1) TPDs' support-seeking practices around social, technical, and policy matters are highly dependent on Twitch, and this dependence acts as a form of platform labor; (2) TPDs need to switch between Discord and Twitch regarding seeking and provision, exacerbating TPDs' platform labor; (3) TPDs' flexible role practices reflect the community's flourishing on Discord but require roles to bridge the two platforms and transfer informal support seeking to possible formal support from Twitch. We propose implications for effectively managing support seeking and provision between formal and informal spaces to improve the development of TPDs. We also contribute to community support practice and to platform ecology work in CSCW.

</details>


### [128] [Language Preferences and Practices in Multilingual EdTech: Flexible Primary Language Use with Secondary Language Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07843)
*Christine Kwon,Phenyo Phemelo Moletsane,Michael W. Asher,Dieyu Ouyang,Lingkan Wang,Debbie Eleene Conejo,John Stamper,Paulo F. Carvalho,Amy Ogan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了乌干达远程教育技术课程中，学习者如何通过混合语言（英语和本地语言Leb-Lango）进行学习，发现坚持使用双语的学习者课程参与时间更长，并首次提供了双语远程教育中学习者自主性的实证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管母语学习的好处已有充分证据，但殖民语言仍主导教育体系，边缘化本地语言，限制了依赖母语理解的学习者。随着教育技术的快速发展，存在整合多语言教学以同时支持殖民语言和本地语言的潜力。

Method: 在乌干达进行的大规模准实验研究，学习者可选择英语、Leb-Lango（本地语言）或混合模式（两种语言结合）参与远程教育技术课程。研究重点分析选择混合模式的学习者如何在不同语言间导航。

Result: 许多混合学习者并未持续使用两种语言，但那些坚持使用双语的学习者在课程中表现出更长的持久性。学习者分享了他们如何管理语言复杂性，研究首次提供了双语远程教育技术教学中学习者自主性的实证证据。

Conclusion: 研究为设计包容性多语言学习解决方案提供了见解，强调了在远程教育技术中支持学习者语言自主选择的重要性，以及双语使用与学习持久性之间的积极关联。

Abstract: The benefits of learning in one's mother tongue are well documented, yet colonial languages dominate education, marginalizing local languages and limiting access for learners who rely on their mother tongue for understanding. With the rapid growth of educational technology, there is potential to integrate multilingual instruction supporting both colonial and local languages. This study is part of a larger quasi-experiment conducted in Uganda, where learners could choose to learn in English, Leb-Lango (a local language), or in Hybrid mode (a combination of both) in a remote EdTech course. We examined how learners who chose the Hybrid option navigated English and Leb-Lango. While many Hybrid learners did not consistently use both languages, those who did persisted longer in the course. Learners also shared how they managed language complexities. We provide the first empirical evidence of learner agency in bilingual remote EdTech instruction and offer insights for designing inclusive multilingual learning solutions.

</details>


### [129] [From Clicking to Moving: Embodied Micro-Movements as a New Modality for Data Literacy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07881)
*Annabella Sakunkoo,Jonathan Sakunkoo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Kinetiq系统将全身微动作融入数据素养学习，通过自然手势替代鼠标点击，将抽象的数据问题解决转化为具身体验，在保持学习效果的同时显著提升情感体验和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字学习虽然普及但导致久坐、点击式交互，引发数字疲劳、认知灵活性下降和健康风险；数据素养作为数据驱动社会的关键能力，通常通过被动、脱离身体的界面教授，缺乏身体参与。

Method: 开发Kinetiq系统，将全身微动作（如伸展、闪避、头球、肘击、抬膝等自然手势）直接融入数据和计算问题解决中，替代传统的鼠标选择答案方式，创建任务整合的运动范式；系统为跨平台Web和移动应用，支持在受限日常空间中进行全身学习。

Result: 初步组内研究显示，与传统平台相比，参与者报告显著更高的情感效价、享受度、参与度和动机，同时保持相当的学习效果；提供了任务整合运动范式、跨平台系统和初步实证证据。

Conclusion: 具身微动作可以丰富数据素养学习的情感体验，Kinetiq系统展示了将身体运动整合到数字学习中的可行性，为减少数字疲劳、提高参与度提供了新途径。

Abstract: Widespread digital learning has expanded access to education but has resulted in highly sedentary, click-based interaction, contributing to digital fatigue, reduced cognitive flexibility, and health risks associated with prolonged passive screen time. Meanwhile, data literacy has become an essential competency in a data-driven society, yet it is typically taught through passive, disembodied interfaces that offer little physical engagement. We present Kinetiq (Kinetic+IQ), a novel system that integrates fun, full-body micro-movements directly into data and numeracy problem solving. Instead of selecting answers with a mouse, learners interact through natural gestures such as reaching, dodging, heading, elbowing, or knee-raising, thus turning abstract data problem-solving into embodied experiences that integrate thinking with movement. In a preliminary within-subjects study comparing Kinetiq with conventional platforms, participants reported significantly higher affective valence, enjoyment, engagement, and motivation, while maintaining comparable learning gains. We contribute: (1) a task-integrated movement paradigm for data learning, (2) a cross-platform web and mobile app system enabling full-body learning in constrained everyday spaces, and (3) preliminary empirical evidence that embodied micro-movements can enrich the affective experience of data literacy learning.

</details>


### [130] [From Gaze to Guidance: Interpreting and Adapting to Users' Cognitive Needs with Multimodal Gaze-Aware AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08062)
*Valdemar Danry,Javier Hernandez,Andrew Wilson,Pattie Maes,Judith Amores*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于凝视感知的多模态LLM助手，通过眼动追踪识别用户阅读困难点，提供针对性帮助，相比纯文本LLM在评估准确性、个性化程度和信息回忆方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM助手在回答问题时功能强大，但缺乏对用户行为上下文（何时何地遇到困难）的访问能力。需要利用凝视等行为信号来识别用户的认知困难点。

Method: 开发凝视感知的多模态LLM助手，使用带有凝视叠加的自中心视频来识别可能的困难点，并针对性地提供回顾性帮助。通过对照研究（n=36）比较凝视感知AI助手与纯文本LLM助手。

Result: 相比传统LLM助手，凝视感知助手在评估用户阅读行为方面被评定为显著更准确和个性化，显著提高了用户的信息回忆能力。用户与凝视感知助手交流时使用的词汇显著减少，表明交互效率更高。定性结果显示了在理解方面的感知益处，但也指出了当凝视行为解释不准确时的挑战。

Conclusion: 凝视感知的LLM助手能够推理用户的认知需求，从而改善用户的认知结果。这种方法为开发更智能、更个性化的AI助手提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Current LLM assistants are powerful at answering questions, but they have limited access to the behavioral context that reveals when and where a user is struggling. We present a gaze-grounded multimodal LLM assistant that uses egocentric video with gaze overlays to identify likely points of difficulty and target follow-up retrospective assistance. We instantiate this vision in a controlled study (n=36) comparing the gaze-aware AI assistant to a text-only LLM assistant. Compared to a conventional LLM assistant, the gaze-aware assistant was rated as significantly more accurate and personalized in its assessments of users' reading behavior and significantly improved people's ability to recall information. Users spoke significantly fewer words with the gaze-aware assistant, indicating more efficient interactions. Qualitative results underscored both perceived benefits in comprehension and challenges when interpretations of gaze behaviors were inaccurate. Our findings suggest that gaze-aware LLM assistants can reason about cognitive needs to improve cognitive outcomes of users.

</details>


### [131] [From Binary Groundedness to Support Relations: Towards a Reader-Centred Taxonomy for Comprehension of AI Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08082)
*Advait Sarkar,Christian Poelitz,Viktor Kewenig*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于读者中心的文本支持关系分类框架，用于评估生成式AI如何将源文档证据转化为答案，超越简单的二元支持判断


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于检索增强生成的AI工具评估方法将答案与源文档关系简化为二元判断（支持或不支持），这掩盖了模型将证据转化为答案时的句法操作（如直接引用vs.释义）和解释性操作（如归纳vs.演绎），限制了基准测试和面向用户的溯源界面开发

Method: 提出开发基于读者中心的文本支持关系分类框架，从语言学和语言哲学的前期研究中综合构建，通过基准测试和人工标注协议进行评估

Result: 论文提出了理论框架和方法论，但尚未展示具体实验结果，主要贡献在于概念框架的提出和研究方向的规划

Conclusion: 需要开发更精细的文本支持关系分类框架，使界面不仅能传达某个主张是否基于证据，还能说明如何基于证据，从而改进生成式AI的基准测试和溯源界面

Abstract: Generative AI tools often answer questions using source documents, e.g., through retrieval augmented generation. Current groundedness and hallucination evaluations largely frame the relationship between an answer and its sources as binary (the answer is either supported or unsupported). However, this obscures both the syntactic moves (e.g., direct quotation vs. paraphrase) and the interpretive moves (e.g., induction vs. deduction) performed when models reformulate evidence into an answer. This limits both benchmarking and user-facing provenance interfaces.
  We propose the development of a reader-centred taxonomy of grounding as a set of support relations between generated statements and source documents. We explain how this might be synthesised from prior research in linguistics and philosophy of language, and evaluated through a benchmark and human annotation protocol. Such a framework would enable interfaces that communicate not just whether a claim is grounded, but how.

</details>


### [132] [StoryEcho: A Generative Child-as-Actor Storytelling System for Picky-Eating Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08114)
*Yanuo Zhou,Jun Fang,Yuntao Wang,Yi Wang,Nan Gao,Jinlei Liu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: StoryEcho：一个生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事系统，用于挑食干预，通过个性化故事让孩子成为故事角色，并根据实际饮食行为更新故事内容


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童挑食问题会破坏饮食多样性、影响健康饮食习惯养成，并造成家庭喂养压力。现有干预措施多关注进餐本身，很少将儿童定位为超越单次进餐互动的主动参与者。需要理解日常应对挑食的方式和儿童可接受的干预机制。

Method: 1. 与看护者和幼儿园教师进行形成性研究；2. 基于设计考虑和利益相关者迭代评审设计StoryEcho系统；3. StoryEcho系统特点：生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事系统，让孩子在非进餐时间参与个性化故事（孩子作为持久故事角色），通过现实世界饮食行为影响故事发展；4. 结合非进餐时间故事参与、轻量级餐后反馈和行为驱动的故事更新；5. 在11个学龄前儿童家庭中进行组间现场研究评估。

Result: 初步证据表明StoryEcho能显著提高儿童接触和尝试低偏好食物的意愿，同时减少父母在喂养方面的压力。结果表明生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事方法作为家庭行为支持设计具有潜力。

Conclusion: 生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事系统（StoryEcho）通过将儿童置于个性化故事中心，并根据其实际饮食行为更新故事内容，能够有效支持儿童挑食干预。这种方法将干预扩展到单次进餐之外，融入日常家庭常规，展示了基于家庭的行为支持设计前景。

Abstract: Picky eating in children can undermine dietary diversity and the development of healthy eating habits, while also creating recurring tension in family feeding routines. Prior interventions have explored food-centered designs, enhanced utensils, and mealtime interactive systems, but few position children as active participants in intervention processes that extend beyond single mealtime interactions. To better understand everyday responses to picky eating and child-acceptable intervention mechanisms, we conducted a formative study with caregivers and kindergarten teachers. Based on the resulting design considerations and iterative stakeholder review, we designed StoryEcho, a generative child-as-actor storytelling system for picky eating intervention. StoryEcho engages children outside mealtimes through personalized stories in which the child appears as a persistent story character and later shapes story development through real-world food-related behavior. The system combines non-mealtime story engagement, lightweight post-meal feedback, and behavior-informed story updates to support repeated intervention across everyday family routines. We evaluated StoryEcho in a between-group field study with 11 families of preschool children. Results provide preliminary evidence that StoryEcho can significantly increase children's willingness to approach and try target low-preference foods while reducing parental pressure around feeding. These findings suggest the promise of generative child-as-actor storytelling as a design approach for home-based behavior support that unfolds through recurring family routines.

</details>


### [133] [Let Me Introduce You: Stimulating Taste-Broadening Serendipity Through Song Introductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08385)
*Brett Binst,Ulysse Maes,Martijn C. Willemsen,Annelien Smets*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索沉浸式和信息性歌曲介绍对拓宽用户音乐品味的效果，发现"运输感"是预测品味拓宽意外发现的最强因素


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐系统难以促进用户探索和多样性聆听，即使能推荐超出用户常规偏好的有价值内容，也难以激发用户兴趣

Method: 通过用户研究，检验沉浸式和信息性歌曲介绍在激发用户对超出常规偏好歌曲兴趣的效果，关注"品味拓宽意外发现"体验

Result: 发现两个重要机制：运输感（沉浸于叙事世界）是品味拓宽意外发现的最强预测因素；认知阐述（了解艺术家或社会背景）效果较弱但更容易激发

Conclusion: 歌曲介绍可以在音乐流媒体平台上促进探索和增加聆听多样性方面发挥重要作用

Abstract: Research on how people experience music emphasizes the importance of exploration and diversity in listening. However, music recommender systems struggle with facilitating exploration. Even when music recommender systems are able to recommend something valuable to users that is outside their typical preferences, it still remains difficult to spark their interest. This paper presents a user study examining the efficacy of immersive and informative introductions in stimulating interest in songs that are beyond one's usual preferences, an experience called Taste-Broadening Serendipity. We uncover two important mechanisms behind the effect of introductions: transportation and cognitive elaboration. Our findings indicate that transportation (i.e., being absorbed into a narrative world) is the strongest predictor of Taste-Broadening Serendipity, while cognitive elaboration (i.e., learning something new about the artist or social context in which the music emerged) has a weaker effect but is easier to stimulate. We propose that song introductions can play an important role in facilitating exploration and increasing diversity of listening on music streaming platforms.

</details>


### [134] [Bridging the Gap between Micro-scale Traffic Simulation and 4D Digital Cityscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08497)
*Longxiang Jiao,Lukas Hofmann,Yiru Yang,Zhanyi Wu,Jonas Egeler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发实时4D可视化框架，将SUMO交通仿真与苏黎世高保真VR场景结合，验证仿真动态与人类感知的相关性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观交通仿真虽为城市规划提供关键数据，但缺乏高保真可视化和听觉化功能，难以支持有效的利益相关者沟通

Method: 构建实时4D可视化框架，通过C++数据管道将SUMO交通仿真与Unreal Engine 5中的苏黎世高保真VR场景同步，集成OSC接口支持外部听觉化引擎，并通过用户研究验证

Result: 用户研究显示仿真交通动态与人类感知高度一致，用户能正确从4D仿真中解读安全风险；空间化音频会改变用户的安全感知，证明多模态在交通仿真中的重要性

Conclusion: 该框架成功将微观交通仿真与高保真多模态呈现相结合，验证了其在利益相关者沟通中的有效性，强调了视觉和听觉多模态对交通仿真感知的重要性

Abstract: While micro-scale traffic simulations provide essential data for urban planning, they are rarely coupled with the high-fidelity visualization or auralization necessary for effective stakeholder communication. In this work, we present a real-time 4D visualization framework that couples the SUMO traffic with a photorealistic, geospatially accurate VR representation of Zurich in Unreal Engine 5. Our architecture implements a robust C++ data pipeline for synchronized vehicle visualization and features an Open Sound Control (OSC) interface to support external auralization engines. We validate the framework through a user study assessing the correlation between simulated traffic dynamics and human perception. Results demonstrate a high degree of perceptual alignment, where users correctly interpret safety risks from the 4D simulation. Furthermore, our findings indicate that the inclusion of spatialized audio alters the user's sense of safety, showing the importance of multimodality in traffic simulations.

</details>


### [135] ["Because we are no longer ashamed of our disabilities, we are proud": Advocating and Reclaiming Next-Gen Accessibility Symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08514)
*Karen Joy,Chris Dodge,Harsh Chavda,Alyssa Sheehan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨无障碍符号与新兴技术如何支持残障披露，通过设计创作会议发现符号与可穿戴设备、移动界面等技术结合时最有效，强调用户对可见性的控制和可选的解释路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索如何通过符号与新兴技术的结合来改善残障人士的披露体验，解决现有符号在采用和解释方面的障碍，创造更包容的无障碍支持系统。

Method: 采用23次远程设计创作会议结合半结构化访谈，通过参与者草图绘制和未来导向故事板探究，分析参与者对现有符号的认知、线上线下使用情况以及采用障碍。

Result: 研究发现符号与可穿戴设备、移动界面和便携工具等技术结合时最有效，强调可定制和情境敏感的披露方式。符号效果最佳时需提供用户对可见性的控制权以及可选的解释路径。

Conclusion: 应将基于符号的援助重新构想为更广泛的披露系统的一部分，其中意义取决于符号本身、载体和情境。这项工作为在不同环境中创建更包容的无障碍支持提供了指导。

Abstract: Our study investigates the relationship between accessibility symbols and emerging technologies in supporting disability disclosure. We conducted twenty three remote design creation sessions with semi structured interviews to examine participants awareness of existing symbols, how they use symbols across online and offline contexts, and barriers to adoption and interpretation. Through participant sketching and future oriented storyboard probes, participants proposed ways to integrate symbols into wearable devices, mobile interfaces, and portable tools, emphasizing customizable and context sensitive disclosure. Our findings suggest symbols are most effective when paired with technologies that provide user control over visibility and optional pathways for explanation, helping reduce misinterpretation while supporting agency in disclosure moments. By reimagining symbol based assistance as part of a broader disclosure system where meaning depends on the symbol, its carrier, and context, this work informs more inclusive accessibility supports across diverse settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Safe Large-Scale Robust Nonlinear MPC in Milliseconds via Reachability-Constrained System Level Synthesis on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07644)
*Jeffrey Fang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPU-SLS：一个GPU并行化的框架，用于安全、鲁棒的非线性模型预测控制，能够扩展到高维不确定机器人系统和长规划时域，实现实时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高维机器人系统（如全身四足机器人和人形机器人）需要实时、安全的非线性模型预测控制，但现有方法在计算效率和可扩展性方面存在限制，特别是在处理长规划时域和大规模决策变量时。

Method: 开发了一个GPU并行化框架，包含：1）使用顺序二次规划和新型GPU加速QP求解器（基于ADMM框架，采用并行关联扫描和自适应缓存）；2）使用相同的GPU QP后端通过系统级合成优化鲁棒跟踪控制器和闭环可达集；3）支持可达性约束控制，适用于固定时域和滚动时域设置。

Result: 实现了显著的性能提升：相对于最先进的CPU求解器，名义轨迹求解时间减少97.7%；相对于GPU求解器减少71.8%；SLS基控制和可达性计算加速237倍。在复杂非线性系统（包括61D四足机器人和75D人形机器人）上验证，平均在线合成鲁棒控制策略仅需20毫秒，可扩展到2×10^5决策变量和8×10^4约束的问题，并实现100%经验安全性。

Conclusion: GPU-SLS框架成功实现了高维机器人系统的实时、安全非线性模型预测控制，通过GPU并行化和新颖的优化算法显著提升了计算效率和可扩展性，为复杂机器人系统的在线鲁棒控制提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present GPU-SLS, a GPU-parallelized framework for safe, robust nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that scales to high-dimensional uncertain robotic systems and long planning horizons. Our method jointly optimizes an inequality-constrained, dynamically-feasible nominal trajectory, a tracking controller, and a closed-loop reachable set under disturbance, all in real-time. To efficiently compute nominal trajectories, we develop a sequential quadratic programming procedure with a novel GPU-accelerated quadratic program (QP) solver that uses parallel associative scans and adaptive caching within an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework. The same GPU QP backend is used to optimize robust tracking controllers and closed-loop reachable sets via system level synthesis (SLS), enabling reachability-constrained control in both fixed- and receding-horizon settings. We achieve substantial performance gains, reducing nominal trajectory solve times by 97.7% relative to state-of-the-art CPU solvers and 71.8% compared to GPU solvers, while accelerating SLS-based control and reachability by 237x. Despite large problem scales, our method achieves 100% empirical safety, unlike high-dimensional learning-based reachability baselines. We validate our approach on complex nonlinear systems, including whole-body quadrupeds (61D) and humanoids (75D), synthesizing robust control policies online on the GPU in 20 milliseconds on average and scaling to problems with 2 x 10^5 decision variables and 8 x 10^4 constraints. The implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/Jeff300fang/gpu_sls.

</details>


### [137] [Evaluation as Evolution: Transforming Adversarial Diffusion into Closed-Loop Curricula for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07378)
*Yicheng Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Chengkai Xu,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E²框架将对抗场景生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，通过运输正则化稀疏控制和拓扑驱动支持选择，在保持真实性的同时高效发现安全关键故障案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在交互交通环境中面临静态数据集安全关键尾部事件稀缺的问题，这导致学习策略偏向平均情况行为并降低鲁棒性。现有对抗压力测试方法主要是开环和事后处理，难以将发现的故障案例整合到训练过程中。

Method: 提出E²（Evaluation as Evolution）闭环框架，将对抗场景合成公式化为基于学习反向时间SDE先验的运输正则化稀疏控制。采用拓扑驱动支持选择识别关键交互智能体，引入拓扑锚定稳定生成过程，在约束偏离真实数据分布的同时定向发现故障案例。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上碰撞故障发现率提升9.01%，在nuPlan数据集上提升高达21.43%，同时保持低无效性和高真实性。将发现的边界案例用于闭环策略微调可获得显著的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: E²框架成功将对抗生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，在保持真实性的同时高效发现安全关键故障案例，为自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性评估和训练提供了有效的闭环解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles in interactive traffic environments are often limited by the scarcity of safety-critical tail events in static datasets, which biases learned policies toward average-case behaviors and reduces robustness. Existing evaluation methods attempt to address this through adversarial stress testing, but are predominantly open-loop and post-hoc, making it difficult to incorporate discovered failures back into the training process. We introduce Evaluation as Evolution ($E^2$), a closed-loop framework that transforms adversarial generation from a static validation step into an adaptive evolutionary curriculum. Specifically, $E^2$ formulates adversarial scenario synthesis as transport-regularized sparse control over a learned reverse-time SDE prior. To make this high-dimensional generation tractable, we utilize topology-driven support selection to identify critical interacting agents, and introduce Topological Anchoring to stabilize the process. This approach enables the targeted discovery of failure cases while strictly constraining deviations from realistic data distributions. Empirically, $E^2$ improves collision failure discovery by 9.01% on the nuScenes dataset and up to 21.43% on the nuPlan dataset over the strongest baselines, while maintaining low invalidity and high realism. It further yields substantial robustness gains when the resulting boundary cases are recycled for closed-loop policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [138] [On-Policy Distillation of Language Models for Autonomous Vehicle Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07944)
*Amirhossein Afsharrad,Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Sanjay Lall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义知识蒸馏（GKD）的方法，将大型语言模型（LLM）的运动规划知识迁移到更小的学生模型中，用于自动驾驶轨迹规划，在模型规模减少5倍的情况下仍能接近教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中展现出潜力，但受限于资源受限的车载系统部署。需要将大型教师LLM的知识有效迁移到更小、更可部署的学生模型中。

Method: 基于GPT-Driver框架，将驾驶场景表示为语言提示，通过思维链推理生成轨迹点。研究了两种学生训练范式：1) 基于策略的广义知识蒸馏（GKD），使用教师模型的密集令牌级反馈训练学生模型自身生成的输出；2) 密集反馈强化学习基线，将教师模型的log概率作为每令牌奖励信号用于策略梯度框架。

Result: 在nuScenes基准测试中，GKD显著优于RL基线，在模型规模减少5倍的情况下仍能接近教师级性能。

Conclusion: 基于策略的蒸馏是一种原则性且有效的方法，可用于在自动驾驶系统中部署基于LLM的规划器，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong potential for autonomous vehicle motion planning by reformulating trajectory prediction as a language generation problem. However, deploying capable LLMs in resource-constrained onboard systems remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we study how to effectively transfer motion planning knowledge from a large teacher LLM to a smaller, more deployable student model. We build on the GPT-Driver framework, which represents driving scenes as language prompts and generates waypoint trajectories with chain-of-thought reasoning, and investigate two student training paradigms: (i) on-policy generalized knowledge distillation (GKD), which trains the student on its own self-generated outputs using dense token-level feedback from the teacher, and (ii) a dense-feedback reinforcement learning (RL) baseline that uses the teacher's log-probabilities as per-token reward signals in a policy gradient framework. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that GKD substantially outperforms the RL baseline and closely approaches teacher-level performance despite a 5$\times$ reduction in model size. These results highlight the practical value of on-policy distillation as a principled and effective approach to deploying LLM-based planners in autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [139] [OpenPRC: A Unified Open-Source Framework for Physics-to-Task Evaluation in Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07423)
*Yogesh Phalak,Wen Sin Lor,Apoorva Khairnar,Benjamin Jantzen,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPRC是一个开源Python框架，为物理储层计算提供统一的物理到任务流程，包含GPU加速物理引擎、实验数据摄取、模块化学习层、分析工具和物理感知优化，通过通用HDF5模式确保可重复性和互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算利用物理基板的固有非线性动力学作为固定计算储层，提供节能和具身机器学习的范例。然而，现有工具通常只处理流程的孤立部分，缺乏能够通过相同数据接口表示高保真模拟轨迹和真实实验测量的统一框架，无法实现跨基板和数据源的可重复评估、分析和物理感知优化。

Method: OpenPRC采用模式驱动的物理到任务流程，包含五个模块：1) GPU加速的混合RK4-PBD物理引擎(demlat)；2) 基于视频的实验数据摄取层(openprc.vision)；3) 模块化学习层(reservoir)；4) 信息论分析和基准测试工具(analysis)；5) 物理感知优化(optimize)。通过通用HDF5模式确保可重复性和互操作性。

Result: 框架展示了折纸镶嵌模拟、从物理储层基于视频的轨迹提取，以及标准化PRC基准测试、相关性诊断和容量分析的通用接口。能够将GPU模拟和实验获取的轨迹无缝集成到相同下游工作流程中。

Conclusion: OpenPRC填补了物理储层计算领域统一框架的空白，旨在作为PRC社区的标准化层，兼容外部物理引擎如PyBullet、PyElastica和MERLIN，促进可重复评估、分析和优化。

Abstract: Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) leverages the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of physical substrates, mechanical, optical, spintronic, and beyond, as fixed computational reservoirs, offering a compelling paradigm for energy-efficient and embodied machine learning. However, the practical workflow for developing and evaluating PRC systems remains fragmented: existing tools typically address only isolated parts of the pipeline, such as substrate-specific simulation, digital reservoir benchmarking, or readout training. What is missing is a unified framework that can represent both high-fidelity simulated trajectories and real experimental measurements through the same data interface, enabling reproducible evaluation, analysis, and physics-aware optimization across substrates and data sources. We present OpenPRC, an open-source Python framework that fills this gap through a schema-driven physics-to-task pipeline built around five modules: a GPU-accelerated hybrid RK4-PBD physics engine (demlat), a video-based experimental ingestion layer (openprc.vision), a modular learning layer (reservoir), information-theoretic analysis and benchmarking tools (analysis), and physics-aware optimization (optimize). A universal HDF5 schema enforces reproducibility and interoperability, allowing GPU-simulated and experimentally acquired trajectories to enter the same downstream workflow without modification. Demonstrated capabilities include simulations of Origami tessellations, video-based trajectory extraction from a physical reservoir, and a common interface for standardized PRC benchmarking, correlation diagnostics, and capacity analysis. The longer-term vision is to serve as a standardizing layer for the PRC community, compatible with external physics engines including PyBullet, PyElastica, and MERLIN.

</details>


### [140] [CMP: Robust Whole-Body Tracking for Loco-Manipulation via Competence Manifold Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07457)
*Ziyang Cheng,Haoyu Wei,Hang Yin,Xiuwei Xu,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)方法，通过能力流形投影提高足式移动机械臂全身控制策略对分布外输入的鲁棒性，同时保持任务性能连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式移动机械臂的解耦控制方案虽然鲁棒，但学习型全身控制策略在跟踪全局末端执行器位姿时，对传感器噪声或不可行用户命令引起的分布外输入非常脆弱，需要在不牺牲任务性能和连续性的情况下提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)：1) 使用Frame-Wise Safety Scheme将无限时域安全约束转化为计算高效的单步流形包含问题；2) 采用Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator区分未掌握意图与训练分布；3) 引入Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS)对齐流形几何与安全概率，实现O(1)复杂度对任意分布外意图的无缝防御。

Result: 实验表明CMP在典型分布外场景中生存率提升高达10倍（基线方法出现灾难性失败），同时跟踪性能下降低于10%。系统展现出对分布外目标的"尽力而为"泛化行为，通过遵循能力边界逐步完成任务。

Conclusion: CMP方法有效提高了足式移动机械臂全身控制策略对分布外输入的鲁棒性，在保持任务性能的同时实现了对不可行命令的安全处理，并展现出渐进完成分布外目标的泛化能力。

Abstract: While decoupled control schemes for legged mobile manipulators have shown robustness, learning holistic whole-body control policies for tracking global end-effector poses remains fragile against Out-of-Distribution (OOD) inputs induced by sensor noise or infeasible user commands. To improve robustness against these perturbations without sacrificing task performance and continuity, we propose Competence Manifold Projection (CMP). Specifically, we utilize a Frame-Wise Safety Scheme that transforms the infinite-horizon safety constraint into a computationally efficient single-step manifold inclusion. To instantiate this competence manifold, we employ a Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator that distinguishes unmastered intentions from the training distribution. We then introduce an Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS) that aligns manifold geometry with safety probability, enabling efficient O(1) seamless defense against arbitrary OOD intents. Experiments demonstrate that CMP achieves up to a 10-fold survival rate improvement in typical OOD scenarios where baselines suffer catastrophic failure, incurring under 10% tracking degradation. Notably, the system exhibits emergent ``best-effort'' generalization behaviors to progressively accomplish OOD goals by adhering to the competence boundaries. Result videos are available at: https://shepherd1226.github.io/CMP.

</details>


### [141] [Active Reward Machine Inference From Raw State Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07480)
*Mohamad Louai Shehab,Antoine Aspeel,Necmiye Ozay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 从原始状态和策略信息中学习奖励机器，无需奖励、标签或机器节点观测，并扩展到主动学习以提高数据效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动指定奖励机器（包括捕捉决策所需高层特征的标记函数）是一项艰巨任务，现有方法需要访问奖励、标签或机器节点观测，限制了实际应用

Method: 提出在信息稀缺环境下直接从原始状态和策略信息学习奖励机器的方法，确定何种轨迹数据足以学习奖励机器，并扩展到主动学习设置，通过增量查询轨迹扩展来提高数据和计算效率

Result: 在多个网格世界示例中验证了方法的有效性，展示了在缺乏奖励、标签和机器节点观测的情况下仍能成功学习奖励机器

Conclusion: 证明了从原始状态和策略信息学习奖励机器的可行性，通过主动学习提高了数据效率，为实际应用中自动学习复杂任务结构提供了新途径

Abstract: Reward machines are automaton-like structures that capture the memory required to accomplish a multi-stage task. When combined with reinforcement learning or optimal control methods, they can be used to synthesize robot policies to achieve such tasks. However, specifying a reward machine by hand, including a labeling function capturing high-level features that the decisions are based on, can be a daunting task. This paper deals with the problem of learning reward machines directly from raw state and policy information. As opposed to existing works, we assume no access to observations of rewards, labels, or machine nodes, and show what trajectory data is sufficient for learning the reward machine in this information-scarce regime. We then extend the result to an active learning setting where we incrementally query trajectory extensions to improve data (and indirectly computational) efficiency. Results are demonstrated with several grid world examples.

</details>


### [142] [Grasp as You Dream: Imitating Functional Grasping from Generated Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07517)
*Chao Tang,Jiacheng Xu,Haofei Lu,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Hong Zhang,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspDreamer利用视觉生成模型合成的人类演示实现零样本功能抓取，无需大规模数据收集，在多种机器人手上表现出优越的数据效率和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够在开放世界环境中执行功能抓取的通用机器人面临巨大挑战，现有方法要么局限于狭窄的对象/任务集，要么依赖大规模数据收集来捕捉现实世界的变化性。

Method: GraspDreamer利用在互联网规模人类数据上预训练的视觉生成模型（如视频生成模型）合成人类演示，这些模型隐式编码了人类与物理世界交互的广义先验，结合特定具身动作优化实现零样本功能抓取。

Result: 在多个公开基准测试和不同机器人手上的实验表明，GraspDreamer相比先前方法具有优越的数据效率和泛化性能。真实世界评估进一步验证了其在真实机器人上的有效性。

Conclusion: GraspDreamer提供了一种无需大规模数据收集的功能抓取方法，能够自然地扩展到下游操作任务，并生成数据支持视觉运动策略学习，为构建通用机器人提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Building generalist robots capable of performing functional grasping in everyday, open-world environments remains a significant challenge due to the vast diversity of objects and tasks. Existing methods are either constrained to narrow object/task sets or rely on prohibitively large-scale data collection to capture real-world variability. In this work, we present an alternative approach, GraspDreamer, a method that leverages human demonstrations synthesized by visual generative models (VGMs) (e.g., video generation models) to enable zero-shot functional grasping without labor-intensive data collection. The key idea is that VGMs pre-trained on internet-scale human data implicitly encode generalized priors about how humans interact with the physical world, which can be combined with embodiment-specific action optimization to enable functional grasping with minimal effort. Extensive experiments on the public benchmarks with different robot hands demonstrate the superior data efficiency and generalization performance of GraspDreamer compared to previous methods. Real-world evaluations further validate the effectiveness on real robots. Additionally, we showcase that GraspDreamer can (1) be naturally extended to downstream manipulation tasks, and (2) can generate data to support visuomotor policy learning.

</details>


### [143] [SANDO: Safe Autonomous Trajectory Planning for Dynamic Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07599)
*Kota Kondo,Jesús Tordesillas,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANDO是一个用于3D动态未知环境的安全轨迹规划器，通过热图A*全局规划、时空安全飞行走廊生成和MIQP优化，在保证安全的同时实现快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D动态未知环境中，障碍物位置和运动事先未知，现有软约束规划器速度快但无法保证无碰撞路径，硬约束方法确保安全但计算时间长，需要解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 1) 热图A*全局规划器使用软成本引导路径远离高风险区域，时空安全飞行走廊生成器产生时间分层多面体；2) 将轨迹优化建模为带硬碰撞避免约束的混合整数二次规划，采用变量消除技术减少决策变量；3) 形式化安全分析建立明确速度边界和估计误差假设下的无碰撞保证。

Result: 变量消除技术使优化时间加速达7.4倍，STSFC在密集动态环境中对可行性至关重要。在标准化静态基准、障碍物丰富的静态森林和动态环境中的基准测试显示，SANDO在所有难度级别上始终实现最高成功率且无约束违反。硬件实验在无人机上完成6次静态环境安全飞行和10次动态障碍物安全飞行。

Conclusion: SANDO成功解决了动态未知环境中安全与计算效率的权衡问题，通过热图引导、时空安全走廊和高效MIQP优化，在保证形式化安全的同时实现快速重规划，在仿真和硬件实验中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: SANDO is a safe trajectory planner for 3D dynamic unknown environments, where obstacle locations and motions are unknown a priori and a collision-free plan can become unsafe at any moment, requiring fast replanning. Existing soft-constraint planners are fast but cannot guarantee collision-free paths, while hard-constraint methods ensure safety at the cost of longer computation. SANDO addresses this trade-off through three contributions. First, a heat map-based A* global planner steers paths away from high-risk regions using soft costs, and a spatiotemporal safe flight corridor (STSFC) generator produces time-layered polytopes that inflate obstacles only by their worst-case reachable set at each time layer, rather than by the worst case over the entire horizon. Second, trajectory optimization is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) with hard collision-avoidance constraints, and a variable elimination technique reduces the number of decision variables, enabling fast computation. Third, a formal safety analysis establishes collision-free guarantees under explicit velocity-bound and estimation-error assumptions. Ablation studies show that variable elimination yields up to 7.4x speedup in optimization time, and that STSFCs are critical for feasibility in dense dynamic environments. Benchmark simulations against state-of-the-art methods across standardized static benchmarks, obstacle-rich static forests, and dynamic environments show that SANDO consistently achieves the highest success rate with no constraint violations across all difficulty levels; perception-only experiments without ground truth obstacle information confirm robust performance under realistic sensing. Hardware experiments on a UAV with fully onboard planning, perception, and localization demonstrate six safe flights in static environments and ten safe flights among dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [144] [EgoVerse: An Egocentric Human Dataset for Robot Learning from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07607)
*Ryan Punamiya,Simar Kareer,Zeyi Liu,Josh Citron,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Xiongyi Cai,Alexey Gavryushin,Jiaqi Chen,Davide Liconti,Lawrence Y. Zhu,Patcharapong Aphiwetsa,Baoyu Li,Aniketh Cheluva,Pranav Kuppili,Yangcen Liu,Dhruv Patel,Aidan Gao,Hye-Young Chung,Ryan Co,Renee Zbizika,Jeff Liu,Xiaomeng Xu,Haoyu Xiong,Geng Chen,Sebastiano Oliani,Chenyu Yang,Xi Wang,James Fort,Richard Newcombe,Josh Gao,Jason Chong,Garrett Matsuda,Aseem Doriwala,Marc Pollefeys,Robert Katzschmann,Xiaolong Wang,Shuran Song,Judy Hoffman,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoVerse是一个用于机器人学习的协作平台，统一了人类数据收集、处理和访问，包含1,362小时人类演示数据，并通过多实验室研究验证了人类数据对机器人策略性能的积极影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要大量多样化数据，但机器人数据收集成本高且难以扩展。人类自我中心数据提供了丰富的日常操作行为，但现有数据集范围有限、难以扩展且分散在不同机构。

Method: 开发了EgoVerse协作平台，统一数据收集、处理和访问框架；收集了1,362小时人类演示数据（80k片段），涵盖1,965个任务、240个场景、2,087名演示者；进行了大规模人类到机器人迁移研究，在多个实验室、任务和机器人形态下使用共享协议进行实验。

Result: 当前版本包含大规模标准化数据集；研究发现策略性能通常随人类数据增加而提升，但有效扩展取决于人类数据与机器人学习目标的对齐；建立了可重复的人类数据驱动机器人学习基础。

Conclusion: EgoVerse平台、数据集和研究共同为人类数据驱动的机器人学习建立了可重复进展的基础，表明人类数据对机器人学习具有重要价值，但需要数据与学习目标的对齐。

Abstract: Robot learning increasingly depends on large and diverse data, yet robot data collection remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric human data offer a promising alternative by capturing rich manipulation behavior across everyday environments. However, existing human datasets are often limited in scope, difficult to extend, and fragmented across institutions. We introduce EgoVerse, a collaborative platform for human data-driven robot learning that unifies data collection, processing, and access under a shared framework, enabling contributions from individual researchers, academic labs, and industry partners. The current release includes 1,362 hours (80k episodes) of human demonstrations spanning 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 unique demonstrators, with standardized formats, manipulation-relevant annotations, and tooling for downstream learning. Beyond the dataset, we conduct a large-scale study of human-to-robot transfer with experiments replicated across multiple labs, tasks, and robot embodiments under shared protocols. We find that policy performance generally improves with increased human data, but that effective scaling depends on alignment between human data and robot learning objectives. Together, the dataset, platform, and study establish a foundation for reproducible progress in human data-driven robot learning. Videos and additional information can be found at https://egoverse.ai/

</details>


### [145] [Reset-Free Reinforcement Learning for Real-World Agile Driving: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07672)
*Kohei Honda,Hirotaka Hosogaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无重置强化学习在真实世界敏捷驾驶中的应用，发现仿真与真实世界存在显著差距，残差学习在仿真中有效但在真实世界中可能失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究真实世界无重置强化学习在高速驾驶中的应用，解决复杂车辆动力学、执行延迟和未建模效应带来的挑战，这些因素阻碍了精确仿真和直接仿真到真实环境的策略迁移。

Method: 使用模型预测路径积分控制(MPPI)作为重置策略和残差学习的基础策略，系统比较PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2三种代表性RL算法在仿真和真实世界实验中的表现，包括有无残差学习的情况。

Result: 仿真与真实世界存在明显差距：SAC加残差学习在仿真中获得最高回报，但只有TD-MPC2在物理平台上持续优于MPPI基线；残差学习在仿真中明显有益，但在真实世界中无法转移其优势，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 真实世界的无重置强化学习面临仿真中不存在的独特挑战，需要针对野外训练进一步开发算法。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical study of reset-free reinforcement learning (RL) for real-world agile driving, in which a physical 1/10-scale vehicle learns continuously on a slippery indoor track without manual resets. High-speed driving near the limits of tire friction is particularly challenging for learning-based methods because complex vehicle dynamics, actuation delays, and other unmodeled effects hinder both accurate simulation and direct sim-to-real transfer of learned policies. To enable autonomous training on a physical platform, we employ Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) as both the reset policy and the base policy for residual learning, and systematically compare three representative RL algorithms, i.e., PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2, with and without residual learning in simulation and real-world experiments. Our results reveal a clear gap between simulation and real-world: SAC with residual learning achieves the highest returns in simulation, yet only TD-MPC2 consistently outperforms the MPPI baseline on the physical platform. Moreover, residual learning, while clearly beneficial in simulation, fails to transfer its advantage to the real world and can even degrade performance. These findings reveal that reset-free RL in the real world poses unique challenges absent from simulation, calling for further algorithmic development tailored to training in the wild.

</details>


### [146] [Bird-Inspired Spatial Flapping Wing Mechanism via Coupled Linkages with Single Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07677)
*Daniel Huczala,Sun-Pill Jung,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于耦合空间四杆机构的仿鸟扑翼机构，采用单电机驱动，通过被动状态切换实现协调的扫掠-折叠翼运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间单环机构（如Bennett连杆）具有单自由度驱动和非平凡空间轨迹的特点，适用于轻量化仿生机器人设计。然而，其几何任务综合因数学复杂性而难以实现，限制了工程应用。

Method: 提出简化的运动学方法，从包含所需表面积的四边形构建Bennett连杆；采用两个耦合的空间四杆机构，一个主动驱动产生空间扫掠行程，另一个串联耦合的连杆保持非驱动状态，在行程周期内被动切换伸展和折叠的翼型配置。

Result: 通过3D打印原型组装和测试，验证了机构能够实现预期的空间行程和被动折叠行为，实现了单驱动输入的协调扫掠-折叠翼运动。

Conclusion: 该架构通过机械诱导的被动状态切换，实现了单驱动输入的协调翼运动，降低了重量和控制复杂性，为轻量化仿生机器人设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial single-loop mechanisms such as Bennett linkages offer a unique combination of one-degree-of-freedom actuation and nontrivial spatial trajectories, making them attractive for lightweight bio-inspired robotic design. However, although they appear simple and elegant, the geometric task-based synthesis is rather complicated and often avoided in engineering tasks due to the mathematical complexity involved. This paper presents a bird-inspired flapping-wing mechanism built from two coupled spatial four-bars, driven by a single motor. One linkage is actuated to generate the desired spatial sweeping stroke, while the serially coupled linkage remains unactuated and passively switches between extended and folded wing configurations over the stroke cycle. We introduce a simplified kinematic methodology for constructing Bennett linkages from quadrilaterals that contain a desired surface area and further leverage mechanically induced passive state switching. This architecture realizes a coordinated sweep-and-fold wing motion with a single actuation input, reducing weight and control complexity. A 3D-printed prototype is assembled and tested, demonstrating the intended spatial stroke and passive folding behavior.

</details>


### [147] [Vision-Language Navigation for Aerial Robots: Towards the Era of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07705)
*Xingyu Xia,Lekai Zhou,Yujie Tang,Xiaozhou Zhu,Hai Zhu,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述对空中视觉语言导航领域进行了全面分析，特别关注大语言模型和视觉语言模型的整合，提出了分类体系、评估框架和七大开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在复杂三维环境中自主导航需求的增长，以及大语言模型和视觉语言模型的发展，需要系统梳理空中视觉语言导航领域的研究现状、技术挑战和发展方向，为该领域的进一步发展提供指导。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法：1) 形式化定义Aerial VLN问题及两种交互范式；2) 建立五类架构分类法；3) 分析各类方法的设计原理和技术权衡；4) 评估现有基础设施的局限性；5) 在共享基准上进行跨方法比较；6) 识别关键架构权衡。

Result: 提出了完整的Aerial VLN研究框架，包括：两种交互范式（单指令和对话式）、五类架构分类、现有评估基础设施的局限性分析、跨方法性能比较、关键架构权衡分析，以及七大具体开放性问题。

Conclusion: Aerial VLN是一个快速发展的跨学科领域，大语言模型和视觉语言模型的整合带来了新机遇，但仍面临长时指令理解、视点鲁棒性、空间表示、连续动作执行、部署实践、基准标准化和多无人机协同等七大挑战，需要进一步研究突破。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (Aerial VLN) aims to enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to interpret natural language instructions and autonomously navigate complex three-dimensional environments by grounding language in visual perception. This survey provides a critical and analytical review of the Aerial VLN field, with particular attention to the recent integration of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). We first formally introduce the Aerial VLN problem and define two interaction paradigms: single-instruction and dialog-based, as foundational axes. We then organize the body of Aerial VLN methods into a taxonomy of five architectural categories: sequence-to-sequence and attention-based methods, end-to-end LLM/VLM methods, hierarchical methods, multi-agent methods, and dialog-based navigation methods. For each category, we systematically analyze design rationales, technical trade-offs, and reported performance. We critically assess the evaluation infrastructure for Aerial VLN, including datasets, simulation platforms, and metrics, and identify their gaps in scale, environmental diversity, real-world grounding, and metric coverage. We consolidate cross-method comparisons on shared benchmarks and analyze key architectural trade-offs, including discrete versus continuous actions, end-to-end versus hierarchical designs, and the simulation-to-reality gap. Finally, we synthesize seven concrete open problems: long-horizon instruction grounding, viewpoint robustness, scalable spatial representation, continuous 6-DoF action execution, onboard deployment, benchmark standardization, and multi-UAV swarm navigation, with specific research directions grounded in the evidence presented throughout the survey.

</details>


### [148] [RoboAgent: Chaining Basic Capabilities for Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07774)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAgent：一种基于能力驱动的具身任务规划框架，通过调度器主动调用不同子能力，将复杂规划分解为VLM更易处理的基础视觉语言问题，实现更透明可控的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在多模态理解和推理方面取得显著进展，但在涉及多轮交互、长时程推理和扩展上下文分析的具身任务规划中表现仍有限。现有方法难以处理复杂的规划任务，需要更透明可控的推理框架。

Method: 提出RoboAgent能力驱动规划框架：1）调度器主动调用不同子能力；2）每个能力维护独立上下文，根据调度器查询产生中间推理结果或与环境交互；3）所有组件均用单一VLM实现；4）采用三阶段训练范式：行为克隆、DAgger训练、强化学习；5）利用环境模拟器内部信息构建高质量监督，引入增强和合成数据提升多样性。

Result: 在广泛使用的具身任务规划基准测试中进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。具体性能指标未在摘要中提供，但代码将在CVPR26会议上开源。

Conclusion: RoboAgent通过能力驱动框架将复杂具身规划分解为VLM更易处理的基础问题，实现了更透明可控的推理过程。该方法不依赖外部工具，通过多阶段训练和高质量监督数据提升了模型在多样化场景中的性能。
